const hasFiles = (e) => [...(e.dataTransfer?.types ?? [])].includes("Files");
const dragging = (on) => {
  document.body.classList.toggle("dragging", on);
  $("cbox").classList.toggle("drop", on);
};
document.addEventListener("dragenter", (e) => {
  if (hasFiles(e) && state.current) {
    dragDepth++;
    dragging(true);
  }
});
document.addEventListener("dragleave", (e) => {
  if (hasFiles(e) && --dragDepth <= 0) {
    dragDepth = 0;
    dragging(false);
  }
});
document.addEventListener("dragover", (e) => hasFiles(e) && e.preventDefault()); // otherwise the browser navigates to the dropped file
document.addEventListener("drop", (e) => {
  if (!hasFiles(e)) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  dragDepth = 0;
  dragging(false);
  const files = [];
  let folders = 0;
  if (e.dataTransfer.items) {
    for (const it of e.dataTransfer.items) {
      if (it.kind !== "file") continue;
      if (it.webkitGetAsEntry?.()?.isDirectory) folders++;
      else if (it.getAsFile()) files.push(it.getAsFile());
    }
  } else files.push(...e.dataTransfer.files);
  if (folders) addNote("Folders can't be attached. Zip the folder first.", "errtxt");
  addFiles(files);
});

// ---------- slash menu ("/" for commands and skills) ----------
const slash = { items: [], idx: 0, dismissed: false };
const slashEl = $("slash");
function slashQuery() {
  const m = /^\/([^\s]*)$/.exec($("input").value.trimStart());
  return m ? m[1].toLowerCase() : null;
}
function slashMatches(q) {
  const rank = (c) => {
    const n = c.name.toLowerCase();
    if (n === q) return 0;
    if (n.startsWith(q)) return 1;
    if (n.split(/[:\-_]/).some((p) => p.startsWith(q))) return 2;
    if (n.includes(q)) return 3;
    if (c.description.toLowerCase().includes(q)) return 4;
    return 9;
  };
  const commands = (state.commands[cur()?.provider || "claude"] || []).filter(
    (command) => command.name.toLowerCase() !== "host",
  );
  return [{ name: "host", description: "Host CPU, RAM, disk and top processes" }, ...commands]
    .filter((c) => !c.name.startsWith("_"))
    .map((c) => [rank(c), c])
    .filter(([r]) => r < 9)
    .sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0] || a[1].name.localeCompare(b[1].name))
    .slice(0, 60)
    .map(([, c]) => c);
}
function closeSlash() {
  slashEl.hidden = true;
  slash.items = [];
  $("input").setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  $("input").removeAttribute("aria-activedescendant");
}
function renderSlash() {
  slashEl.replaceChildren();
  if (!slash.items.length) {
    const li = el(
      "li",
      "none",
      (state.commands[cur()?.provider || "claude"] || []).length
        ? "No matching commands"
        : "Commands load after your first message.",
    );
    slashEl.append(li);
    return;
  }
  slash.items.forEach((c, i) => {
    const li = el("li");
    li.id = `slash-${i}`;
    li.setAttribute("role", "option");
    li.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(i === slash.idx));
    const head = el("span", "cmd", "/" + c.name);
    if (c.argumentHint) head.append(el("span", "arg", c.argumentHint));
    li.append(head, el("span", "desc", c.description));
    li.onmousedown = (e) => e.preventDefault(); // keep focus in the textarea
    li.onclick = () => acceptSlash(c);
    slashEl.append(li);
  });
  $("input").setAttribute("aria-activedescendant", `slash-${slash.idx}`);
}
function updateSlash() {
  const q = slashQuery();
  if (q === null || slash.dismissed || $("input").disabled) return closeSlash();
  slash.items = slashMatches(q);
  slash.idx = Math.min(slash.idx, Math.max(0, slash.items.length - 1));
  slashEl.hidden = false;
  $("input").setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
  renderSlash();
}
function moveSlash(d) {
  if (!slash.items.length) return;
  slash.idx = (slash.idx + d + slash.items.length) % slash.items.length;
  [...slashEl.children].forEach((li, i) => {
    li.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(i === slash.idx));
  });
  slashEl.children[slash.idx]?.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" });
  $("input").setAttribute("aria-activedescendant", `slash-${slash.idx}`);
}
function acceptSlash(c) {
  const box = $("input");
  box.value = `/${c.name} `;
  box.focus();
  box.setSelectionRange(box.value.length, box.value.length);
  box.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
}
$("input").addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
  const shown = !slashEl.hidden;
  const open = shown && slash.items.length > 0;
  if (shown && e.key === "Escape") {
    e.preventDefault();
    slash.dismissed = true;
    return closeSlash();
  }
  if (open) {
    if (e.key === "ArrowDown" || e.key === "ArrowUp") {
      e.preventDefault();
      return moveSlash(e.key === "ArrowDown" ? 1 : -1);
    }
    if ((e.key === "Tab" || e.key === "Enter") && !e.shiftKey && !e.isComposing) {
      const c = slash.items[slash.idx];
      const exact = c.name.toLowerCase() === slashQuery();
      if (e.key === "Enter" && exact) {
        e.preventDefault();
        return submit(e.altKey ? "steer" : undefined);
      } else {
        e.preventDefault();
        return acceptSlash(c);
      }
    }
  }
  const desktop = matchMedia("(pointer:fine)").matches;
  const command = /^\/[a-z][\w:-]*(?:\s[\s\S]*)?$/i.test($("input").value.trim());
  if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey && !e.isComposing && (desktop || command)) {
    e.preventDefault();
    submit(e.altKey ? "steer" : undefined); // Alt+Enter: steer the running turn now instead of queueing
  }
});
function sizeInput() {
  const box = $("input");
  const scrollTop = box.scrollTop;
  box.style.height = "auto";
  const viewport = window.visualViewport?.height || window.innerHeight;
  const line = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(box).lineHeight);
  const chat = getComputedStyle($("chat"));
  const reserve =
    $("head").offsetHeight +
    $("composer").offsetHeight -
    box.offsetHeight +
    parseFloat(chat.paddingTop) +
    parseFloat(chat.paddingBottom) +
    line;
  box.style.height =
    Math.max(line, Math.min(box.scrollHeight, viewport * 0.35, viewport - reserve)) + "px";
  // Measuring at full height clears the editable's scroll position. Restore it before revealing the caret.
  box.scrollTop = scrollTop;
  const selection = getSelection();
  if (document.activeElement !== box || !selection?.focusNode || !box.contains(selection.focusNode))
    return;
  const caret = document.createRange();
  caret.setStart(selection.focusNode, selection.focusOffset);
  caret.collapse(true);
  let rect = caret.getBoundingClientRect();
  // Blank lines need their newline or placeholder <br> to supply the range's bounds.
  if (
    !rect.height &&
    selection.focusNode.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE &&
    selection.focusNode.textContent[selection.focusOffset] === "\n"
  ) {
    caret.setEnd(selection.focusNode, selection.focusOffset + 1);
    rect = caret.getBoundingClientRect();
  }
  const next =
    selection.focusNode.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE
      ? selection.focusNode.nextSibling
      : selection.focusNode.childNodes[selection.focusOffset];
  if (!rect.height && next?.nodeName === "BR") {
    caret.selectNode(next);
    rect = caret.getBoundingClientRect();
  }
  if (!rect.height) return;
  const bounds = box.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (rect.bottom > bounds.bottom) box.scrollTop += rect.bottom - bounds.bottom;
  else if (rect.top < bounds.top) box.scrollTop -= bounds.top - rect.top;
}
$("input").addEventListener("input", () => {
  saveDraft();
  sizeInput();
  slash.dismissed = false;
  slash.idx = 0;
  updateSlash();
  renderAction();
});
// Safari can resize only its visual viewport when the keyboard opens. Preserve pinch-to-zoom.
let viewportFrame;
function fitViewport() {
  cancelAnimationFrame(viewportFrame);
  viewportFrame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    const v = window.visualViewport;
    if (v && Math.abs(v.scale - 1) >= 0.01) {
      document.documentElement.style.removeProperty("--viewport-height");
      return;
    }
    if (v) document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--viewport-height", `${v.height}px`);
    sizeInput();
    if (following()) scrollDown(true);
  });
}
window.visualViewport?.addEventListener("resize", fitViewport);
fitViewport();
$("input").addEventListener("blur", () => setTimeout(closeSlash, 120));
$("menu").onclick = () => setNav(!document.body.classList.contains("nav"));
$("scrim").onclick = () => setNav(false);
document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
  if (e.key === "Escape" && document.body.classList.contains("nav")) {
    setNav(false);
    $("menu").focus();
  }
});
// The new-thread dialog doubles as the scheduled-task editor: same agent, folder, model, effort and permission fields, plus the task's
// name, instructions and schedule.
let dialogTask = null; // "new" or the task being edited; null for a new thread
let dialogAssignment = null;
let assignmentPending = false;
$("dlg").addEventListener("cancel", (event) => {
  if (assignmentPending) event.preventDefault();
  workflowBrief = null;
});
function dialogTitleText(editing, isTask) {
  if (editing) return "Edit scheduled task";
  return isTask ? "New scheduled task" : "New thread";
}
function dialogOkText(editing, isTask) {
  if (editing) return "Save task";
  return isTask ? "Schedule task" : "Create thread";
}
function fillEditingTask(task, providerId) {
  if (!menus.dCwd.items().some((item) => item.value === task.cwd))
    menus.dCwd.setItems([
      ...menus.dCwd.items(),
      { value: task.cwd, label: task.cwd.replace(/^.*\/repos\/?/, "~/repos/"), icon: "folder" },
    ]);
  menus.dCwd.setValue(task.cwd);
  if (menus.dModel.items().some((item) => item.value === task.model)) {
    menus.dModel.setValue(task.model);
    dialogEffort(task.model, providerId);
  }
  menus.dEffort.setValue(task.effort || "default");
  menus.dMode.setValue(task.mode);
  $("d-warn").hidden = task.mode !== "bypassPermissions";
}
function fillTaskDialog(task, editing) {
  const providerId = editing ? task.provider : prov("pi")?.ok ? "pi" : menus.dProv.value;
  menus.dProv.setValue(providerId);
  fillDialog(providerId);
  if (editing) fillEditingTask(task, providerId);
  $("d-title").value = editing ? task.title : "";
  $("d-prompt").value = editing ? task.prompt : "";
  menus.dRepeat.setValue(editing ? task.repeat : "daily");
  menus.dDay.setValue(String(editing ? task.day : 1));
  $("d-time").value = editing ? task.time : "09:00";
  $("d-save").checked = editing ? task.saveOutput : true;
  syncWhen();
}
function selectFirstOkProvider() {
  const first = [...state.providers.values()].find((prov) => prov.ok)?.id;
  if (first) {
    menus.dProv.setValue(first);
    fillDialog(first);
  }
}
function setDialogKind(task) {
  dialogAssignment = null;
  $("d-assignment-error").hidden = true;
  menus.dProv.setItems(
    [...state.providers.values()].map((p) => ({ value: p.id, label: p.label, disabled: !p.ok })),
    menus.dProv.value,
  );
  const wasTask = dialogTask !== null;
  dialogTask = task;
  $("d-auto-panel").hidden = true;
  $("d-auto-info").setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  $("d-auto-note").textContent = AUTO_HINT;
  const isTask = task !== null,
    editing = isTask && task !== "new";
  $("task-fields").hidden = $("save-field").hidden = !isTask;
  $("dlg-title").textContent = dialogTitleText(editing, isTask);
  $("dlg-sub").textContent = isTask
    ? "Runs on this server with nobody watching. Each run opens a thread you can reply to."
    : "Run a coding agent in one of your folders.";
  $("d-title").required = $("d-prompt").required = isTask;
  if (isTask) fillTaskDialog(task, editing);
  else if (wasTask) selectFirstOkProvider();
  $("d-ok").textContent = dialogOkText(editing, isTask);
  dialogFork = null;
  $("context-field").hidden = true;
  $("cwd-field").hidden = false;
  syncDialogAccount();
}
// Fork reuses the dialog: prefilled from the source thread, folder fixed, plus how much of the conversation to carry.
let dialogFork = null; // the thread being forked
function syncForkContext() {
  const t = state.threads.get(dialogFork);
  const native =
    t &&
    menus.dProv.value === t.provider &&
    (t.provider !== "claude" || menus.dAccount.value === (t.claudeAccount || "primary"));
  menus.dContext.setItems(
    [
      {
        value: "full",
        label: "Full conversation",
        desc: native
          ? "Continue the same session; nothing is lost."
          : "Replay the whole conversation as text.",
      },
      {
        value: "recent",
        label: "Recent context only",
        desc: "Carry a short handoff; the full transcript stays available.",
      },
    ],
    menus.dContext.value || "full",
  );
}
function openForkDialog(id) {
  const t = state.threads.get(id);
  if (!t) return;
  if (t.status === "running")
    return void toast("Wait for the current turn to finish before forking.");
  setNav(false);
  setDialogKind(null);
  dialogFork = id;
  const pid =
    prov(t.provider)?.ok !== false
      ? t.provider
      : [...state.providers.values()].find((p) => p.ok)?.id;
  menus.dProv.setValue(pid);
  fillDialog(pid);
  if (pid === t.provider) {
    menus.dAccount.setValue(t.claudeAccount || "primary");
    if (menus.dModel.items().some((i) => i.value === t.model)) {
      menus.dModel.setValue(t.model);
      dialogEffort(t.model, pid);
    }
    if (menus.dEffort.items().some((i) => i.value === t.effort)) menus.dEffort.setValue(t.effort);
    if (menus.dMode.items().some((i) => i.value === t.mode)) menus.dMode.setValue(t.mode);
    $("d-warn").hidden = menus.dMode.value !== "bypassPermissions";
  }
  $("dlg-title").textContent = "Fork thread";
  $("dlg-sub").textContent =
    `Continue “${t.title}” in a new thread, in ${t.cwd.replace(/^.*\/repos\/?/, "~/repos/")}.`;
  $("context-field").hidden = false;
  $("cwd-field").hidden = true;
  menus.dContext.setValue("full");
  $("d-ok").textContent = "Fork thread";
  syncDialogAccount();
  $("dlg").showModal();
}
function openPremiumDialog(issue) {
  setNav(false);
  setDialogKind(null);
  const providers = [...state.providers.values()].filter((p) => ["claude", "codex"].includes(p.id));
  const chosen = providers.find((p) => p.ok);
  if (!chosen) return void toast("No premium agent is ready. Sign in to Claude or Codex first.");
  dialogAssignment = issue;
  menus.dProv.setItems(
    providers.map((p) => ({ value: p.id, label: p.label, disabled: !p.ok })),
    chosen.id,
  );
  fillDialog(chosen.id);
  const related = [...state.threads.values()].filter(
    (t) =>
      t.issueRun?.repo?.toLowerCase() === issue.repo.toLowerCase() &&
      t.issueRun.number === issue.number,
  );
  $("dlg-title").textContent = "Assign to premium";
  $("dlg-sub").textContent =
    `${issue.repo}#${issue.number} · ${related.length} related runs. The new session receives remaining work, current state, failed thread IDs and linked PRs.`;
  $("cwd-field").hidden = true;
  $("d-ok").textContent = "Start premium session";
  $("dlg").showModal();
}
async function submitPremiumAssignment(settings, account) {
  if (assignmentPending) return;
  assignmentPending = true;
  const issue = dialogAssignment;
  const cancel = $("dform").querySelector('button[value="cancel"]');
  cancel.disabled = true;
  $("d-ok").disabled = true;
  $("d-ok").textContent = "Starting…";
  $("d-assignment-error").hidden = true;
  const reset = () => {
    assignmentPending = false;
    cancel.disabled = false;
    $("d-ok").textContent = "Start premium session";
    syncDialogAccount();
  };
  let response;
  try {
    const { cwd, ...choice } = settings;
    response = await apiFetch("/api/automation/assign", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Accept: "application/x-ndjson" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ v: 1, ...issue, ...choice, ...account }),
    });
    if (!response.ok || !response.headers.get("content-type")?.includes("ndjson")) {
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || "Could not start a premium session.");
      $("dlg").close();
      dialogAssignment = null;
      return void openThread(result.thread);
    }
  } catch (error) {
    $("d-assignment-error").textContent = error.message;
    $("d-assignment-error").hidden = false;
    return void reset();
  }
  // Checkout preparation takes up to a minute: the settings dialog gives way to a live timeline of its steps.
  $("dlg").close();
  reset();
  const back = (message) => {
    if (dialogAssignment !== issue) openPremiumDialog(issue);
    else {
      setNav(false);
      $("dlg").showModal();
    }
    $("d-assignment-error").textContent = message;
    $("d-assignment-error").hidden = false;
  };
  try {
    await showAssignmentProgress(response, {
      title: `${issue.repo}#${issue.number}`,
      onBack: () => back($("ap-error").textContent),
      onOpen: (id) => {
        if (dialogAssignment === issue) dialogAssignment = null;
        openThread(id);
      },
    });
    if (!$("ap").open) toast("Premium session started.");
  } catch (error) {
    if (!$("ap").open) toast(error.message);
  }
}
function syncWhen() {
  const r = menus.dRepeat.value;
  $("d-day").hidden = r !== "weekly";
  $("d-time").hidden = r === "manual";
  $("d-time").required = r !== "manual";
  const zone = Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone;
  $("d-next").textContent =
    r === "manual"
      ? "Runs only when you press Run now."
      : r === "hourly"
        ? `Every hour at the minute shown (${zone}).`
        : `Your time zone: ${zone}.`;
}
function openNewDialog() {
  setNav(false);
  workflowBrief = null;
  setDialogKind(null);
  menus.dAccount.setValue("primary");
  syncDialogAccount();
  $("dlg").showModal();
}
// + New workflow on /automation lands here: the same new-thread dialog, titled for a workflow, and the thread starts with
// the drawing, step and alert rules already written into the composer. The brief is editable and never sent by itself.
const WORKFLOW_BRIEF = `Add a new workflow to the Automation page (/automation) end to end.

Read first: server/automation/contracts.ts, server/automation/snapshot.ts, server/automation/events.ts, public/automation.js, public/automation.css, docs/automation.md and docs/monitoring.md.

1. Data before pixels. Add the workflow as a Pipeline in FLOW (contracts.ts) with group, icon, phases and ordered StepId steps; add each FlowNode and every FlowEdge with the condition that takes it. One step = one atomic action with a real trigger, state and event; never a decorative box.
2. Real steps only. Every step writes start/ok/fail/info events to the automation log (or reads a real unit/status source), has a disabled state, and can be enabled or disabled independently. The sidebar entry and detail view must come from FLOW, not bespoke HTML.
3. Draw it with the existing system: hand-drawn ICONS in public/automation.js (one stroke, no emoji, no icon library), console tokens and CSS classes. Phases run left to right, arrows carry their condition, loops are boxed with their label, and the phone layout is one vertical column. No new dependency.
4. Alerts. Phone web push is the default and only channel for non-security alerts. AgentMail only for security alerts, used minimally: batch into a daily digest, never one mail per event, and stay within the 3,000 messages per month allowance. Never put secrets, tokens or raw advisory text in a mail.
5. Guard rails. Keep the existing steps, tests, API shapes and version checks working. Add a failing test first, then fix it. Update test/automation-ui.ts (and test/sec-automations-ui.ts when security) for the new sidebar entry, its steps and its states, plus docs/automation.md.
6. Follow the pr-standards skill; end the PR body with ## Self-review and evidence screenshots at phone and desktop width.`;
let workflowBrief = null;
function maybeOpenWorkflowDialog() {
  const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  if (params.get("new") !== "workflow" || !state.ready) return;
  params.delete("new");
  const query = params.toString();
  history.replaceState(null, "", `${location.pathname}${query ? `?${query}` : ""}${location.hash}`);
  openNewDialog();
  workflowBrief = WORKFLOW_BRIEF;
  $("dlg-title").textContent = "New workflow";
  $("dlg-sub").textContent =
    "The brief already covers the drawing, atomic steps and alert limits. Choose an agent and folder.";
  $("d-ok").textContent = "Create thread";
}
// Android share sheet -> manifest share_target -> /?share_text=...&share_url=... . Strip the params first so a reload never re-shares.
let sharingLink = false;
async function maybeShareLink() {
  const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  const text = params.get("share_text") || "";
  const url = params.get("share_url") || "";
  const title = params.get("share_title") || "";
  if (!text && !url) return;
  if (sharingLink) return;
  sharingLink = true;
  for (const key of ["share_text", "share_url", "share_title"]) params.delete(key);
  const query = params.toString();
  history.replaceState(null, "", `${location.pathname}${query ? `?${query}` : ""}${location.hash}`);
  try {
    let choice = await pickShareTarget();
    if (choice === "") choice = await createShareThread();
    if (choice === null) return void toast("Share cancelled");
    await prepareShareDraft(choice, url || text);
    const response = await apiFetch("/api/share", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ text, url, title, thread: choice }),
    });
    const body = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!response.ok || body.thread !== choice)
      throw new Error(body.error || "Could not fetch that link.");
    await fillShareDraft(body);
  } catch (error) {
    shareFailure(error);
  } finally {
    sharingLink = false;
  }
}
function shareFailure(error) {
  if (!signedOut)
    banner(`${error.message || "Could not fetch that link."} Original link kept in the draft.`, {
      label: "Dismiss",
      run: () => banner(""),
    });
}
async function prepareShareDraft(thread, text) {
  if (!(await waitForThread(thread))) throw new Error("The shared thread did not load.");
  openThread(thread);
  const box = $("input");
  box.value = box.value.trim() ? `${box.value}\n${text}` : text;
  saveDraft();
  sizeInput();
  if (!signedOut) banner("Fetching shared link… Original link saved in the draft.");
}
function handleCreated(threadId) {
  if (shareCreate?.submitted) return resolveShareCreate(threadId);
  openThread(threadId);
  if (!workflowBrief) return;
  writeDraft(threadId, workflowBrief);
  $("input").value = workflowBrief;
  sizeInput();
  workflowBrief = null;
  $("input").focus();
  toast("Workflow brief ready. Edit it, then send.");
}
// Share + compose: the normal New thread dialog picks provider, account, model and folder. Only the `created` that follows this
// dialog's own submit resolves the share (flag set on submit, consumed once); cancelling the dialog resolves null.
let shareCreate = null;
function resolveShareCreate(value) {
  const pendingShare = shareCreate;
  if (!pendingShare) return;
  shareCreate = null;
  clearTimeout(pendingShare.timer);
  pendingShare.resolve(value);
}
function createShareThread() {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    shareCreate = { submitted: false, resolve, timer: 0 };
    openNewDialog();
    $("dlg-sub").textContent += " The shared post will be attached to the new thread as a draft.";
  });
}
$("dlg").addEventListener("close", () => {
  if (shareCreate && !shareCreate.submitted) resolveShareCreate(null);
});
$("dform").addEventListener("submit", (e) => {
  if (!shareCreate || e.submitter?.value !== "ok" || dialogTask !== null) return;
  if (dialogAssignment || dialogFork) return;
  shareCreate.submitted = true;
  shareCreate.timer = setTimeout(() => resolveShareCreate(null), 30_000);
});
// The thread can reach the client over the WebSocket slightly after the POST resolves.
async function waitForThread(id) {
  for (let tries = 0; tries < 50 && !state.threads.has(id); tries++)
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
  return state.threads.has(id);
}
// Put the share prompt and its already-uploaded files into the composer as a draft; the user edits and sends.
async function fillShareDraft(body) {
  const thread = body.thread;
  if (!(await waitForThread(thread))) return void toast("Shared, but the thread did not load.");
  const box = $("input");
  const text = state.current === thread ? box.value : drafts.get(thread) || "";
  const draft = text.trim() ? `${text}\n${body.prompt || ""}` : body.prompt || "";
  writeDraft(thread, draft);
  const list = attachmentDrafts.get(thread) || [];
  attachmentDrafts.set(thread, list);
  const saved = [];
  for (const file of body.attachments || []) {
    const p = {
      key: crypto.randomUUID(),
      thread,
      id: file.id,
      name: file.name,
      kind: file.kind,
      size: file.size,
      n: 0,
      prepared: true,
      order: (attachmentOrder = Math.max(Date.now(), attachmentOrder + 1)),
      url: `/uploads/${thread}/${file.id}/${encodeURIComponent(file.name)}`,
      ready: Promise.resolve(),
    };
    list.push(p);
    saved.push(attachmentStorage.save(p));
  }
  if (state.current === thread) {
    pending = list;
    renderThumbs();
    box.value = draft;
    sizeInput();
    box.focus();
  }
  await Promise.all(saved);
  if (!signedOut) banner("");
}
// Share mode: the left drawer doubles as the target picker. Resolves "" for a new thread, a thread id, or null when cancelled.
function finishShare(value) {
  const resolve = shareResolve;
  if (!resolve) return;
  shareResolve = null;
  document.body.classList.remove("sharing");
  $("share-banner").hidden = true;
  resolve(value);
}
function pickShareTarget() {
  finishShare(null);
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    shareResolve = resolve;
    setLane("threads");
    document.body.classList.add("sharing");
    $("share-banner").hidden = false;
    setNav(true);
  });
}
$("share-cancel").onclick = () => setNav(false);
function openTaskDialog(task) {
  setNav(false);
  setDialogKind(task || "new");
  $("dlg").showModal();
  $(task ? "d-prompt" : "d-title").focus();
}
$("new-task").onclick = () => openTaskDialog();
// Threads | Scheduled: two views of the same sidebar place, remembered per device
function setLane(lane, focus = false) {
  state.lane = lane;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem("ac_lane", lane);
  } catch {}
  for (const [name, tab, pane] of [
    ["threads", "lane-threads", "pane-threads"],
    ["tasks", "lane-tasks", "pane-tasks"],
    ["auto", "lane-auto", "pane-auto"],
  ]) {
    $(tab).setAttribute("aria-selected", String(lane === name));
    $(tab).tabIndex = lane === name ? 0 : -1;
    $(pane).hidden = lane !== name;
  }
  if (focus) $(`lane-${lane}`).focus();
}
$("lane-threads").onclick = () => setLane("threads");
$("lane-tasks").onclick = () => setLane("tasks");
$("lane-auto").onclick = () => setLane("auto");
$("lanes").addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
  if (!["ArrowLeft", "ArrowRight", "Home", "End"].includes(e.key)) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const order = ["threads", "tasks", "auto"],
    at = order.indexOf(state.lane);
  setLane(
    e.key === "Home"
      ? order[0]
      : e.key === "End"
        ? order.at(-1)
        : order[(at + (e.key === "ArrowRight" ? 1 : order.length - 1)) % order.length],
    true,
  );
});
setLane(state.lane);
$("new").onclick = () => {
  if (!shareResolve) return openNewDialog();
  finishShare("");
};
$("archive-toggle").onclick = () => {
  state.archiveOpen = !state.archiveOpen;
  localStorage.setItem("ac_archive_open", state.archiveOpen ? "1" : "");
  renderThreads();
};
$("reload").onclick = () => location.reload();

    else if (v === "ideas") location.href = $("ideas-link").href;

$("dform").addEventListener("submit", (e) => {
  if (e.submitter?.value !== "ok") {
    workflowBrief = null;
    return;
  }
  const settings = {
    provider: menus.dProv.value,
    cwd: menus.dCwd.value,
    model: menus.dModel.value,
    effort: menus.dEffort.value,
    mode: menus.dMode.value,
  };
  const account =
    settings.provider === "claude" ? { claudeAccount: menus.dAccount.value || "primary" } : {};
  if (dialogAssignment) {
    e.preventDefault();
    return void submitPremiumAssignment(settings, account);
  }
  if (dialogFork) {
    const { cwd, ...pick } = settings;
    return void send({
      t: "fork",
      thread: dialogFork,
      ...pick,
      ...account,
      context: menus.dContext.value,
    });
  }
  if (dialogTask === null)
    return void send({
      t: "create",
      ...settings,
      ...(settings.provider === "claude" ? { claudeAccount: menus.dAccount.value || "primary" } : {}),
    });
  send({
    t: "task_save",
    ...(dialogTask !== "new" ? { id: dialogTask.id } : {}),
    ...settings,
    title: $("d-title").value,
    prompt: $("d-prompt").value,
    repeat: menus.dRepeat.value,
    day: Number(menus.dDay.value),
    time: $("d-time").value || "09:00",
    tz: Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone,
    saveOutput: $("d-save").checked,
  });
  setLane("tasks");
});

if ("serviceWorker" in navigator) {
  navigator.serviceWorker.register("/sw.js").catch(() => {});
  navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener("message", (event) => {
    if (event.data?.type !== "notification-open") return;
    let url;
    try {
      url = new URL(event.data.url, location.origin);
    } catch {
      return;
    }
    if (url.origin !== location.origin || url.pathname !== "/") return;
    const id = new URLSearchParams(url.hash.slice(1)).get("thread");
    if (id && state.ready && state.threads.has(id)) openThread(id);
    location.hash = url.hash;
  });
}

  attachmentOrder = Math.max(attachmentOrder, p.order || 0);
  const list = attachmentDrafts.get(p.thread) || [];
  list.push(p);
  attachmentDrafts.set(p.thread, list);

try {
  const recovered = Object.keys(localStorage)
    .filter((key) => key.startsWith("ac_sending:"))
    .map((key) => ({ key, draft: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)) }));
  recovered.sort(
    (a, b) => b.draft.createdAt - a.draft.createdAt || b.draft.sequence - a.draft.sequence,
  );
  for (const { key, draft } of recovered) {
    if (draft?.thread && typeof draft.text === "string" && Array.isArray(draft.attachments)) {
      const cid = key.slice("ac_sending:".length);
      if (draft.accepted) await finishSendingDraft(cid, draft);
      else await recoverSendingDraft(cid, draft);
    }
  }
} catch {
  toast("A message recovery copy could not be loaded.");
}

// Module worker for ```arch diagrams (public/arch.js): routes all edges in one libavoid (WASM) orthogonal pass.
// libavoid's embind glue needs eval, so it runs here under this script's own CSP (server/index.ts), never in the page.
// Ported from brain/tools/pr-architecture/capture.mjs without its side-choice search: each edge takes its preferred
// sides. Request {requestId, boxes: Map id -> box, headers, edges}; reply {requestId, routes, labels} or {requestId, error}.
import { AvoidLib } from "/vendor/libavoid/index.js";

// Served as application/wasm by express.static, so Emscripten uses instantiateStreaming; it falls back to
// ArrayBuffer instantiation by itself when streaming fails.
const WASM_URL = "/vendor/libavoid/libavoid.wasm";
const avoidReady = AvoidLib.load(WASM_URL).then(() => AvoidLib.getInstance());

// Facing sides on the separated axis (capture.mjs candidates()[0]).
function preferredSides(source, target) {
  const across = source.l + source.r <= target.l + target.r ? ["r", "l"] : ["l", "r"];
  const down = source.t + source.b <= target.t + target.b ? ["b", "t"] : ["t", "b"];
  const separatedX = target.l >= source.r || source.l >= target.r;
  const separatedY = target.t >= source.b || source.t >= target.b;
  return separatedY && !separatedX ? down : across;
}

// Ends sharing a side sit symmetrically about its centre, GAP px apart, ordered by where the other box lies.
const GAP = 24;
function collectEnds(edges, sides, boxes) {
  const ends = new Map();
  for (const [edgeIndex, edge] of edges.entries()) {
    const pairs = [
      [edge.from, edge.to, sides[edgeIndex][0], 0],
      [edge.to, edge.from, sides[edgeIndex][1], 1],
    ];
    for (const [endId, otherId, side, slot] of pairs) {
      const key = `${endId}:${side}`;
      if (!ends.has(key)) ends.set(key, []);
      const other = boxes.get(otherId);
      const horizontal = side === "l" || side === "r";
      const aim = horizontal ? (other.t + other.b) / 2 : (other.l + other.r) / 2;
      ends.get(key).push({ edgeIndex, slot, box: boxes.get(endId), side, aim });
    }
  }
  return ends;
}

function spreadEnds(edges, sides, boxes) {
  const offsets = edges.map(() => [0.5, 0.5]);
  for (const list of collectEnds(edges, sides, boxes).values()) {
    const { box, side } = list[0];
    const length = side === "l" || side === "r" ? box.b - box.t : box.r - box.l;
    const gap = Math.min(GAP / Math.max(length, 1), 0.8 / Math.max(1, list.length - 1));
    list.sort((first, second) => first.aim - second.aim || first.edgeIndex - second.edgeIndex);
    for (const [position, end] of list.entries())
      offsets[end.edgeIndex][end.slot] = 0.5 + (position - (list.length - 1) / 2) * gap;
  }
  return offsets;
}

// Outward unit normal and libavoid ConnDirFlags (up 1, down 2, left 4, right 8) per side.
const NORMAL = { t: [0, -1, 1], b: [0, 1, 2], l: [-1, 0, 4], r: [1, 0, 8] };
const STUB = 20;

function sidePoint(box, side, offset) {
  if (side === "l" || side === "r")
    return [side === "l" ? box.l : box.r, box.t + offset * (box.b - box.t)];
  return [box.l + offset * (box.r - box.l), side === "t" ? box.t : box.b];
}

function pinPosition(side, offset) {
  if (side === "l") return [0, offset];
  if (side === "r") return [1, offset];
  return side === "t" ? [offset, 0] : [offset, 1];
}

// One router for one pass. Every value object (points, rectangles, ends) is tracked and freed in release(); the
// router owns shapes, pins and connectors and frees them when it is deleted.
function createRouter(Avoid, layout) {
  const router = new Avoid.Router(Avoid.RouterFlag.OrthogonalRouting.value);
  const temps = [];
  const track = (value) => {
    temps.push(value);
    return value;
  };
  const point = (pointX, pointY) => track(new Avoid.Point(pointX, pointY));
  const rectangle = (box) => track(new Avoid.Rectangle(point(box.l, box.t), point(box.r, box.b)));
  const param = Avoid.RoutingParameter;
  router.setRoutingParameter(param.shapeBufferDistance, 18);
  router.setRoutingParameter(param.idealNudgingDistance, 14);
  router.setRoutingParameter(param.segmentPenalty, 40);
  router.setRoutingParameter(param.crossingPenalty, 400);
  router.setRoutingOption(Avoid.RoutingOption.nudgeOrthogonalSegmentsConnectedToShapes, true);
  router.setRoutingOption(Avoid.RoutingOption.nudgeSharedPathsWithCommonEndPoint, true);
  const shapes = new Map();
  for (const box of layout.boxes.values())
    if (!box.group) shapes.set(box.id, new Avoid.ShapeRef(router, rectangle(box)));
  for (const header of layout.headers) new Avoid.ShapeRef(router, rectangle(header));
  const release = () => {
    for (const value of temps) value.delete();
    router.delete();
  };
  return { Avoid, router, shapes, point, track, release, pins: new Map(), nextPin: 1 };
}

// Groups are boundaries, not obstacles: their ends start just outside the border and get a stub afterwards.
// Pins are created on demand, one per distinct offset.
function connEnd(ctx, box, side, offset) {
  const { Avoid } = ctx;
  const shape = ctx.shapes.get(box.id);
  if (!shape) {
    const [pointX, pointY] = sidePoint(box, side, offset);
    const [normalX, normalY] = NORMAL[side];
    return ctx.track(
      new Avoid.ConnEnd(ctx.point(pointX + normalX * STUB, pointY + normalY * STUB)),
    );
  }
  const key = `${box.id}:${side}:${offset.toFixed(4)}`;
  if (!ctx.pins.has(key)) {
    const pin = ctx.nextPin++;
    const [pinX, pinY] = pinPosition(side, offset);
    new Avoid.ShapeConnectionPin(shape, pin, pinX, pinY, true, 0, NORMAL[side][2]).setExclusive(
      false,
    );
    ctx.pins.set(key, pin);
  }
  return ctx.track(new Avoid.ConnEnd(shape, ctx.pins.get(key)));
}

function readRoute(connector) {
  const line = connector.displayRoute();
  const points = [];
  for (let index = 0; index < line.size(); index++) {
    const point = line.at(index);
    points.push([Math.round(point.x * 10) / 10, Math.round(point.y * 10) / 10]);
    point.delete();
  }
  line.delete();
  return points;
}

const nearlyEqual = (first, second) => Math.abs(first - second) < 0.5;
const collinear = (before, middle, after) =>
  (nearlyEqual(before[0], middle[0]) && nearlyEqual(middle[0], after[0])) ||
  (nearlyEqual(before[1], middle[1]) && nearlyEqual(middle[1], after[1]));

function dropRedundantPoints(points) {
  const clean = [points[0]];
  for (const point of points.slice(1)) {
    const last = clean[clean.length - 1];
    if (nearlyEqual(point[0], last[0]) && nearlyEqual(point[1], last[1])) continue;
    if (clean.length >= 2 && collinear(clean[clean.length - 2], last, point)) clean.pop();
    clean.push(point);
  }
  return clean;
}

// Adds the border stub for group ends so every wire meets its box at 90 degrees.
function finishRoute(points, ends) {
  const [fromEnd, toEnd] = ends;
  const result = points.slice();
  if (fromEnd.box.group) result.unshift(sidePoint(fromEnd.box, fromEnd.side, fromEnd.offset));
  if (toEnd.box.group) result.push(sidePoint(toEnd.box, toEnd.side, toEnd.offset));
  return dropRedundantPoints(result);
}

/** Routes every edge in one libavoid transaction; returns one point list per edge (canvas coordinates). */
function routeEdges(Avoid, layout, edges) {
  const { boxes } = layout;
  const sides = edges.map((edge) => preferredSides(boxes.get(edge.from), boxes.get(edge.to)));
  const offsets = spreadEnds(edges, sides, boxes);
  const ctx = createRouter(Avoid, layout);
  try {
    const plans = edges.map((edge, index) => [
      { box: boxes.get(edge.from), side: sides[index][0], offset: offsets[index][0] },
      { box: boxes.get(edge.to), side: sides[index][1], offset: offsets[index][1] },
    ]);
    const connectors = plans.map(([fromEnd, toEnd]) => {
      const source = connEnd(ctx, fromEnd.box, fromEnd.side, fromEnd.offset);
      const target = connEnd(ctx, toEnd.box, toEnd.side, toEnd.offset);
      const connector = new Avoid.ConnRef(ctx.router, source, target);
      connector.setHateCrossings(true);
      return connector;
    });
    ctx.router.processTransaction();
    return connectors.map((connector, index) => finishRoute(readRoute(connector), plans[index]));
  } finally {
    ctx.release();
  }
}

// Point halfway along the polyline.
function midpoint(points) {
  const lengths = points
    .slice(1)
    .map((point, index) => Math.hypot(point[0] - points[index][0], point[1] - points[index][1]));
  let remaining = lengths.reduce((sum, length) => sum + length, 0) / 2;
  for (const [index, length] of lengths.entries()) {
    if (remaining <= length && length > 0) {
      const share = remaining / length;
      const [start, end] = [points[index], points[index + 1]];
      return [start[0] + (end[0] - start[0]) * share, start[1] + (end[1] - start[1]) * share];
    }
    remaining -= length;
  }
  return points[0];
}

self.onmessage = async ({ data }) => {
  const { requestId, boxes, headers, edges } = data;
  try {
    const Avoid = await avoidReady;
    const started = performance.now();
    const routes = routeEdges(Avoid, { boxes, headers }, edges);
    const labels = routes.map((points) => (points.length ? midpoint(points) : null));
    self.postMessage({ requestId, routes, labels, routerMs: performance.now() - started });
  } catch (err) {
    self.postMessage({ requestId, error: String(err?.message || err) });
  }
};

================
File: public/arch.js
================
// ```arch blocks: renders a JSON architecture spec (same format as the PR architecture diagrams, see
// brain/skills/pr-evidence) as a styled diagram: title, nested groups, nodes (icon tile, label, tech, desc, status pill,
// step badge) and a legend. Icons come only from the vendored Lucide map (/vendor/lucide/icons.json); anything else
// (d2:, file paths, unknown names) gets a fallback glyph. Nothing is fetched from remote URLs.
// Arrows: routeArch(container) routes in a libavoid (WASM) module worker and draws the edges (container.__archEdges) as an SVG overlay.

export const COLORS = {
  blue: "#2563eb",
  indigo: "#4f46e5",
  purple: "#7c3aed",
  pink: "#db2777",
  orange: "#ea580c",
  teal: "#0d9488",
  cyan: "#0891b2",
  slate: "#475569",
};
export const STATUSES = {
  unchanged: "Unchanged",
  added: "Added",
  modified: "Modified",
  removed: "Removed",
};
const BOUNDARIES = ["solid", "dashed", "none"];
const NODE_KEYS = ["id", "label", "tech", "desc", "icon", "color", "status", "step"];
const GROUP_KEYS = [
  "id",
  "group",
  "sub",
  "icon",
  "color",
  "border",
  "direction",
  "status",
  "step",
  "items",
];
const EDGE_KEYS = ["from", "to", "label", "step", "status", "both", "dashed"];
const ROOT_KEYS = ["title", "subtitle", "note", "direction", "items", "edges"];
const isObject = (value) => value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
const isText = (value) => typeof value === "string" && value.trim() !== "";

// ---------- validation ----------
function checkKeys(item, allowed, where, errors) {
  const unknown = Object.keys(item).filter((key) => !allowed.includes(key));
  if (unknown.length) errors.push(`${where}: unknown keys ${unknown.join(", ")}`);
}

function checkChoice(item, field, choices, where, errors) {
  if (item[field] !== undefined && !choices.includes(item[field]))
    errors.push(`${where}: ${field} must be one of ${choices.join(", ")}`);
}

function checkStep(item, where, errors) {
  const step = item.step;
  if (step === undefined) return;
  const ok = (typeof step === "number" || isText(step)) && typeof step !== "boolean";
  if (!ok) errors.push(`${where}: step must be a number or short string`);
}

function checkOptionalText(item, field, where, errors) {
  if (item[field] !== undefined && !isText(item[field]))
    errors.push(`${where}: ${field} must be a non-empty string`);
}

function checkCommon(item, allowed, where, errors) {
  checkKeys(item, allowed, where, errors);
  checkChoice(item, "color", Object.keys(COLORS), where, errors);
  checkChoice(item, "status", Object.keys(STATUSES), where, errors);
  checkStep(item, where, errors);
  checkChoice(item, "direction", ["row", "column"], where, errors);
}

function registerId(item, where, ids, errors) {
  if (!isText(item.id)) return errors.push(`${where}: id must be a non-empty string`);
  if (ids.has(item.id)) errors.push(`${where}: duplicate id ${item.id}`);
  ids.add(item.id);
}

function checkItems(items, where, ids, errors) {
  if (!Array.isArray(items) || !items.length) {
    errors.push(`${where}: items must be a non-empty list`);
    return;
  }
  for (const [index, item] of items.entries())
    checkItem(item, `${where} item ${index + 1}`, ids, errors);
}

function checkItem(item, where, ids, errors) {
  if (!isObject(item)) return errors.push(`${where}: each item must be an object`);
  if ("group" in item) {
    checkCommon(item, GROUP_KEYS, where, errors);
    checkChoice(item, "border", BOUNDARIES, where, errors);
    if (!isText(item.group)) errors.push(`${where}: group must be a non-empty string`);
    if ("id" in item) registerId(item, where, ids, errors);
    checkItems(item.items, where, ids, errors);
    return;
  }
  checkCommon(item, NODE_KEYS, where, errors);
  registerId(item, where, ids, errors);
  if (!isText(item.label)) errors.push(`${where}: label must be a non-empty string`);
}

function checkEdge(edge, index, ids, errors) {
  const where = `edge ${index + 1}`;
  if (!isObject(edge)) return errors.push(`${where}: must be an object`);
  checkKeys(edge, EDGE_KEYS, where, errors);
  for (const end of ["from", "to"])
    if (!ids.has(edge[end]))
      errors.push(`${where}: ${end} must reference a node or group id, got ${edge[end]}`);
  checkChoice(edge, "status", Object.keys(STATUSES), where, errors);
  checkStep(edge, where, errors);
  checkOptionalText(edge, "label", where, errors);
  if (isText(edge.label) && edge.label.length > 40)
    errors.push(`${where}: label must be 40 characters or fewer`);
}

/** Returns a list of error strings; empty when the spec is valid. */
export function validateArch(spec) {
  const errors = [];
  if (!isObject(spec)) return ["spec must be a JSON object"];
  checkKeys(spec, ROOT_KEYS, "spec", errors);
  if (!isText(spec.title)) errors.push("spec: title must be a non-empty string");
  checkOptionalText(spec, "subtitle", "spec", errors);
  checkOptionalText(spec, "note", "spec", errors);
  checkChoice(spec, "direction", ["row", "column"], "spec", errors);
  const ids = new Set();
  checkItems(spec.items, "spec", ids, errors);
  const edges = spec.edges ?? [];
  if (!Array.isArray(edges)) errors.push("edges must be a list");
  else for (const [index, edge] of edges.entries()) checkEdge(edge, index, ids, errors);
  return errors;
}

// ---------- icons ----------
let iconMap = null;
let iconLoad = null;

/** Tests (or callers with their own map) can provide the name -> inner SVG markup map directly. */
export function setArchIcons(map) {
  iconMap = map;
}

function loadIcons() {
  if (iconMap) return Promise.resolve(iconMap);
  iconLoad ||= fetch("/vendor/lucide/icons.json")
    .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : {}))
    .catch(() => ({}))
    .then((map) => (iconMap = map));
  return iconLoad;
}

const SVG_NS = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
const FALLBACK_GLYPH = '<rect width="18" height="18" x="3" y="3" rx="2"/>';

function svgGlyph(inner) {
  const svg = document.createElementNS(SVG_NS, "svg");
  svg.setAttribute("viewBox", "0 0 24 24");
  svg.setAttribute("fill", "none");
  svg.setAttribute("stroke", "currentColor");
  svg.setAttribute("stroke-linecap", "round");
  svg.setAttribute("stroke-linejoin", "round");
  svg.setAttribute("stroke-width", "2");
  svg.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  svg.setAttribute("class", "glyph");
  svg.innerHTML = inner; // vendored Lucide markup or the fixed fallback only
  return svg;
}

function fillIcon(holder, map) {
  holder.replaceChildren(svgGlyph(map[holder.dataset.icon] || FALLBACK_GLYPH));
}

function iconHolder(tag, cls, name) {
  const holder = document.createElement(tag);
  holder.className = cls;
  holder.dataset.icon = typeof name === "string" ? name : "box";
  fillIcon(holder, iconMap || {});
  return holder;
}

// ---------- DOM ----------
function div(cls, text) {
  const node = document.createElement("div");
  node.className = cls;
  if (text !== undefined) node.textContent = text;
  return node;
}

function span(cls, text) {
  const node = document.createElement("span");
  node.className = cls;
  node.textContent = text;
  return node;
}

const stepBadge = (step) => (step === undefined ? null : span("step", String(step)));
const statusPill = (status) => (status === "unchanged" ? null : span("pill", STATUSES[status]));
const accent = (item, fallback) => COLORS[item.color] || COLORS[fallback];

function buildNode(node) {
  const status = node.status || "unchanged";
  const root = div(`node ${status}`);
  root.dataset.id = node.id;
  root.style.setProperty("--accent", accent(node, "blue"));
  const tile = iconHolder("div", "tile", node.icon || "box");
  const parts = [stepBadge(node.step), statusPill(status), tile, div("label", node.label)];
  if (node.tech) parts.push(div("tech", node.tech));
  if (node.desc) parts.push(div("desc", node.desc));
  root.append(...parts.filter(Boolean));
  return root;
}

function groupHeader(group, status) {
  const header = document.createElement("header");
  const title = span("gtitle", group.group);
  if (group.sub) {
    const small = document.createElement("small");
    small.textContent = group.sub;
    title.append(small);
  }
  const icon = group.icon ? iconHolder("span", "gicon", group.icon) : null;
  header.append(...[stepBadge(group.step), icon, title, statusPill(status)].filter(Boolean));
  return header;
}

function buildGroup(group) {
  const status = group.status || "unchanged";
  const border = group.border || "dashed";
  const quiet = status === "unchanged" ? " quiet" : "";
  const root = document.createElement("section");
  root.className = `group ${border} ${status}${quiet}`;
  if (group.id) root.dataset.id = group.id;
  root.style.setProperty("--accent", accent(group, "slate"));
  root.append(groupHeader(group, status), buildItems(group.items, group.direction || "column"));
  return root;
}

function buildItems(items, direction) {
  const box = div(`items ${direction}`);
  for (const item of items) box.append("group" in item ? buildGroup(item) : buildNode(item));
  return box;
}

function buildLegend(hasSteps) {
  const legend = div("legend");
  legend.append(document.createElement("b"));
  legend.firstChild.textContent = "Legend";
  for (const [name, label] of Object.entries(STATUSES)) {
    const key = span("key", label);
    const swatch = document.createElement("i");
    swatch.className = `${name} ${name}-fill`;
    key.prepend(swatch);
    legend.append(key);
  }
  if (hasSteps) {
    const key = span("key", "Step order");
    key.prepend(span("step", "1"));
    legend.append(key);
  }
  return legend;
}

const hasStep = (items) =>
  items.some((item) => item.step !== undefined || (item.items && hasStep(item.items)));

function normaliseEdge(edge) {
  return {
    from: edge.from,
    to: edge.to,
    label: (edge.label || "").trim(),
    step: edge.step === undefined ? null : String(edge.step),
    status: edge.status || "unchanged",
    both: Boolean(edge.both),
    dashed: Boolean(edge.dashed),
  };
}

function buildHeading(spec) {
  const heading = document.createElement("h1");
  heading.textContent = spec.title;
  const parts = [heading];
  if (spec.subtitle)
    parts.push(
      Object.assign(document.createElement("p"), {
        className: "subtitle",
        textContent: spec.subtitle,
      }),
    );
  if (spec.note) parts.push(span("note", spec.note));
  return parts;
}

let styled = false;
function ensureStyle() {
  if (styled || document.getElementById("arch-style")) return;
  const style = document.createElement("style");
  style.id = "arch-style";
  style.textContent = ARCH_CSS;
  document.head.append(style);
  styled = true;
}

/**
 * Builds the diagram DOM for a validated spec into `container`. Returns a promise that resolves once the Lucide icons
 * are filled in and routing has run. Throws if the spec is invalid.
 */
export function buildArch(spec, container) {
  const errors = validateArch(spec);
  if (errors.length) throw new Error(errors.join("; "));
  ensureStyle();
  container.classList.add("arch");
  const canvas = div("canvas");
  canvas.append(buildItems(spec.items, spec.direction || "row"));
  container.replaceChildren(
    ...buildHeading(spec),
    canvas,
    buildLegend(hasStep(spec.items) || (spec.edges || []).some((e) => e.step !== undefined)),
  );
  container.__archEdges = (spec.edges || []).map(normaliseEdge);
  container.__archCanvas = canvas;
  return loadIcons().then((map) => {
    for (const holder of container.querySelectorAll("[data-icon]")) fillIcon(holder, map);
    return routeArch(container);
  });
}

// ---------- routing: measured here, routed by libavoid in a module worker (public/arch-route-worker.js) ----------
// libavoid's embind glue needs eval, which the page CSP forbids; the worker script carries its own CSP.
const WORKER_URL = "/arch-route-worker.js";
const REROUTE_DELAY = 100;
const ROUTE_TIMEOUT = 15000;
let routeWorker = null;
let nextRequest = 1;
const pending = new Map(); // requestId -> { resolve, reject, timer }

function settle(requestId, outcome) {
  const entry = pending.get(requestId);
  if (!entry) return;
  pending.delete(requestId);
  clearTimeout(entry.timer);
  outcome(entry);
}

// A failed worker (load error, WASM abort) is dropped; the next pass starts a fresh one.
function dropWorker(reason) {
  routeWorker?.terminate();
  routeWorker = null;
  for (const requestId of [...pending.keys()])
    settle(requestId, (entry) => entry.reject(new Error(reason)));
}

function getWorker() {
  if (routeWorker) return routeWorker;
  routeWorker = new Worker(WORKER_URL, { type: "module", name: "arch-route" });
  routeWorker.onmessage = ({ data }) => {
    if (data.error) dropWorker(data.error);
    else settle(data.requestId, (entry) => entry.resolve(data));
  };
  routeWorker.onerror = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault?.();
    dropWorker(event.message || "arch route worker failed to load");
  };
  return routeWorker;
}

// One shared worker, reused across diagrams and passes; WASM loads once per worker.
function requestRoutes(layout, edges) {
  const requestId = nextRequest++;
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => dropWorker("arch routing timed out"), ROUTE_TIMEOUT);
    pending.set(requestId, { resolve, reject, timer });
    getWorker().postMessage({ requestId, ...layout, edges });
  });
}

// Route cache: same edges, node rects (rounded) and canvas size give the same routes, so re-renders and
// streaming of an unchanged diagram skip the worker. In memory, least recently used out past ROUTE_CACHE_SIZE.
const ROUTE_CACHE_SIZE = 30;
const routeCache = new Map();

function hashText(text) {
  let djb = 5381;
  let fnv = 2166136261;
  for (let index = 0; index < text.length; index++) {
    const code = text.charCodeAt(index);
    djb = ((djb << 5) + djb + code) | 0;
    fnv = Math.imul(fnv ^ code, 16777619);
  }
  return `${(djb >>> 0).toString(36)}.${(fnv >>> 0).toString(36)}.${text.length}`;
}

function routeKey(layout, edges, size) {
  const round = (box) =>
    [box.id, box.l, box.t, box.r, box.b].map((value) =>
      typeof value === "number" ? Math.round(value) : value,
    );
  const rects = [...layout.boxes.values()].map(round);
  const headers = layout.headers.map((box) => [box.l, box.t, box.r, box.b].map(Math.round));
  return hashText(JSON.stringify([edges, rects, headers, size]));
}

function cachedRoutes(key) {
  const hit = routeCache.get(key);
  if (hit) {
    routeCache.delete(key);
    routeCache.set(key, hit);
  }
  return hit;
}

function rememberRoutes(key, result) {
  routeCache.set(key, result);
  if (routeCache.size > ROUTE_CACHE_SIZE) routeCache.delete(routeCache.keys().next().value);
}

function relativeRect(element, origin) {
  const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
  return {
    l: rect.left - origin.left,
    t: rect.top - origin.top,
    r: rect.right - origin.left,
    b: rect.bottom - origin.top,
  };
}

function unionRect(rects) {
  return {
    l: Math.min(...rects.map((rect) => rect.l)),
    t: Math.min(...rects.map((rect) => rect.t)),
    r: Math.max(...rects.map((rect) => rect.r)),
    b: Math.max(...rects.map((rect) => rect.b)),
  };
}

// Node and group boxes plus group titles (obstacles, so wires never cross them), relative to the canvas.
function measureCanvas(canvas) {
  const origin = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  const boxes = new Map();
  for (const element of canvas.querySelectorAll("[data-id]")) {
    const group = element.classList.contains("group");
    boxes.set(element.dataset.id, {
      id: element.dataset.id,
      group,
      ...relativeRect(element, origin),
    });
  }
  const headers = [];
  for (const header of canvas.querySelectorAll(".group > header")) {
    const parts = [...header.children].map((part) => relativeRect(part, origin));
    if (parts.length) headers.push(unionRect(parts));
  }
  return { boxes, headers };
}

// ---------- overlay ----------
const ROUND = 10;
function roundedPath(points) {
  let path = `M${points[0][0]},${points[0][1]}`;
  for (let index = 1; index < points.length - 1; index++) {
    const [prev, corner, next] = [points[index - 1], points[index], points[index + 1]];
    const before = Math.min(ROUND, Math.hypot(corner[0] - prev[0], corner[1] - prev[1]) / 2);
    const after = Math.min(ROUND, Math.hypot(next[0] - corner[0], next[1] - corner[1]) / 2);
    const inX = Math.sign(corner[0] - prev[0]);
    const inY = Math.sign(corner[1] - prev[1]);
    const outX = Math.sign(next[0] - corner[0]);
    const outY = Math.sign(next[1] - corner[1]);
    path += ` L${corner[0] - inX * before},${corner[1] - inY * before}`;
    path += ` Q${corner[0]},${corner[1]} ${corner[0] + outX * after},${corner[1] + outY * after}`;
  }
  const last = points[points.length - 1];
  return `${path} L${last[0]},${last[1]}`;
}

function svgElement(tag, attrs) {
  const element = document.createElementNS(SVG_NS, tag);
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(attrs)) element.setAttribute(name, String(value));
  return element;
}

// Colours are CSS variables of the node pill palette, so a theme switch recolours wires without a reroute.
function arrowMarkers(prefix) {
  const defs = svgElement("defs", {});
  for (const status of Object.keys(STATUSES)) {
    const marker = svgElement("marker", {
      id: `${prefix}-${status}`,
      viewBox: "0 0 10 10",
      refX: 9,
      refY: 5,
      markerWidth: 7,
      markerHeight: 7,
      orient: "auto-start-reverse",
    });
    const tip = svgElement("path", { d: "M0,0 L10,5 L0,10 z" });
    tip.style.fill = `var(--a-${status})`;
    marker.append(tip);
    defs.append(marker);
  }
  return defs;
}

function wirePath(edge, points, prefix) {
  const path = svgElement("path", {
    d: roundedPath(points),
    fill: "none",
    "stroke-width": edge.status === "unchanged" ? 1.75 : 2.5,
    "marker-end": `url(#${prefix}-${edge.status})`,
  });
  path.classList.add("wire", edge.status);
  path.style.stroke = `var(--a-${edge.status})`;
  if (edge.dashed || edge.status === "removed") path.setAttribute("stroke-dasharray", "7 5");
  if (edge.both) path.setAttribute("marker-start", `url(#${prefix}-${edge.status})`);
  return path;
}

function edgeChip(edge) {
  const chip = div(`edge-label ${edge.status}`);
  if (edge.step) chip.append(span("step", edge.step));
  if (edge.label) chip.append(span("text", edge.label));
  return chip;
}

const CHIP_PAD = 3;
const overlaps = (first, second) =>
  first.l < second.r + CHIP_PAD &&
  first.r > second.l - CHIP_PAD &&
  first.t < second.b + CHIP_PAD &&
  first.b > second.t - CHIP_PAD;

function longestSegment(points) {
  let best = [points[0], points[1]];
  let bestLength = -1;
  for (const [index, start] of points.slice(0, -1).entries()) {
    const end = points[index + 1];
    const length = Math.hypot(end[0] - start[0], end[1] - start[1]);
    if (length > bestLength) [best, bestLength] = [[start, end], length];
  }
  return best;
}

// Chip centres to try: the path midpoint, then ±25% and ±50% from the centre of the longest segment.
function chipSpots(points, midpoint) {
  const [start, end] = longestSegment(points);
  const along = (share) => [
    start[0] + (end[0] - start[0]) * share,
    start[1] + (end[1] - start[1]) * share,
  ];
  return [midpoint, along(0.75), along(0.25), along(1), along(0)];
}

// First spot whose chip box clears every node and every chip placed so far; else the path midpoint.
function placeChip(chip, spots, obstacles) {
  const halfWidth = chip.offsetWidth / 2;
  const halfHeight = chip.offsetHeight / 2;
  const boxAt = ([left, top]) => ({
    l: left - halfWidth,
    r: left + halfWidth,
    t: top - halfHeight,
    b: top + halfHeight,
  });
  const spot =
    spots.find((candidate) => !obstacles.some((other) => overlaps(boxAt(candidate), other))) ||
    spots[0];
  chip.style.left = `${spot[0]}px`;
  chip.style.top = `${spot[1]}px`;
  obstacles.push(boxAt(spot));
}

let overlayCount = 0;
function drawOverlay(canvas, edges, { routes, labels }, layout) {
  for (const old of canvas.querySelectorAll(":scope > svg.wires, :scope > .edge-label"))
    old.remove();
  const prefix = `arch-arrow-${++overlayCount}`;
  const svg = svgElement("svg", { class: "wires", "aria-hidden": "true" });
  svg.append(arrowMarkers(prefix));
  const chips = [];
  for (const [index, points] of routes.entries()) {
    if (points.length < 2) continue;
    const edge = edges[index];
    svg.append(wirePath(edge, points, prefix));
    if (edge.label || edge.step)
      chips.push({ chip: edgeChip(edge), spots: chipSpots(points, labels[index]) });
  }
  canvas.append(svg, ...chips.map((item) => item.chip));
  const obstacles = [...layout.boxes.values()].filter((box) => !box.group);
  for (const { chip, spots } of chips) placeChip(chip, spots, obstacles);
}

// ---------- routing lifecycle ----------
const canvasSize = (canvas) => `${canvas.offsetWidth}x${canvas.offsetHeight}`;
const laidOut = (canvas) =>
  canvas.isConnected &&
  typeof canvas.getBoundingClientRect === "function" &&
  canvas.offsetWidth > 0;

async function routeNow(container) {
  const canvas = container.__archCanvas;
  const edges = container.__archEdges;
  if (!laidOut(canvas)) return; // not attached or hidden yet: the ResizeObserver routes once it has a size
  // Late web fonts can widen a group title without resizing the canvas: measure once they are in.
  if (document.fonts && document.fonts.status !== "loaded") await document.fonts.ready;
  if (canvas !== container.__archCanvas || !laidOut(canvas)) return;
  const size = canvasSize(canvas);
  container.__archSize = size;
  const started = performance.now();
  const layout = measureCanvas(canvas);
  const key = routeKey(layout, edges, size);
  let result = cachedRoutes(key);
  container.dataset.routeCache = result ? "hit" : "miss";
  if (!result) {
    result = await requestRoutes(layout, edges);
    rememberRoutes(key, result);
    if (canvas !== container.__archCanvas || canvasSize(canvas) !== container.__archSize) return; // rebuilt or resized meanwhile
  }
  drawOverlay(canvas, edges, result, layout);
  container.dataset.routeMs = (performance.now() - started).toFixed(1);
  container.dataset.routerMs = result.routerMs.toFixed(1); // libavoid pass alone, inside the worker
}

function safeRoute(container) {
  return routeNow(container).catch((err) => {
    // Routing is decoration: the boxes stay readable without wires.
    container.dataset.routeError = String(err?.message || err);
    console.warn("arch: edge routing failed", err);
  });
}

// Debounced reroute when the canvas changes size. The overlay is absolutely positioned inside the canvas, so
// drawing it never resizes the canvas and cannot retrigger the observer.
function watchResize(container) {
  container.__archObserver?.disconnect();
  if (typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined") return;
  const canvas = container.__archCanvas;
  let timer = 0;
  const observer = new ResizeObserver(() => {
    if (!laidOut(canvas) || canvasSize(canvas) === container.__archSize) return;
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (canvasSize(canvas) !== container.__archSize) safeRoute(container);
    }, REROUTE_DELAY);
  });
  observer.observe(canvas);
  container.__archObserver = observer;
}

/**
 * Draws container.__archEdges over container.__archCanvas: libavoid routes all edges in one orthogonal pass in a
 * lazily started module worker, then an SVG overlay gets paths, arrowheads, label chips and step badges. Reroutes
 * on resize. The last pass's duration in ms (measure + worker round trip + draw; the first pass also includes the
 * worker start and WASM load) is in data-route-ms.
 */
export function routeArch(container) {
  const canvas = container.__archCanvas;
  if (!canvas || !container.__archEdges?.length) return Promise.resolve();
  watchResize(container);
  return safeRoute(container);
}

================
File: public/attachment-drafts.js

// Store binary drafts outside localStorage so large files never block typing.
export function attachmentDraftStore(onError) {
  let database;
  const opened = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const request = indexedDB.open("ac_composer", 1);
    request.onupgradeneeded = () =>
      request.result.createObjectStore("attachments", { keyPath: "key" });
    request.onsuccess = () => {
      database = request.result;
      database.onversionchange = () => database.close();
      resolve(database);
    };
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
    request.onblocked = () => reject(new Error("Draft storage is blocked by another tab."));
  }).catch((error) => {
    onError(error);
    return null;
  });
  async function transact(mode, operation) {
    const db = await opened;
    if (!db) return;
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const transaction = db.transaction("attachments", mode);
      const request = operation(transaction.objectStore("attachments"));
      transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve(request.result);
      transaction.onerror = transaction.onabort = () => reject(transaction.error || request.error);
    }).catch((error) => {
      onError(error);
    });
  }
  return {
    load: async () =>
      ((await transact("readonly", (store) => store.getAll())) || []).sort(
        (a, b) => (a.order || 0) - (b.order || 0),
      ),
    save: (p) =>
      transact("readwrite", (store) =>
        store.put({
          key: p.key,
          thread: p.thread,
          name: p.name,
          kind: p.kind,
          size: p.size,
          order: p.order,
          n: p.n,
          id: p.id,
          prepared: p.prepared,
          labelPending: p.labelPending,
          ...(!p.id ? { blob: p.blob } : {}),
        }),
      ),
    remove: (key) => transact("readwrite", (store) => store.delete(key)),
  };
}

File: public/chat-search.js

// Search the rendered transcript without rewriting message nodes or disturbing copy/code handlers.
const SKIP_SELECTOR = "button,svg,.actions,.sr-only,.code-head,script,style,[hidden]";
const BLOCK_SELECTOR = "p,pre,li,td,th,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,summary,.bubble,.think-body";

function lookup(suffix) {
  return document.getElementById(`chat-search${suffix ? `-${suffix}` : ""}`);
}

function paintHighlights(ctx) {
  const all = new Highlight();
  for (const range of ctx.ranges) all.add(range);
  CSS.highlights.set("chat-search", all);
  CSS.highlights.set(
    "chat-search-active",
    new Highlight(...(ctx.ranges[ctx.index] ? [ctx.ranges[ctx.index]] : [])),
  );
}

function clearActiveMarks(ctx) {
  ctx.chat.querySelectorAll(".search-active").forEach((node) => {
    node.classList.remove("search-active");
  });
}

function countLabel(ctx) {
  const { ranges, index, query } = ctx;
  if (!query) return "Type to search";
  const loading = query && (ctx.history().older || ctx.history().busy);
  const position = ranges.length
    ? `${index + 1} of ${ranges.length}`
    : loading
      ? "Searching…"
      : "No matches";
  return `${position}${loading && ranges.length ? " · Searching…" : ""}`;
}

function paint(ctx) {
  if (ctx.supported) paintHighlights(ctx);
  clearActiveMarks(ctx);
  if (!ctx.supported)
    ctx.ranges[ctx.index]?.startContainer.parentElement
      ?.closest(".msg")
      ?.classList.add("search-active");
  ctx.count.textContent = countLabel(ctx);
  lookup("next").disabled = lookup("prev").disabled = !ctx.ranges.length;
}

function expandDetails(node, chat) {
  for (let parent = node; parent && parent !== chat; parent = parent.parentElement) {
    if (parent instanceof HTMLDetailsElement) parent.open = true;
  }
}

function expandToolOutput(node) {
  const output = node.closest(".tool-out");
  if (!output) return;
  output.classList.add("expanded");
  const more = output.querySelector(".more");
  if (more) {
    more.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
    more.textContent = "Show less";
  }
}

function centerRange(ctx, range, ticket) {
  if (ticket !== ctx.generation || ctx.bar.hidden || !range.startContainer.isConnected) return;
  const rect = range.getBoundingClientRect(),
    box = ctx.chat.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (rect.height) ctx.chat.scrollTop += rect.top + rect.height / 2 - box.top - box.height / 2;
}

function reveal(ctx) {
  const range = ctx.ranges[ctx.index];
  if (!range) return;
  ctx.navigate();
  const node = range.startContainer.parentElement;
  expandDetails(node, ctx.chat);
  expandToolOutput(node);
  const ticket = ctx.generation;
  node.scrollIntoView({ block: "center", inline: "nearest", behavior: "instant" });
  requestAnimationFrame(() => centerRange(ctx, range, ticket));
}

function collectTextParts(message) {
  const walker = document.createTreeWalker(message, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
  const parts = [];
  let text = "",
    lastBlock,
    node;
  while ((node = walker.nextNode())) {
    if (!node.data || node.parentElement.closest(SKIP_SELECTOR)) continue;
    const block = node.parentElement.closest(BLOCK_SELECTOR) || message;
    if (lastBlock && block !== lastBlock) text += "\n";
    parts.push({ node, start: text.length, end: text.length + node.length });
    text += node.data;
    lastBlock = block;
  }
  return { text, parts };
}

function appendMatchRanges(target, text, parts, pattern) {
  let firstIndex = 0,
    lastIndex = 0;
  for (const match of text.matchAll(pattern)) {
    const start = match.index,
      end = start + match[0].length;
    while (firstIndex < parts.length && parts[firstIndex].end <= start) firstIndex++;
    while (lastIndex < parts.length && parts[lastIndex].end < end) lastIndex++;
    const first = parts[firstIndex],
      last = parts[lastIndex];
    if (!first || !last) continue;
    const range = document.createRange();
    range.setStart(first.node, start - first.start);
    range.setEnd(last.node, end - last.start);
    target.push(range);
  }
}

function collectRanges(ctx) {
  ctx.prepare();
  const pattern = new RegExp(ctx.query.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&"), "giu");
  for (const message of ctx.chat.querySelectorAll(":scope > .msg")) {
    const { text, parts } = collectTextParts(message);
    appendMatchRanges(ctx.ranges, text, parts, pattern);
  }
}

function nextIndex(ctx, previous, previousIndex) {
  const { ranges } = ctx;
  const preserved =
    previous &&
    ranges.findIndex(
      (range) =>
        range.startContainer === previous.startContainer &&
        range.startOffset === previous.startOffset,
    );
  if (!ranges.length) return -1;
  return preserved >= 0 ? preserved : Math.max(0, Math.min(previousIndex, ranges.length - 1));
}

function scan(ctx) {
  clearTimeout(ctx.timer);
  ctx.timer = 0;
  if (ctx.bar.hidden) return;
  if (!ctx.current()) return close(ctx, false);
  if (ctx.history().busy) return; // The history completion hooks resume the search.
  const previous = ctx.ranges[ctx.index],
    previousIndex = ctx.index;
  ctx.ranges = [];
  if (ctx.query) collectRanges(ctx);
  ctx.index = nextIndex(ctx, previous, previousIndex);
  paint(ctx);
  if (ctx.jump && ctx.ranges.length) {
    ctx.jump = false;
    reveal(ctx);
  }
  // Older history is fetched only while a nonempty search is open, one existing page at a time.
  if (ctx.query && ctx.history().older) ctx.loadEarlier();
}

function refresh(ctx) {
  if (ctx.bar.hidden || ctx.timer) return;
  ctx.timer = setTimeout(() => scan(ctx), 120);
}

function open(ctx) {
  if (!ctx.current()) return;
  if (ctx.bar.hidden) {
    ctx.bar.hidden = false;
    ctx.toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
    ctx.observer.observe(ctx.chat, { childList: true, subtree: true, characterData: true });
    refresh(ctx);
  }
  ctx.input.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  ctx.input.select();
}

function close(ctx, focus = true) {
  ctx.generation++;
  clearTimeout(ctx.timer);
  ctx.timer = 0;
  ctx.observer.disconnect();
  ctx.bar.hidden = true;
  ctx.toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  ctx.input.value = ctx.query = "";
  ctx.ranges = [];
  ctx.index = -1;
  ctx.jump = false;
  if (ctx.supported) {
    CSS.highlights.delete("chat-search");
    CSS.highlights.delete("chat-search-active");
  }
  clearActiveMarks(ctx);
  if (focus) ctx.toggle.focus({ preventScroll: true });
}

function move(ctx, delta) {
  if (!ctx.ranges.length) return;
  ctx.index = (ctx.index + delta + ctx.ranges.length) % ctx.ranges.length;
  paint(ctx);
  reveal(ctx);
}

function handleInput(ctx) {
  ctx.generation++;
  ctx.query = ctx.input.value;
  ctx.ranges = [];
  ctx.index = -1;
  ctx.jump = true;
  paint(ctx);
  refresh(ctx);
}

function handleInputKey(ctx, event) {
  if (event.key === "Enter" && !event.isComposing) {
    event.preventDefault();
    move(ctx, event.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
  }
}

function isFindShortcut(ctx, event) {
  return (
    (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) &&
    !event.altKey &&
    event.key.toLowerCase() === "f" &&
    ctx.current()
  );
}

function handleDocumentKey(ctx, event) {
  if (event.defaultPrevented || document.querySelector("dialog[open]")) return;
  if (isFindShortcut(ctx, event)) {
    event.preventDefault();
    open(ctx);
  } else if (event.key === "Escape" && !ctx.bar.hidden) {
    event.preventDefault();
    close(ctx);
  }
}

export function createChatSearch({ current, history, loadEarlier, prepare, navigate }) {
  const ctx = {
    current,
    history,
    loadEarlier,
    prepare,
    navigate,
    bar: lookup(""),
    input: lookup("input"),
    count: lookup("count"),
    toggle: lookup("toggle"),
    chat: document.getElementById("chat"),
    ranges: [],
    index: -1,
    query: "",
    timer: undefined,
    jump: false,
    generation: 0,
    supported: !!(globalThis.CSS?.highlights && globalThis.Highlight),
    observer: null,
  };
  ctx.observer = new MutationObserver(() => refresh(ctx));
  ctx.input.addEventListener("input", () => handleInput(ctx));
  ctx.input.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => handleInputKey(ctx, event));
  ctx.toggle.onclick = () => (ctx.bar.hidden ? open(ctx) : close(ctx));
  lookup("close").onclick = () => close(ctx);
  lookup("next").onclick = () => move(ctx, 1);
  lookup("prev").onclick = () => move(ctx, -1);
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => handleDocumentKey(ctx, event));
  return {
    open: () => open(ctx),
    close: (focus) => close(ctx, focus),
    refresh: () => refresh(ctx),
    isOpen: () => !ctx.bar.hidden,
  };
}

================
File: public/composer-field.js
================
// The composer is a contenteditable, not a <textarea>: Chrome on Android (Gboard clipboard, keyboard stickers) only
// delivers pasted images to richly editable hosts, and answers "Chrome does not support image pasting here" for a
// <textarea> or a plaintext-only host. This gives the element the textarea surface app.js uses: value, disabled, placeholder, selection.
// Typing and paste keep "\n" inside text nodes; programmatic inserts (and some IMEs) wrap lines in <div>s. Read both.
const BLOCK = new Set(["DIV", "P"]);
const text = (root, stop, stopOffset) => {
  let out = "",
    seen = false,
    done = false;
  const walk = (n) => {
    if (done) return;
    if (n === stop && n.nodeType === 1) {
      for (let i = 0; i < stopOffset && i < n.childNodes.length; i++) walk(n.childNodes[i]);
      done = true;
      return;
    }
    if (n.nodeType === 3) {
      seen = true;
      if (n === stop) {
        out += n.data.slice(0, stopOffset);
        done = true;
      } else out += n.data;
    } else if (n.nodeName === "BR") {
      if (n.parentNode.childNodes.length > 1) {
        out += "\n";
        seen = true;
      }
    } else if (n.nodeType === 1) {
      if (BLOCK.has(n.nodeName)) {
        if (seen) out += "\n";
        seen = true;
      }
      for (const c of n.childNodes) walk(c);
    }
  };
  walk(root);
  return out;
};

export function composerField(box) {
  const offset = (node, off) => (box.contains(node) ? text(box, node, off).length : null);
  const locate = (pos) => {
    let left = pos;
    const it = document.createTreeWalker(box, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
    for (let n = it.nextNode(); n; n = it.nextNode()) {
      if (left <= n.data.length) return [n, left];
      left -= n.data.length;
    }
    return [box, box.childNodes.length];
  };
  Object.defineProperties(box, {
    value: {
      get: () => text(box),
      set(v) {
        box.textContent = v;
      },
    },
    disabled: {
      get: () => box.getAttribute("contenteditable") === "false",
      set(v) {
        box.setAttribute("contenteditable", v ? "false" : "true");
        box.setAttribute("aria-disabled", String(!!v));
      },
    },
    placeholder: {
      get: () => box.dataset.placeholder ?? "",
      set(v) {
        box.dataset.placeholder = v;
      },
    },
    selectionStart: {
      get: () => {
        const s = getSelection();
        return s.rangeCount ? offset(s.anchorNode, s.anchorOffset) : null;
      },
    },
    selectionEnd: {
      get: () => {
        const s = getSelection();
        return s.rangeCount ? offset(s.focusNode, s.focusOffset) : null;
      },
    },
  });
  box.setSelectionRange = (a, b) => {
    const r = document.createRange();
    r.setStart(...locate(a));
    r.setEnd(...locate(b));
    const s = getSelection();
    s.removeAllRanges();
    s.addRange(r);
  };
  box.setAttribute("contenteditable", "true");
  // Richly editable, but only ever text: pasted or dropped HTML is flattened.
  box.addEventListener("beforeinput", (e) => {
    if (e.inputType !== "insertFromPaste" && e.inputType !== "insertFromDrop") return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const t = e.dataTransfer?.getData("text/plain");
    if (t) document.execCommand("insertText", false, t);
  });
  box.setAttribute("role", "combobox");
  return box;
}
