// Rendering and metrics helpers for the file viewer. All state lives on the shared `viewer` context.
import {
  el as createElement,
  highlightInto,
  langFromPath,
  langLabel,
  renderMarkdown,
} from "/render.js";

export const metricThread = (request) =>
  request.scope === "repos" ? "viewer:repos" : request.thread;
export const scopeParams = (request) =>
  request.scope === "repos" ? { scope: "repos" } : { thread: request.thread };

export function paint() {
  return new Promise((resolve) => requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(resolve)));
}

/** True when a load/render finished after the viewer moved on or closed. */
export function isStale(viewer, token) {
  return token !== viewer.generation || !viewer.dialog.open;
}

export function revokeImage(viewer) {
  if (viewer.imageObjectUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(viewer.imageObjectUrl);
  viewer.imageObjectUrl = null;
}

// A closed dialog keeps its body, so playback must stop explicitly.
export function stopMedia(viewer) {
  for (const media of viewer.body.querySelectorAll("audio, video")) media.pause();
}

export function record(viewer, sample) {
  const safe = { kind: sample.kind, thread: sample.thread };
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(sample))
    if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value))
      safe[key] = Math.max(0, Math.round(value * 10) / 10);
  viewer.metrics.push(safe);
  if (viewer.metrics.length > 30) viewer.metrics.shift();
  viewer.report(safe);
  updatePerformance(viewer);
}

function sizeText(bytes) {
  if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;
  return bytes < 1048576 ? `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB` : `${(bytes / 1048576).toFixed(1)} MB`;
}

function agoText(timestamp) {
  const seconds = Math.max(0, (Date.now() - timestamp) / 1000);
  if (seconds < 60) return "just now";
  if (seconds < 3600) return `${Math.floor(seconds / 60)} min ago`;
  return seconds < 86400
    ? `${Math.floor(seconds / 3600)} h ago`
    : `${Math.floor(seconds / 86400)} d ago`;
}

function metaRows(meta, chain, file) {
  const rows = [];
  if (meta)
    rows.push(
      ["Modified", new Date(meta.mtime).toLocaleString()],
      ["Size", `${meta.size.toLocaleString()} bytes`],
      ["Mode", meta.mode],
      ["Owner uid", String(meta.uid)],
      ["Links", String(meta.nlink)],
    );
  if (chain.length)
    rows.push(
      ["Opened as", chain[0].path],
      ["Target", chain.at(-1).to],
      ["Hops", String(chain.length)],
      ["Real path", file.path],
    );
  return rows;
}

export function applyMeta(viewer, file) {
  const meta = file?.meta,
    chain = meta?.symlink || [];
  viewer.linkPill.hidden = !chain.length;
  viewer.outsidePill.hidden = !chain.some((hop) => !hop.inside);
  viewer.extra.textContent = meta ? `· ${sizeText(meta.size)} · ${agoText(meta.mtime)}` : "";
  const rows = metaRows(meta, chain, file);
  if (file?.path)
    viewer.path.textContent = chain.length ? `${chain[0].path} → ${chain.at(-1).to}` : file.path;
  viewer.infoList.replaceChildren(
    ...rows.flatMap(([label, value]) => [
      createElement("dt", "", label),
      createElement("dd", "", value),
    ]),
  );
}

function timingText(sample) {
  return `${sample.kind.replace("viewer_", "")} · ${Math.round(sample.total_ms || 0)} ms total · ${Math.round(sample.roundtrip_ms || 0)} ms network · ${Math.round(sample.server_ms || 0)} ms server · ${Math.round(sample.decode_ms || 0)} ms decode · ${Math.round(sample.module_ms || 0)} ms module · ${Math.round(sample.render_ms || 0)} ms drawing · ${Math.round(sample.bytes || 0).toLocaleString()} bytes`;
}

export function updatePerformance(viewer) {
  if (viewer.performanceDetails.hidden) return;
  viewer.timingList.replaceChildren(
    ...viewer.metrics.slice(-3).map((sample) => createElement("div", "", timingText(sample))),
  );
}

/** Split highlighted DOM at newlines, retaining nested highlight spans without reparsing untrusted HTML. */
function addTextLines(lines, node, ancestors) {
  const parts = node.textContent.split("\n");
  for (let index = 0; index < parts.length; index++) {
    if (index) lines.push(document.createDocumentFragment());
    if (!parts[index]) continue;
    let target = lines.at(-1);
    for (const source of ancestors) {
      const span = source.cloneNode(false);
      target.append(span);
      target = span;
    }
    target.append(document.createTextNode(parts[index]));
  }
}

function walkHighlight(code, lines, node, ancestors) {
  if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) {
    addTextLines(lines, node, ancestors);
    return;
  }
  for (const child of node.childNodes)
    walkHighlight(code, lines, child, node === code ? ancestors : [...ancestors, node]);
}

function highlightedLines(text, lang) {
  const code = document.createElement("code");
  highlightInto(code, text, lang);
  const lines = [document.createDocumentFragment()];
  walkHighlight(code, lines, code, []);
  return lines;
}

function positionText(ref, selected, last) {
  if (!ref.line) return "";
  return `Line ${selected}${last > selected ? `–${last}` : ""}${ref.column ? `, column ${ref.column}` : ""}`;
}

function statusText(count, plain, lang) {
  return `${count.toLocaleString()} ${count === 1 ? "line" : "lines"} · ${plain ? "Plain text" : langLabel(lang)}`;
}

function setHeading(viewer, file) {
  viewer.title.textContent = file.name || file.path.split("/").at(-1);
  viewer.path.textContent = file.path;
  viewer.path.title = file.path;
}

function syncSourceButton(viewer, markdown) {
  const { source } = viewer;
  source.hidden = !markdown;
  source.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(viewer.markdownSource));
  source.title = viewer.markdownSource ? "Show rendered Markdown" : "Show Markdown source";
}

function renderMarkdownBody(viewer, count) {
  const prose = createElement("div", "prose file-markdown");
  renderMarkdown(viewer.content, prose);
  viewer.body.classList.add("is-markdown");
  viewer.body.append(prose);
  viewer.status.textContent = `${count.toLocaleString()} lines · Markdown`;
  viewer.position.textContent = "";
  viewer.copy.disabled = false;
  viewer.wrap.disabled = true;
}

function renderPlain(viewer, lines, ref, range, token) {
  const { body, content } = viewer;
  const { selected, last } = range;
  const gutter = createElement(
    "pre",
    "file-plain-gutter",
    lines.map((_line, index) => index + 1).join("\n"),
  );
  gutter.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  const pre = createElement("pre", "file-plain-text");
  const code = document.createElement("code");
  code.textContent = content || "\n";
  pre.append(code);
  body.append(gutter, pre);
  body.classList.add("is-plain");
  if (ref.line) {
    const marker = createElement("div", "file-plain-selection");
    marker.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
    marker.style.top = `calc(var(--file-line-height) * ${selected - 1})`;
    marker.style.height = `calc(var(--file-line-height) * ${last - selected + 1})`;
    body.append(marker);
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    if (viewer.dialog.open && token === viewer.generation)
      body.scrollTop = Math.max(0, (selected - 5) * parseFloat(getComputedStyle(pre).lineHeight));
  });
}

function lineRow(text, index, ref, range) {
  const row = createElement("div", "file-line");
  row.dataset.line = String(index + 1);
  if (ref.line && index + 1 >= range.selected && index + 1 <= range.last)
    row.classList.add("is-selected");
  const numberCell = createElement("span", "file-line-number", index + 1);
  numberCell.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  const code = createElement("span", "file-line-text hljs");
  code.append(text);
  if (!code.hasChildNodes()) code.append(document.createTextNode("\u200b"));
  row.append(numberCell, code);
  return row;
}

function scrollToSelection(viewer, ref, selected, token) {
  const { body } = viewer;
  if (!viewer.dialog.open || token !== viewer.generation) return;
  const row = body.querySelector(`[data-line="${selected}"]`);
  if (row && ref.line) body.scrollTop = Math.max(0, row.offsetTop - body.clientHeight / 3);
  // Point to the requested column while preserving the original text for copying.
  if (ref.column && row)
    body.scrollLeft = Math.max(
      0,
      (ref.column - 8) * parseFloat(getComputedStyle(row).fontSize) * 0.6,
    );
}

function renderCode(viewer, lang, ref, range, token) {
  const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
  const highlighted = highlightedLines(viewer.content, lang);
  for (let index = 0; index < highlighted.length; index++)
    fragment.append(lineRow(highlighted[index], index, ref, range));
  viewer.body.append(fragment);
  requestAnimationFrame(() => scrollToSelection(viewer, ref, range.selected, token));
}

export function renderFile(viewer, file, ref, token) {
  const { body } = viewer;
  body.replaceChildren();
  body.classList.remove("is-plain", "is-image", "is-markdown");
  viewer.content = file.content;
  setHeading(viewer, file);
  const lines = viewer.content.split("\n");
  const count = lines.length;
  body.style.setProperty("--gutter-ch", String(Math.max(2, String(count).length)));
  const lang = langFromPath(file.path, viewer.content);
  // Bound highlighting work and element count; very large files stay available as plain text.
  const plain = viewer.content.length > 128 * 1024 || count > 2000;
  const markdown = lang === "markdown" && !plain;
  syncSourceButton(viewer, markdown);
  if (markdown && !viewer.markdownSource) {
    renderMarkdownBody(viewer, count);
    return;
  }
  viewer.status.textContent = statusText(count, plain, lang);
  const selected = Math.min(ref.line || 1, count);
  const range = { selected, last: Math.min(ref.endLine || selected, count) };
  viewer.position.textContent = positionText(ref, selected, range.last);
  if (plain) renderPlain(viewer, lines, ref, range, token);
  else renderCode(viewer, lang, ref, range, token);
  viewer.copy.disabled = false;
  viewer.wrap.disabled = plain;
}

function checkedUrl(url, allowed, message) {
  const parsed = new URL(url, location.origin);
  if (parsed.origin !== location.origin || !allowed.test(parsed.pathname + parsed.search))
    throw new Error(message);
  return parsed.pathname + parsed.search;
}

export async function renderImage(viewer, url, name, token, signal) {
  const target = checkedUrl(url, /^\/(?:uploads\/|api\/file-image\?)/, "Invalid image URL.");
  const startedAt = performance.now();
  const response = await viewer.apiFetch(target, { signal });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Could not open this image.");
  const blob = await response.blob(),
    receivedAt = performance.now();
  if (!/^image\/(png|jpeg|gif|webp)$/.test(blob.type) || blob.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024)
    throw new Error("This image format cannot be previewed.");
  if (isStale(viewer, token)) return {};
  viewer.imageObjectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const image = createElement("img", "file-viewer-image");
  image.alt = name;
  image.src = viewer.imageObjectUrl;
  const decodeAt = performance.now();
  await image.decode();
  if (isStale(viewer, token)) return {};
  const readyAt = performance.now();
  viewer.body.classList.add("is-image");
  viewer.body.append(image);
  viewer.status.textContent = `${image.naturalWidth.toLocaleString()} × ${image.naturalHeight.toLocaleString()} · Image`;
  return {
    bytes: blob.size,
    imageNetwork: receivedAt - startedAt,
    imageDecode: readyAt - decodeAt,
    readyAt,
    receivedAt,
  };
}

function mediaReady(media, signal, failure) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    media.addEventListener("loadedmetadata", resolve, { once: true });
    media.addEventListener("error", () => reject(new Error(failure)), { once: true });
    signal.addEventListener("abort", () => reject(new DOMException("Aborted", "AbortError")), {
      once: true,
    });
  });
}

function mediaStatus(media, video) {
  const length = Number.isFinite(media.duration) ? media.duration : 0;
  const clock = `${Math.floor(length / 60)}:${String(Math.floor(length % 60)).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  if (video && media.videoWidth)
    return `${media.videoWidth} × ${media.videoHeight} · ${clock} · Video`;
  return `${clock} · ${video ? "Video" : "Audio"}`;
}

export async function renderMedia(viewer, file, token, signal) {
  const target = checkedUrl(file.url, /^\/api\/file-media\?/, "Invalid media URL.");
  const video = /^video\//.test(file.media_type);
  const media = createElement(video ? "video" : "audio", "file-viewer-media");
  media.controls = true;
  media.preload = "metadata";
  media.playsInline = true;
  media.setAttribute("aria-label", file.name);
  const startedAt = performance.now();
  const ready = mediaReady(
    media,
    signal,
    video ? "This video cannot be played here." : "This audio cannot be played here.",
  );
  media.src = target;
  viewer.body.classList.add("is-image");
  viewer.body.append(media);
  await ready;
  if (isStale(viewer, token)) return {};
  const readyAt = performance.now();
  viewer.status.textContent = mediaStatus(media, video);
  return { bytes: file.bytes, imageNetwork: readyAt - startedAt, readyAt, receivedAt: readyAt };
}

================
File: public/file-viewer-tree.js

// Project file tree for the file viewer. All state lives on the shared `viewer` context.

import { metricThread, paint, record, scopeParams } from "/file-viewer-render.js";
import { el as createElement } from "/render.js";

export function showTree(viewer) {
  viewer.sidebar.hidden = !viewer.treeShown;
  viewer.files.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(viewer.treeShown));
  viewer.files.setAttribute("aria-label", viewer.treeShown ? "Hide files" : "Show files");
}

export function markSelected(viewer) {
  for (const node of viewer.tree.querySelectorAll("button[data-path]")) {
    const selected = node.dataset.path === viewer.current?.ref.path;
    node.classList.toggle("is-selected", selected);
    if (selected) node.setAttribute("aria-current", "true");
    else node.removeAttribute("aria-current");
  }
}

export function abortTreeRequests(viewer) {
  for (const request of viewer.treeControllers) request.abort();
  viewer.treeControllers.clear();
}

/** Drop every cached folder and invalidate in-flight tree loads. */
export function resetTree(viewer) {
  abortTreeRequests(viewer);
  viewer.treeGeneration++;
  viewer.treeThread = null;
  viewer.treeRoot = null;
  viewer.treeEntries = 0;
  viewer.directories.clear();
  viewer.tree.replaceChildren();
}

export function ensureTree(viewer) {
  if (!viewer.treeShown || !viewer.dialog.open) return;
  if (viewer.treeThread !== metricThread(viewer.current) || !viewer.tree.hasChildNodes()) {
    resetTree(viewer);
    viewer.treeThread = metricThread(viewer.current);
    directory(viewer, viewer.tree, ".");
  } else markSelected(viewer);
}

function entryOrder(left, right) {
  return (
    (left.kind === "directory" ? 0 : 1) - (right.kind === "directory" ? 0 : 1) ||
    left.name.localeCompare(right.name)
  );
}

function entryButton(entry, depth) {
  const button = createElement("button", "file-tree-entry");
  button.type = "button";
  button.dataset.path = entry.path;
  button.title = entry.name;
  button.style.paddingLeft = `${12 + depth * 14}px`;
  if (entry.link) button.title = `${entry.name} → ${entry.link}`;
  button.append(
    createElement(
      "span",
      "file-tree-symbol",
      entry.kind === "directory" ? "▸" : entry.link ? "↪" : "·",
    ),
    createElement("span", "file-tree-name", entry.name),
  );
  if (entry.link) button.append(createElement("span", "file-tree-link", `→ ${entry.link}`));
  return button;
}

function wireFolder(viewer, item, button, entry, depth) {
  button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  const children = createElement("div", "file-tree-children");
  children.hidden = true;
  item.append(children);
  button.onclick = () => {
    children.hidden = !children.hidden;
    button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(!children.hidden));
    button.firstChild.textContent = children.hidden ? "▸" : "▾";
    if (!children.hidden && !children.hasChildNodes())
      directory(viewer, children, entry.path, "", depth + 1);
  };
  if (viewer.current.ref.path.startsWith(entry.path + "/")) button.click();
}

function wireFile(viewer, button, entry) {
  button.onclick = () => {
    viewer.current = {
      ...viewer.current,
      ref: { path: entry.path },
      openedAt: undefined,
      module_ms: 0,
    };
    viewer.load(viewer.current);
    if (matchMedia("(max-width:640px)").matches) {
      viewer.treeShown = false;
      showTree(viewer);
    }
  };
}

function entryItem(viewer, entry, depth) {
  const item = createElement("div", "file-tree-item");
  const button = entryButton(entry, depth);
  item.append(button);
  if (entry.kind === "directory") wireFolder(viewer, item, button, entry, depth);
  else wireFile(viewer, button, entry);
  return item;
}

function entriesFragment(viewer, result, depth) {
  const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
  const remaining = Math.max(0, 2400 - viewer.treeEntries);
  const visibleEntries = result.entries.slice(0, remaining);
  viewer.treeEntries += visibleEntries.length;
  for (const entry of visibleEntries.sort(entryOrder))
    fragment.append(entryItem(viewer, entry, depth));
  return fragment;
}

function appendFooter(viewer, ctx, result) {
  const { group } = ctx;
  if (viewer.treeEntries >= 2400 || result.limit_reached) {
    group.append(
      createElement(
        "p",
        "file-tree-note",
        "File list limit reached. Close and reopen the viewer to browse another folder.",
      ),
    );
  } else if (result.cursor) {
    const more = createElement("button", "file-tree-more", "Load more");
    more.type = "button";
    more.onclick = () => {
      more.remove();
      directory(viewer, ctx.parent, ctx.directoryPath, result.cursor, ctx.depth);
    };
    group.append(more);
  }
}

async function loadDirectory(viewer, ctx, controller) {
  const { group, directoryPath, token, startedAt } = ctx;
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ ...scopeParams(viewer.current), path: directoryPath });
  if (ctx.cursor) params.set("cursor", ctx.cursor);
  const response = await viewer.apiFetch(`/api/files?${params}`, {
    signal: controller.signal,
    cache: "no-store",
  });
  const raw = await response.text(),
    receivedAt = performance.now(),
    result = JSON.parse(raw),
    decodedAt = performance.now();
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || "Could not load files.");
  if (token !== viewer.treeGeneration || !viewer.dialog.open) return;
  viewer.treeRoot ||= result.root;
  if (directoryPath === ".") {
    viewer.treeTitle.textContent = result.root.split("/").at(-1) || "Project files";
    viewer.treeTitle.title = result.root;
  }
  group.append(entriesFragment(viewer, result, ctx.depth));
  ctx.loading.remove();
  appendFooter(viewer, ctx, result);
  markSelected(viewer);
  await paint();
  if (token === viewer.treeGeneration)
    record(viewer, {
      kind: "viewer_tree",
      thread: metricThread(viewer.current),
      total_ms: performance.now() - startedAt,
      wait_ms: receivedAt - startedAt,
      roundtrip_ms: receivedAt - startedAt,
      server_ms: result.read_ms || 0,
      decode_ms: decodedAt - receivedAt,
      render_ms: performance.now() - decodedAt,
      bytes: result.bytes || 0,
      events: result.entries.length,
    });
}

function showDirectoryError(viewer, ctx, failure) {
  ctx.loading.remove();
  if (failure.name === "AbortError" || ctx.token !== viewer.treeGeneration) return;
  const retry = createElement("button", "file-tree-more", "Retry files");
  retry.type = "button";
  retry.title = failure.message;
  retry.onclick = () => {
    retry.remove();
    viewer.directories.delete(ctx.directoryPath);
    ctx.group.remove();
    directory(viewer, ctx.parent, ctx.directoryPath, ctx.cursor, ctx.depth);
  };
  ctx.group.append(retry);
}

/** Reuse a cached folder group, or return null when the folder was attached from cache or capped. */
function claimGroup(viewer, parent, directoryPath, cursor) {
  const existing = viewer.directories.get(directoryPath);
  if (!cursor && existing) {
    parent.append(existing);
    return null;
  }
  if (!cursor && viewer.directories.size >= 24) {
    parent.append(
      createElement("p", "file-tree-note", "Close and reopen the viewer to browse more folders."),
    );
    return null;
  }
  const group = existing || createElement("div", "file-tree-group");
  if (!existing) {
    viewer.directories.set(directoryPath, group);
    parent.append(group);
  }
  return group;
}

export async function directory(viewer, parent, directoryPath, cursor = "", depth = 0) {
  if (!viewer.dialog.open || !viewer.treeShown || depth > 32) return;
  const group = claimGroup(viewer, parent, directoryPath, cursor);
  if (!group) return;
  const controller = new AbortController();
  viewer.treeControllers.add(controller);
  const loading = createElement("span", "file-tree-note", "Loading…");
  group.append(loading);
  const ctx = {
    parent,
    directoryPath,
    cursor,
    depth,
    group,
    loading,
    token: viewer.treeGeneration,
    startedAt: performance.now(),
  };
  try {
    await loadDirectory(viewer, ctx, controller);
  } catch (failure) {
    showDirectoryError(viewer, ctx, failure);
  } finally {
    viewer.treeControllers.delete(controller);
  }
}

// Loaded after the conversation is painted and the browser is idle, or when a file link is first opened.

import { handleEditClick, prepareEdit, resetEdit } from "/file-viewer-edit.js";
import {
  applyMeta,
  isStale,
  metricThread,
  paint,
  record,
  renderFile,
  renderImage,
  renderMedia,
  revokeImage,
  scopeParams,
  stopMedia,
  updatePerformance,
} from "/file-viewer-render.js";
import { abortTreeRequests, ensureTree, resetTree, showTree } from "/file-viewer-tree.js";
import { el as createElement, icon, iconButton, parseFileRef, wireCopy } from "/render.js";

let style;
export function prepare() {
  return (style ||= new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const link = document.createElement("link");
    link.rel = "stylesheet";
    link.href = "/file-viewer.css";
    link.onload = resolve;
    link.onerror = () => {
      link.remove();
      style = null;
      reject(new Error("Could not load the file viewer. Try again."));
    };
    document.head.append(link);
  }));
}

function buildDom() {
  const dialog = createElement("dialog", "file-viewer");
  dialog.id = "file-viewer";
  dialog.setAttribute("aria-labelledby", "file-viewer-title");
  const header = createElement("header", "file-viewer-head");
  const heading = createElement("div", "file-viewer-heading");
  const title = createElement("h2");
  title.id = "file-viewer-title";
  const path = createElement("p", "file-viewer-path");
  path.id = "file-viewer-path";
  const copyPath = iconButton("copy", "Copy path");
  const pathRow = createElement("div", "file-viewer-path-row");
  pathRow.style.cssText = "display:flex;align-items:center;gap:var(--sp-2);";
  pathRow.append(path, copyPath);
  const meta = createElement("div", "file-viewer-meta");
  const status = createElement("span");
  status.id = "file-viewer-status";
  status.setAttribute("role", "status");
  meta.append(status);
  heading.append(title, pathRow, meta);
  const close = iconButton("x", "Close file viewer");
  close.autofocus = true;
  header.append(heading, close);
  const toolbar = createElement("div", "file-viewer-toolbar");
  const wrap = createElement("button", "btn sm ghost");
  wrap.type = "button";
  wrap.setAttribute("aria-label", "Wrap lines");
  wrap.setAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
  wrap.append(icon("wrap"), createElement("span", "", "Wrap"));
  const copy = createElement("button", "btn sm ghost");
  copy.type = "button";
  copy.setAttribute("aria-label", "Copy file");
  copy.title = "Copy file";
  copy.append(icon("copy"), createElement("span", "lab", "Copy file"));
  const position = createElement("span", "file-viewer-position");
  const source = createElement("button", "btn sm ghost", "Source");
  source.type = "button";
  source.hidden = true;
  source.setAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
  source.title = "Show Markdown source";
  const files = createElement("button", "btn sm ghost", "Files");
  files.type = "button";
  files.setAttribute("aria-label", "Show files");
  files.setAttribute("aria-controls", "file-viewer-tree");
  const timings = createElement("button", "btn sm ghost", "Info");
  timings.type = "button";
  timings.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  timings.setAttribute("aria-controls", "file-viewer-performance");
  const edit = createElement("a", "btn sm ghost", "Edit");
  edit.id = "file-viewer-edit";
  edit.hidden = true;
  edit.title = "Edit in Acode";
  edit.setAttribute("aria-label", "Edit");
  toolbar.append(files, edit, source, wrap, copy, timings, position);
  const performanceDetails = createElement("div", "file-viewer-performance");
  performanceDetails.id = "file-viewer-performance";
  performanceDetails.hidden = true;
  const infoList = createElement("dl", "file-viewer-info"),
    timingList = createElement("div", "file-viewer-timings");
  performanceDetails.append(infoList, timingList);
  const setup = createElement("a", "", "Set up Acode editing");
  setup.href = "/acode-setup";
  setup.target = "_blank";
  setup.rel = "noopener";
  performanceDetails.append(setup);
  const linkPill = createElement("span", "file-viewer-readonly", "Symlink");
  linkPill.hidden = true;
  const outsidePill = createElement(
    "span",
    "file-viewer-readonly file-viewer-warn",
    "Outside repo",
  );
  outsidePill.hidden = true;
  const extra = createElement("span");
  meta.append(linkPill, outsidePill, extra);
  const workspace = createElement("div", "file-viewer-workspace");
  const sidebar = createElement("nav", "file-viewer-tree");
  sidebar.id = "file-viewer-tree";
  sidebar.setAttribute("aria-label", "Project files");
  const treeTitle = createElement("div", "file-tree-title", "Project files");
  const tree = createElement("div", "file-tree-list");
  sidebar.append(treeTitle, tree);
  const main = createElement("div", "file-viewer-main");
  const body = createElement("div", "file-viewer-code");
  body.id = "file-viewer-code";
  body.tabIndex = 0;
  body.setAttribute("role", "region");
  body.setAttribute("aria-label", "File contents");
  const error = createElement("div", "file-viewer-error");
  error.hidden = true;
  const retry = createElement("button", "btn", "Retry");
  retry.type = "button";
  error.append(retry);
  main.append(error, body);
  workspace.append(sidebar, main);
  dialog.append(header, toolbar, performanceDetails, workspace);
  return {
    dialog,
    title,
    path,
    copyPath,
    close,
    wrap,
    copy,
    position,
    source,
    files,
    timings,
    edit,
    status,
    infoList,
    timingList,
    performanceDetails,
    linkPill,
    outsidePill,
    extra,
    sidebar,
    treeTitle,
    tree,
    body,
    error,
    retry,
  };
}

function newViewer(apiFetch, report) {
  return {
    ...buildDom(),
    apiFetch,
    report,
    controller: undefined,
    current: undefined,
    content: "",
    restoreFocus: undefined,
    generation: 0,
    imageObjectUrl: undefined,
    currentTextFile: undefined,
    markdownSource: false,
    editTicket: undefined,
    editTimer: undefined,
    editPending: undefined,
    treeGeneration: 0,
    treeThread: undefined,
    treeRoot: undefined,
    treeEntries: 0,
    treeShown: !matchMedia("(max-width:640px)").matches,
    directories: new Map(),
    treeControllers: new Set(),
    metrics: (window.agentConsoleViewerMetrics ||= []),
    load: undefined,
  };
}

async function toggleSource(viewer) {
  if (!viewer.currentTextFile) return;
  const token = viewer.generation,
    startedAt = performance.now();
  viewer.markdownSource = !viewer.markdownSource;
  viewer.body.scrollTop = 0;
  viewer.body.scrollLeft = 0;
  renderFile(viewer, viewer.currentTextFile.file, viewer.currentTextFile.ref, token);
  await paint();
  if (!isStale(viewer, token))
    record(viewer, {
      kind: "viewer_open",
      thread: metricThread(viewer.current),
      total_ms: performance.now() - startedAt,
      render_ms: performance.now() - startedAt,
      bytes: viewer.currentTextFile.file.bytes || 0,
      events: 1,
    });
}

function openMarkdownLink(viewer, event) {
  if (event.type === "auxclick" && event.button !== 1) return;
  if (!viewer.body.classList.contains("is-markdown")) return;
  const link = event.target.closest?.("a");
  const ref = link && parseFileRef(link.dataset.fileRef || link.getAttribute("href"));
  if (!ref) return;
  event.preventDefault();
  if (!ref.path.startsWith("/") && !ref.path.startsWith("~/"))
    ref.path = decodeURIComponent(
      new URL(ref.path, new URL(viewer.currentTextFile.file.path, location.origin)).pathname,
    );
  viewer.current = { ...viewer.current, ref, openedAt: undefined, module_ms: 0 };
  viewer.load(viewer.current);
}

function setWrapped(viewer, on) {
  viewer.wrap.setAttribute(
    "aria-pressed",
    String(viewer.dialog.classList.toggle("is-wrapped", on)),
  );
  try {
    localStorage.setItem("file-viewer-wrap", on ? "1" : "0");
  } catch {}
}

function trapTab(viewer, event) {
  if (event.key !== "Tab") return;
  const controls = [
    ...viewer.dialog.querySelectorAll("button:not(:disabled), a[href], [tabindex='0']"),
  ].filter((node) => node.getClientRects().length);
  const first = controls[0],
    last = controls.at(-1);
  if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
    event.preventDefault();
    last?.focus();
  } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
    event.preventDefault();
    first?.focus();
  }
}

function closeOnBackdrop(viewer, event) {
  const { dialog } = viewer;
  if (event.target !== dialog) return;
  const rect = dialog.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (
    event.clientX < rect.left ||
    event.clientX > rect.right ||
    event.clientY < rect.top ||
    event.clientY > rect.bottom
  )
    dialog.close();
}

function onDialogClose(viewer) {
  resetEdit(viewer);
  viewer.controller?.abort();
  viewer.generation++;
  viewer.treeGeneration++;
  abortTreeRequests(viewer);
  revokeImage(viewer);
  stopMedia(viewer);
  if (viewer.restoreFocus?.isConnected) viewer.restoreFocus.focus({ preventScroll: true });
}

function reloadCurrent(viewer, openedAt) {
  viewer.current = { ...viewer.current, openedAt, module_ms: 0 };
  viewer.load(viewer.current);
}

/** Clear the body and header for a new request, returning the generation token and start time. */
function resetForLoad(viewer, request) {
  const { body, path } = viewer;
  resetEdit(viewer);
  viewer.controller?.abort();
  viewer.controller = new AbortController();
  const token = ++viewer.generation,
    startedAt = request.openedAt || performance.now();
  revokeImage(viewer);
  stopMedia(viewer);
  viewer.content = "";
  viewer.currentTextFile = null;
  viewer.source.hidden = true;
  viewer.copy.disabled = true;
  viewer.wrap.disabled = true;
  viewer.title.textContent = request.ref.path.split("/").at(-1) || request.ref.path;
  path.textContent = request.ref.imageUrl ? "Attached image" : request.ref.path;
  path.title = path.textContent;
  viewer.status.textContent = "Loading…";
  viewer.position.textContent = "";
  applyMeta(viewer, null);
  viewer.error.hidden = true;
  body.hidden = false;
  body.replaceChildren();
  body.classList.remove("is-plain", "is-image", "is-markdown");
  body.scrollTop = 0;
  body.scrollLeft = 0;
  viewer.dialog.setAttribute("aria-busy", "true");
  return { token, startedAt };
}

/** Fold a rendered image/media result into the timing stats. */
function absorbImageStats(stats, imageStats) {
  stats.imageStats = imageStats;
  stats.receivedAt = imageStats.receivedAt || stats.receivedAt;
  stats.decodedAt = imageStats.readyAt || performance.now();
  stats.roundtrip_ms += imageStats.imageNetwork || 0;
  stats.decode_ms += imageStats.imageDecode || 0;
}

async function loadAttachedImage(viewer, request, token, stats) {
  const imageStats = await renderImage(
    viewer,
    request.ref.imageUrl,
    viewer.title.textContent,
    token,
    stats.signal,
  );
  stats.imageStats = imageStats;
  stats.receivedAt = imageStats.receivedAt || stats.requestAt;
  stats.decodedAt = imageStats.readyAt || performance.now();
  stats.roundtrip_ms = imageStats.imageNetwork || 0;
  stats.decode_ms = imageStats.imageDecode || 0;
}

async function renderByKind(viewer, request, token, stats) {
  const { file, signal } = stats;
  if (file.kind === "image") {
    const imageStats = await renderImage(viewer, file.url, file.name, token, signal);
    applyMeta(viewer, file);
    absorbImageStats(stats, imageStats);
  } else if (file.kind === "media") {
    const imageStats = await renderMedia(viewer, file, token, signal);
    applyMeta(viewer, file);
    absorbImageStats(stats, imageStats);
  } else {
    if (typeof file.content !== "string") throw new Error("The server returned an invalid file.");
    viewer.currentTextFile = { file, ref: request.ref };
    viewer.markdownSource = !!request.ref.line;
    renderFile(viewer, file, request.ref, token);
    applyMeta(viewer, file);
    viewer.edit.hidden = false;
    prepareEdit(viewer, token).catch(() => {});
  }
}

/** Fetch and show a file; returns false when the viewer moved on before it rendered. */
async function loadFileRef(viewer, request, token, stats) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ ...scopeParams(request), path: request.ref.path });
  const response = await viewer.apiFetch(`/api/file?${params}`, {
    signal: stats.signal,
    cache: "no-store",
  });
  const raw = await response.text();
  stats.receivedAt = performance.now();
  const file = JSON.parse(raw);
  stats.file = file;
  stats.decodedAt = performance.now();
  stats.roundtrip_ms = stats.receivedAt - stats.requestAt;
  stats.decode_ms = stats.decodedAt - stats.receivedAt;
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(file.error || "Could not open this file.");
  if (isStale(viewer, token)) return false;
  if (typeof file.path !== "string") throw new Error("The server returned an invalid file.");
  viewer.current.ref.path = file.path;
  await renderByKind(viewer, request, token, stats);
  return true;
}

async function showLoadError(viewer, request, token, startedAt, failure) {
  if (token !== viewer.generation || failure.name === "AbortError" || !viewer.dialog.open) return;
  viewer.status.textContent = failure.message || "Could not open this file.";
  viewer.body.hidden = true;
  viewer.error.hidden = false;
  record(viewer, {
    kind: "viewer_open",
    thread: metricThread(request),
    total_ms: performance.now() - startedAt,
    events: 0,
  });
  await paint();
  if (!isStale(viewer, token)) ensureTree(viewer);
}

function recordOpen(viewer, request, startedAt, stats) {
  const { file } = stats;
  record(viewer, {
    kind: "viewer_open",
    thread: metricThread(request),
    total_ms: performance.now() - startedAt,
    module_ms: request.module_ms || 0,
    wait_ms: stats.receivedAt - startedAt,
    roundtrip_ms: stats.roundtrip_ms,
    server_ms: file?.read_ms || 0,
    decode_ms: stats.decode_ms,
    render_ms: performance.now() - stats.decodedAt,
    bytes: stats.imageStats.bytes || file?.bytes || 0,
    events: 1,
  });
}

/** Load and render the requested file; returns false when the viewer moved on early. */
async function fetchContent(viewer, request, token, stats) {
  if (!request.ref.imageUrl) return loadFileRef(viewer, request, token, stats);
  await loadAttachedImage(viewer, request, token, stats);
  return true;
}

async function load(viewer, request) {
  const { token, startedAt } = resetForLoad(viewer, request);
  try {
    const now = performance.now();
    const stats = {
      file: undefined,
      receivedAt: now,
      decodedAt: now,
      decode_ms: 0,
      roundtrip_ms: 0,
      imageStats: {},
      signal: viewer.controller.signal,
      requestAt: performance.now(),
    };
    if (!(await fetchContent(viewer, request, token, stats))) return;
    if (isStale(viewer, token)) return;
    await paint();
    if (isStale(viewer, token)) return;
    recordOpen(viewer, request, startedAt, stats);
    ensureTree(viewer);
  } catch (failure) {
    await showLoadError(viewer, request, token, startedAt, failure);
  } finally {
    if (token === viewer.generation) viewer.dialog.setAttribute("aria-busy", "false");
  }
}

function openWorkspace(viewer, { opener, openedAt, module_ms }) {
  const { body, path } = viewer;
  resetEdit(viewer);
  viewer.controller?.abort();
  viewer.generation++;
  resetTree(viewer);
  viewer.treeTitle.textContent = "repos";
  viewer.treeTitle.title = "~/repos";
  revokeImage(viewer);
  viewer.current = { scope: "repos", ref: { path: "" } };
  viewer.content = "";
  viewer.currentTextFile = null;
  viewer.markdownSource = false;
  viewer.title.textContent = "Files";
  path.textContent = "~/repos";
  path.title = "~/repos";
  viewer.status.textContent = "";
  viewer.position.textContent = "";
  viewer.source.hidden = true;
  viewer.copy.disabled = true;
  viewer.wrap.disabled = true;
  viewer.error.hidden = true;
  body.hidden = false;
  body.classList.remove("is-plain", "is-image", "is-markdown");
  body.replaceChildren(createElement("p", "file-tree-note", "Select a file to preview."));
  body.scrollTop = 0;
  body.scrollLeft = 0;
  viewer.performanceDetails.hidden = true;
  viewer.timings.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  viewer.dialog.setAttribute("aria-busy", "false");
  viewer.restoreFocus = opener || document.activeElement;
  if (!viewer.dialog.open) viewer.dialog.showModal();
  viewer.treeShown = true;
  showTree(viewer);
  ensureTree(viewer);
  record(viewer, {
    kind: "viewer_open",
    thread: "viewer:repos",
    total_ms: performance.now() - openedAt,
    module_ms,
    events: 0,
  });
}

function openFile(viewer, { thread, ref, opener, openedAt, module_ms }) {
  const wasOpen = viewer.dialog.open;
  viewer.current = { thread, ref, openedAt, module_ms };
  if (!wasOpen) {
    viewer.restoreFocus = opener || document.activeElement;
    viewer.dialog.showModal();
    viewer.tree.replaceChildren();
    viewer.directories.clear();
  }
  load(viewer, viewer.current);
}

export function createFileViewer(apiFetch, report = () => {}) {
  const viewer = newViewer(apiFetch, report);
  const { dialog, body } = viewer;
  document.body.append(dialog);
  viewer.load = (request) => load(viewer, request);
  viewer.edit.onclick = (event) => handleEditClick(viewer, event);
  document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", () => {
    if (document.visibilityState !== "visible" || !dialog.open || !viewer.currentTextFile) return;
    // Refresh content and access after returning from the native editor.
    viewer.load({ ...viewer.current, openedAt: performance.now(), module_ms: 0 });
  });
  viewer.timings.onclick = () => {
    const details = viewer.performanceDetails;
    details.hidden = !details.hidden;
    viewer.timings.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(!details.hidden));
    updatePerformance(viewer);
  };
  showTree(viewer);
  viewer.files.onclick = () => {
    viewer.treeShown = !viewer.treeShown;
    showTree(viewer);
    if (viewer.treeShown) ensureTree(viewer);
  };
  wireCopy(viewer.copy, () => viewer.content, "Copy file");
  wireCopy(viewer.copyPath, () => viewer.path.textContent, "Copy path");
  viewer.source.onclick = () => toggleSource(viewer);
  body.addEventListener("click", (event) => openMarkdownLink(viewer, event));
  body.addEventListener("auxclick", (event) => openMarkdownLink(viewer, event));
  viewer.wrap.onclick = () => setWrapped(viewer, !dialog.classList.contains("is-wrapped"));
  try {
    if (localStorage.getItem("file-viewer-wrap") === "1") setWrapped(viewer, true);
  } catch {}
  viewer.close.onclick = () => dialog.close();
  dialog.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => trapTab(viewer, event));
  dialog.addEventListener("click", (event) => closeOnBackdrop(viewer, event));
  dialog.addEventListener("close", () => onDialogClose(viewer));
  viewer.retry.onclick = () => {
    if (viewer.current) reloadCurrent(viewer, performance.now());
  };
  return {
    record: (sample) => record(viewer, sample),
    openWorkspace: (args) => openWorkspace(viewer, args),
    open: (args) => openFile(viewer, args),
  };
}

================
File: public/frontmatter.js
================
/** Split YAML frontmatter (--- ... ---) from markdown source. Returns { meta, body }; meta is the raw block or null. */
export function splitFrontmatter(src) {
  const lines = src.split(/\r?\n/);
  if (!/^---\s*$/.test(lines[0])) return { meta: null, body: src };
  let end = -1;
  for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
    if (/^(---|\.\.\.)\s*$/.test(lines[i])) {
      end = i;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (end === -1) return { meta: null, body: src };
  const meta = lines.slice(1, end).join("\n");
  // Only treat as frontmatter when the block looks like key: value lines or is empty.
  if (meta && !/^[^\S\r\n]*[a-zA-Z_]+\s*:/m.test(meta)) return { meta: null, body: src };
  return { meta, body: lines.slice(end + 1).join("\n") };
}

const cell = (s) => s.replace(/\|/g, "\\|");
const unquote = (s) => s.trim().replace(/^(["'])(.*)\1$/, "$2");

/** Markdown source with its frontmatter shown as a Field/Value table above the body (GitHub style). */
export function frontmatterToMarkdown(src) {
  if (!src) return src;
  const { meta, body } = splitFrontmatter(src);
  if (!meta) return body;
  const rows = [];
  for (const line of meta.split("\n")) {
    const m = /^([a-zA-Z_][\w-]*)\s*:\s*(.*)$/.exec(line);
    if (m) rows.push([m[1], unquote(m[2])]);
    else if (rows.length && line.trim()) {
      const item = unquote(line.trim().replace(/^-\s+/, ""));
      const last = rows[rows.length - 1];
      last[1] = last[1] ? `${last[1]}, ${item}` : item;
    }
  }
  if (!rows.length) return body;
  const table = [
    "| Field | Value |",
    "| --- | --- |",
    ...rows.map(([k, v]) => `| ${cell(k)} | ${cell(v)} |`),
  ];
  return `${table.join("\n")}\n\n${body}`;
}

  <symbol id="i-wrap" viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path d="M2.5 4h11M2.5 12h4M2.5 8h8.5a2 2 0 0 1 0 4H9.5m1.25-1.5L9.25 12l1.5 1.5"/></symbol>

// Accessible popover menu button for value pickers (listbox) and actions (menu).
//   const m = createMenu({ trigger, items:[{value,label,desc?,group?,icon?,tone?,disabled?,keep?,checked?,children?}], value, onSelect, onOpen?, onClose?, kind:"listbox"|"menu", side:"top"|"bottom", align:"start"|"end" })
// Keyboard: trigger opens with Enter / Space / ArrowDown / ArrowUp. In the popover: ArrowUp/Down, Home/End, typeahead, Enter/Space select,
// Right opens a submenu; Left, Back, or Esc returns to its parent. Esc at the root returns focus to the trigger; Tab closes. Click or tap outside closes. Items are >=44px on coarse pointers (see .menu in style.css).
// Local DOM helpers keep this module independent of render.js and its markdown/highlight vendors.
const SVG = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
const el = (tag, cls, text) => {
  const node = document.createElement(tag);
  if (cls) node.className = cls;
  if (text != null) node.textContent = text;
  return node;
};
const icon = (name, cls = "") => {
  const svg = document.createElementNS(SVG, "svg");
  svg.setAttribute("class", cls ? `ico ${cls}` : "ico");
  svg.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  const use = document.createElementNS(SVG, "use");
  use.setAttribute("href", `#i-${name}`);
  svg.append(use);
  return svg;
};

let uid = 0;
const openMenus = new Set();

function isEnabled(ctx, index) {
  return ctx.items[index] && !ctx.items[index].disabled;
}

function currentItem(ctx) {
  return ctx.items.find((item) => item.value === ctx.value);
}

function paintTrigger(ctx) {
  const item = currentItem(ctx);
  if (ctx.config.renderTrigger) return ctx.config.renderTrigger(item, ctx.trigger);
  const span = ctx.trigger.querySelector("span") || ctx.trigger.appendChild(el("span"));
  span.textContent = item ? item.label : "";
}

function scrollNodeIntoPopup(popup, node) {
  if (node.offsetTop < popup.scrollTop) popup.scrollTop = node.offsetTop;
  else if (node.offsetTop + node.offsetHeight > popup.scrollTop + popup.clientHeight)
    popup.scrollTop = node.offsetTop + node.offsetHeight - popup.clientHeight;
}

function setActive(ctx, index, scroll = true) {
  const popup = ctx.pop;
  if (!popup) return;
  ctx.active = index;
  popup.querySelectorAll("[data-active]").forEach((node) => {
    node.removeAttribute("data-active");
  });
  const node = popup.querySelector(`[id="${ctx.id}-${index}"]`);
  if (!node) return popup.removeAttribute("aria-activedescendant");
  node.setAttribute("data-active", "");
  popup.setAttribute("aria-activedescendant", node.id);
  // Scroll the list itself; scrollIntoView also moves the dialog and dismisses this menu.
  if (scroll) scrollNodeIntoPopup(popup, node);
}

function moveActive(ctx, delta, from = ctx.active) {
  let index = from;
  for (let step = 0; step < ctx.items.length; step++) {
    index += delta;
    if (index < 0 || index >= ctx.items.length) return;
    if (isEnabled(ctx, index)) return setActive(ctx, index);
  }
}

function pickSide(preferred, height, above, below) {
  if (preferred === "bottom" && height > below && above > below) return "top";
  if (preferred === "top" && height > above && below > above) return "bottom";
  return preferred;
}

function placeMenu(ctx) {
  const { pop: popup, trigger, config } = ctx;
  const rect = trigger.getBoundingClientRect();
  const viewWidth = document.documentElement.clientWidth,
    viewHeight = window.innerHeight,
    gap = 6,
    pad = 8;
  popup.style.minWidth =
    config.kind === "listbox" && trigger.classList.contains("field") ? `${rect.width}px` : "";
  popup.style.maxHeight = "";
  const height = popup.offsetHeight,
    width = popup.offsetWidth;
  const below = viewHeight - rect.bottom - gap - pad,
    above = rect.top - gap - pad;
  const side = pickSide(config.side, height, above, below);
  const room = side === "top" ? above : below;
  popup.style.maxHeight = `${Math.max(120, Math.min(room, 24 * 16))}px`;
  const shownHeight = Math.min(height, room);
  popup.style.top = `${side === "top" ? rect.top - gap - shownHeight : rect.bottom + gap}px`;
  let left = config.align === "end" ? rect.right - width : rect.left;
  left = Math.max(pad, Math.min(left, viewWidth - width - pad));
  popup.style.left = `${left}px`;
  popup.dataset.side = side;
}

function buildItemNode(ctx, item, index) {
  const { config } = ctx;
  const node = el("div");
  node.id = `${ctx.id}-${index}`;
  node.dataset.value = String(item.value);
  node.setAttribute("role", ctx.itemRole);
  if (config.kind === "listbox")
    node.setAttribute(
      "aria-selected",
      String(item.keep ? !!item.checked : item.value === ctx.value),
    );
  if (item.disabled) node.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "true");
  if (item.tone) node.dataset.tone = item.tone;
  if (item.icon) {
    const itemIcon = icon(item.icon, "m-ico");
    node.append(itemIcon);
  }
  const text = el("span", "m-txt");
  text.append(el("span", "m-label", item.label));
  if (item.desc) text.append(el("span", "m-desc", item.desc));
  node.append(
    text,
    icon(item.children ? "chev-right" : "check", item.children ? "m-ico" : "m-check"),
  );
  if (item.children) node.setAttribute("aria-haspopup", "menu");
  return node;
}

function appendItems(ctx) {
  let group = "";
  ctx.items.forEach((item, index) => {
    if (item.group && item.group !== group) {
      group = item.group;
      const heading = el("div", "m-group", group);
      heading.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
      ctx.pop.append(heading);
    }
    const node = buildItemNode(ctx, item, index);
    node.addEventListener(
      "pointermove",
      () => ctx.active !== index && isEnabled(ctx, index) && setActive(ctx, index, false),
    );
    node.addEventListener("click", () => isEnabled(ctx, index) && chooseItem(ctx, index));
    ctx.pop.append(node);
  });
}

function loadRootItems(ctx) {
  ctx.parents = [];
  ctx.items = ctx.config.getItems ? ctx.config.getItems() : ctx.rootItems;
  ctx.rootItems = ctx.items;
}

function createPopup(ctx, nested) {
  const { config, trigger } = ctx;
  const popup = el("div", "menu");
  ctx.pop = popup;
  popup.id = ctx.id;
  popup.tabIndex = -1;
  popup.setAttribute("role", config.kind);
  popup.setAttribute(
    "aria-label",
    config.label || trigger.getAttribute("aria-label") || trigger.title || "Menu",
  );
  const head = !nested && (typeof config.header === "function" ? config.header() : config.header); // read-only context line, e.g. a thread's full title
  if (head) popup.append(el("div", "m-head", head));
  return popup;
}

function startIndex(ctx, start) {
  const { items } = ctx;
  const selected = items.findIndex((item) => item.value === ctx.value && !item.disabled);
  const first = items.findIndex((_item, index) => isEnabled(ctx, index));
  const last =
    items.length -
    1 -
    [...items].reverse().findIndex((_item, index) => isEnabled(ctx, items.length - 1 - index));
  if (start === "last") return last;
  if (start === "first") return first;
  return selected >= 0 ? selected : first;
}

function openMenu(ctx, start = "selected", nested = false) {
  const { trigger } = ctx;
  if (ctx.pop || trigger.disabled) return;
  if (!nested) loadRootItems(ctx);
  if (!ctx.items.length) return;
  for (const menu of openMenus) menu.close(false);
  const popup = createPopup(ctx, nested);
  appendItems(ctx);
  (trigger.closest("dialog") || document.body).append(popup); // inside an open <dialog> so it sits in the top layer with it
  trigger.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
  trigger.setAttribute("aria-controls", ctx.id);
  placeMenu(ctx);
  ctx.openedAt = performance.now();
  setActive(ctx, startIndex(ctx, start));
  popup.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  popup.addEventListener("keydown", ctx.onKey);
  document.addEventListener("pointerdown", ctx.onOutside, true);
  window.addEventListener("resize", ctx.onDismiss);
  window.addEventListener("scroll", ctx.onScroll, true);
  openMenus.add(ctx.api);
  if (!nested) ctx.config.onOpen();
}

function closeMenu(ctx, returnFocus = true, notify = true) {
  const popup = ctx.pop;
  if (!popup) return;
  ctx.pop = null;
  openMenus.delete(ctx.api);
  document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", ctx.onOutside, true);
  window.removeEventListener("resize", ctx.onDismiss);
  window.removeEventListener("scroll", ctx.onScroll, true);
  ctx.trigger.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  ctx.trigger.removeAttribute("aria-controls");
  popup.remove();
  if (returnFocus) ctx.trigger.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  if (notify) ctx.config.onClose();
}

function showItems(ctx, nextItems, selected) {
  closeMenu(ctx, false, false);
  ctx.items = nextItems;
  ctx.typed = "";
  openMenu(ctx, "first", true);
  if (selected != null) setActive(ctx, selected);
}

function goBack(ctx) {
  const parent = ctx.parents.pop();
  if (parent) showItems(ctx, parent.items, parent.active);
}

function selectValue(ctx, item) {
  const { config } = ctx;
  if (item.keep) {
    config.onSelect(item.value, item);
    return;
  } // an action row (e.g. "Advisor · Opus"): acts without becoming the menu's value
  if (config.kind !== "listbox") {
    config.onSelect(item.value, item);
    return;
  }
  if (item.value === ctx.value) return;
  if (config.onSelect(item.value, item) === false) return;
  ctx.value = item.value;
  paintTrigger(ctx);
}

function chooseItem(ctx, index) {
  const item = ctx.items[index];
  if (item.back) return goBack(ctx);
  if (item.children) {
    ctx.parents.push({ items: ctx.items, active: index });
    return showItems(ctx, [{ value: "menu-back", label: "Back", back: true }, ...item.children]);
  }
  closeMenu(ctx, true);
  selectValue(ctx, item);
}

function keyOpenSubmenu(ctx, event) {
  if (ctx.items[ctx.active]?.children) {
    event.preventDefault();
    chooseItem(ctx, ctx.active);
  }
  return true;
}

function keyBack(ctx, event) {
  if (ctx.parents.length) {
    event.preventDefault();
    goBack(ctx);
  }
  return true;
}

function keyMove(delta, fromEnd) {
  return (ctx, event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (fromEnd == null) moveActive(ctx, delta);
    else moveActive(ctx, delta, fromEnd === "end" ? ctx.items.length : fromEnd);
    return true;
  };
}

function keyChoose(ctx, event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  if (ctx.typed && event.key === " ") return false; // space inside a typeahead word
  if (isEnabled(ctx, ctx.active)) chooseItem(ctx, ctx.active);
  return true;
}

function keyEscape(ctx, event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  event.stopPropagation();
  if (ctx.parents.length) goBack(ctx);
  else closeMenu(ctx, true);
  return true;
}

function keyTab(ctx) {
  closeMenu(ctx, true); // focus is back on the trigger, so Tab moves on from there
  return true;
}

const NAVIGATION_KEYS = {
  ArrowRight: keyOpenSubmenu,
  ArrowLeft: keyBack,
  ArrowDown: keyMove(1),
  ArrowUp: keyMove(-1),
  Home: keyMove(1, -1),
  End: keyMove(-1, "end"),
  Enter: keyChoose,
  " ": keyChoose,
  Escape: keyEscape,
  Tab: keyTab,
};

function typeAhead(ctx, event) {
  const now = Date.now();
  ctx.typed =
    now - ctx.typedAt > 700 ? event.key.toLowerCase() : ctx.typed + event.key.toLowerCase();
  ctx.typedAt = now;
  const order = [...ctx.items.keys()];
  const from = ctx.typed.length === 1 ? ctx.active + 1 : ctx.active; // repeated single letter cycles
  const rotated = order.slice(from).concat(order.slice(0, from));
  const hit = rotated.find(
    (index) => isEnabled(ctx, index) && ctx.items[index].label.toLowerCase().startsWith(ctx.typed),
  );
  if (hit != null) setActive(ctx, hit);
  event.preventDefault();
}

function handleKey(ctx, event) {
  if (event.altKey || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) return;
  const navigate = Object.hasOwn(NAVIGATION_KEYS, event.key) && NAVIGATION_KEYS[event.key];
  if (navigate && navigate(ctx, event)) return;
  if ((event.key.length === 1 && /\S/.test(event.key)) || (event.key === " " && ctx.typed))
    typeAhead(ctx, event);
}

function handleOutside(ctx, event) {
  if (ctx.pop && !ctx.pop.contains(event.target) && !ctx.trigger.contains(event.target))
    closeMenu(ctx, false);
}

// only scrolling that moves the trigger dismisses the menu (the chat scrolling while history loads must not)
function handleScroll(ctx, event) {
  // Ignore the scroll that brings the trigger into view just before a tap opens this menu.
  if (performance.now() - ctx.openedAt < 150) return;
  const target = event.target;
  if (
    ctx.pop &&
    !ctx.pop.contains(target) &&
    (target === document || target.contains?.(ctx.trigger))
  )
    closeMenu(ctx, false);
}

function createMenuApi(ctx) {
  return {
    open: (start, nested) => openMenu(ctx, start, nested),
    close: (returnFocus, notify) => closeMenu(ctx, returnFocus, notify),
    get value() {
      return ctx.value;
    },
    get isOpen() {
      return !!ctx.pop;
    },
    setValue(newValue) {
      ctx.value = newValue;
      paintTrigger(ctx);
    },
    setItems(nextItems, newValue = ctx.value) {
      ctx.rootItems = ctx.items = nextItems;
      ctx.value = newValue;
      paintTrigger(ctx);
    },
    setDisabled(disabled) {
      ctx.trigger.disabled = disabled;
      if (disabled) closeMenu(ctx, false);
    },
    items: () => ctx.items,
  };
}

export function createMenu(opts) {
  const config = {
    kind: "listbox",
    side: "bottom",
    align: "start",
    onSelect() {},
    onOpen() {},
    onClose() {},
    renderTrigger: null,
    label: "",
    ...opts,
  };
  const items = config.items || [];
  const ctx = {
    config,
    trigger: config.trigger,
    id: `menu-${++uid}`,
    itemRole: config.kind === "menu" ? "menuitem" : "option",
    items,
    value: config.value,
    pop: null,
    active: -1,
    typed: "",
    typedAt: 0,
    parents: [],
    rootItems: items,
    openedAt: 0,
    api: null,
  };
  ctx.onKey = (event) => handleKey(ctx, event);
  ctx.onOutside = (event) => handleOutside(ctx, event);
  ctx.onDismiss = () => closeMenu(ctx, false);
  ctx.onScroll = (event) => handleScroll(ctx, event);

  const { trigger } = ctx;
  trigger.setAttribute("aria-haspopup", config.kind === "menu" ? "menu" : "listbox");
  trigger.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  trigger.addEventListener("click", () => (ctx.pop ? closeMenu(ctx, true) : openMenu(ctx)));
  trigger.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
    if (event.key === "ArrowDown" || event.key === "ArrowUp") {
      event.preventDefault();
      ctx.pop || openMenu(ctx, event.key === "ArrowUp" ? "last" : "first");
    }
  });
  ctx.api = createMenuApi(ctx);
  paintTrigger(ctx);
  return ctx.api;
}

================
File: public/questions.js
================
import { el, icon } from "/render.js";

// Question drafts belong to the request, so thread refreshes and socket reconnects cannot erase them.
// Secret answers stay in memory only and are never included in transcript summaries.

const CONNECTION_LOST_MESSAGE =
  "Connection lost. Your answer is saved here; send it after reconnecting.";

function entryKey(thread, questionId) {
  return JSON.stringify([thread, questionId]);
}

function storageKey(thread, questionId) {
  return `ac_question:${entryKey(thread, questionId)}`;
}

function answerValues(entry, question) {
  const draft = entry.drafts[question.id] || {};
  return [
    ...(draft.selected || []),
    ...(draft.other && draft.text?.trim() ? [draft.text.trim()] : []),
  ];
}

function saveEntry(entry) {
  try {
    const drafts = Object.fromEntries(
      entry.event.questions
        .filter((question) => !question.secret)
        .map((question) => [question.id, entry.drafts[question.id] || {}]),
    );
    sessionStorage.setItem(
      storageKey(entry.thread, entry.event.id),
      JSON.stringify({ drafts, step: entry.step }),
    );
  } catch {}
}

function loadDraft(thread, questionId) {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey(thread, questionId)));
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
}

function clearStoredDraft(thread, questionId) {
  try {
    sessionStorage.removeItem(storageKey(thread, questionId));
  } catch {}
}

function remember(ctx, thread, event) {
  const entryId = entryKey(thread, event.id);
  let entry = ctx.entries.get(entryId);
  if (!entry) {
    const draft = loadDraft(thread, event.id);
    entry = {
      thread,
      event,
      drafts: Object.assign(Object.create(null), draft?.drafts || {}),
      step: draft?.step || 0,
      revision: 0,
      active: false,
    };
    ctx.entries.set(entryId, entry);
  }
  // A paged transcript may contain only the answer; retain richer question metadata if available.
  if (event.questions) entry.event = event;
  return entry;
}

function refreshCards(ctx, host = ctx.root()) {
  for (const card of host?.querySelectorAll(".question-card") || []) card.refresh?.();
}

function redraw(ctx, entry) {
  entry.revision++;
  refreshCards(ctx);
  ctx.changed?.();
}

function activateLiveQuestion(ctx, thread, event, entry) {
  const ids = ctx.pending.get(thread) || new Set();
  ids.add(event.id);
  ctx.pending.set(thread, ids);
  entry.active = true;
  delete entry.answer;
}

function closeWithAnswer(ctx, thread, event, entry) {
  entry.active = false;
  entry.busy = false;
  entry.answer = event;
  entry.error = "";
  ctx.pending.get(thread)?.delete(event.id);
  clearStoredDraft(thread, event.id);
  entry.drafts = Object.create(null);
  entry.submitted = null;
}

function receive(ctx, thread, event, live = false) {
  const entry = remember(ctx, thread, event);
  if (event.type === "question" && live) activateLiveQuestion(ctx, thread, event, entry);
  else if (event.type === "question_answer") closeWithAnswer(ctx, thread, event, entry);
  entry.revision++;
  refreshCards(ctx);
  return entry;
}

function dropStaleEntries(ctx, thread, ids) {
  for (const entry of ctx.entries.values())
    if (entry.thread === thread && entry.active && !ids.has(entry.event.id)) {
      entry.active = false;
      entry.busy = false;
      entry.revision++;
    }
}

function sync(ctx, thread, events = []) {
  const ids = new Set(events.map((event) => event.id));
  ctx.pending.set(thread, ids);
  dropStaleEntries(ctx, thread, ids);
  for (const event of events) {
    const entry = remember(ctx, thread, event);
    entry.active = true;
    entry.busy = false;
    delete entry.answer;
    entry.revision++;
  }
  refreshCards(ctx);
}

function failBusyEntries(ctx) {
  for (const entry of ctx.entries.values())
    if (entry.active && entry.busy) {
      entry.busy = false;
      entry.error = CONNECTION_LOST_MESSAGE;
      entry.revision++;
    }
}

function setConnected(ctx, value) {
  ctx.connected = value;
  if (!value) failBusyEntries(ctx);
  for (const entry of ctx.entries.values()) if (entry.active) entry.revision++;
  refreshCards(ctx);
}

function maskSecretAnswers(entry, answers) {
  return Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(answers).map(([questionId, answerList]) => [
      questionId,
      entry.event.questions?.find((question) => question.id === questionId)?.secret
        ? ["Answer sent privately"]
        : answerList,
    ]),
  );
}

function acknowledgeAnswer(ctx, message, entry) {
  if (entry.answer) return; // The canonical transcript answer can arrive before its acknowledgment.
  const submitted = { ...entry.submitted };
  if (submitted.answers) submitted.answers = maskSecretAnswers(entry, submitted.answers);
  receive(ctx, message.thread, { type: "question_answer", id: message.id, ...submitted });
}

function reply(ctx, message) {
  const entry = ctx.entries.get(entryKey(message.thread, message.id));
  if (!entry || (message.request != null && message.request !== entry.request)) return;
  entry.busy = false;
  if (message.t !== "answer_error") {
    acknowledgeAnswer(ctx, message, entry);
    return;
  }
  entry.error = message.message || "Could not send your answer. Try again.";
  redraw(ctx, entry);
}

function submit(ctx, entry, skip = false) {
  if (entry.busy || !entry.active) return;
  const answers = Object.fromEntries(
    (entry.event.questions || []).map((question) => [question.id, answerValues(entry, question)]),
  );
  entry.request = ++ctx.serial;
  entry.submitted = skip ? { skip: true } : { answers };
  const sent = ctx.send({
    t: "answer",
    thread: entry.thread,
    id: entry.event.id,
    ...entry.submitted,
    request: entry.request,
  });
  if (sent) {
    entry.busy = true;
    entry.error = "";
  } else entry.error = "Reconnect to send your answer. Your choices are kept.";
  redraw(ctx, entry);
}

function summaryTitle(answer) {
  if (answer?.cancelled) return "Question cancelled";
  if (answer?.skip) return "Skipped";
  return answer ? "Answered" : "Question closed";
}

function summaryAnswerText(question, raw) {
  if (question.secret) return "Answer sent privately";
  return Array.isArray(raw) ? raw.join(", ") : raw || "Skipped";
}

function buildAnswerList(questions, answer) {
  const list = el("dl", "question-answers");
  const rows = questions.length
    ? questions
    : Object.keys(answer.answers).map((rowId) => ({ id: rowId, header: rowId }));
  for (const question of rows) {
    const text = summaryAnswerText(question, answer.answers[question.id]);
    list.append(el("dt", "", question.header || question.question || "Answer"), el("dd", "", text));
  }
  return list;
}

function renderSummary(card, entry, questions) {
  const answer = entry.answer;
  const head = el("div", "question-summary-head");
  head.append(
    icon(answer && !answer.skip && !answer.cancelled ? "check" : "info"),
    el("span", "", summaryTitle(answer)),
  );
  card.append(head);
  if (answer?.note) card.append(el("p", "question-answer-note", answer.note));
  if (answer?.answers && !answer.skip && !answer.cancelled)
    card.append(buildAnswerList(questions, answer));
}

function buildHead(entry, questions) {
  const head = el("div", "question-head");
  head.append(el("span", "question-status", "Your input"));
  if (questions.length > 1)
    head.append(
      el("span", "question-progress", `Question ${entry.step + 1} of ${questions.length}`),
    );
  return head;
}

function hintText(question) {
  if (question.multiSelect) return "Choose any that apply.";
  return question.options?.length ? "Choose one option." : "Write your answer below.";
}

function updateForm(view) {
  const { entry, question, draft, choices } = view;
  const valid = answerValues(entry, question).length > 0 && (!draft.other || !!draft.text?.trim());
  if (view.next) view.next.disabled = !valid || entry.busy || !view.ctx.connected;
  for (const [input, wrap] of view.otherControls) {
    input.checked = !!draft.other;
    wrap.hidden = !draft.other;
  }
  for (const input of choices.querySelectorAll("input[data-option]"))
    input.checked = draft.selected.includes(input.dataset.option);
  saveEntry(entry);
}

function choiceSelection(view, input, option) {
  const { question, draft } = view;
  if (!question.multiSelect) return [option.label];
  return input.checked
    ? [...draft.selected, option.label]
    : draft.selected.filter((selected) => selected !== option.label);
}

function buildOption(view, option) {
  const { question, draft } = view;
  const label = el("label", "question-option"),
    input = el("input");
  input.type = question.multiSelect ? "checkbox" : "radio";
  input.name = view.fieldId;
  input.value = option.label;
  input.dataset.option = option.label;
  input.checked = draft.selected.includes(option.label);
  const text = el("span", "question-option-text");
  text.append(el("span", "question-option-label", option.label));
  if (option.description)
    text.append(el("span", "question-option-description", option.description));
  input.onchange = () => {
    draft.selected = choiceSelection(view, input, option);
    if (!question.multiSelect) draft.other = false;
    updateForm(view);
  };
  label.append(input, text);
  return label;
}

function buildCustomInput(view) {
  const { question, draft } = view;
  const input = el(question.secret ? "input" : "textarea", "question-text");
  input.id = `${view.fieldId}-other`;
  if (question.secret) {
    input.type = "password";
    input.autocomplete = "off";
  } else {
    input.rows = 2;
    input.placeholder = "Tell the agent what you have in mind…";
  }
  input.value = draft.text || "";
  input.oninput = () => {
    draft.text = input.value;
    updateForm(view);
  };
  return input;
}

function buildOtherToggle(view, input, wrap) {
  const { question, draft } = view;
  const option = el("label", "question-option question-other"),
    toggle = el("input");
  toggle.type = question.multiSelect ? "checkbox" : "radio";
  toggle.name = view.fieldId;
  toggle.checked = !!draft.other;
  toggle.onchange = () => {
    draft.other = toggle.checked;
    if (!question.multiSelect) draft.selected = [];
    updateForm(view);
    if (toggle.checked) input.focus();
  };
  option.append(toggle, el("span", "question-option-label", "Something else"));
  view.choices.append(option);
  view.otherControls.push([toggle, wrap]);
}

function appendCustomAnswer(view) {
  const { question, draft } = view;
  const wrap = el("div", "question-custom"),
    label = el("label", "question-custom-label", "Your answer");
  const input = buildCustomInput(view);
  label.htmlFor = input.id;
  wrap.append(label, input);
  if (question.options?.length) buildOtherToggle(view, input, wrap);
  else draft.other = true;
  wrap.hidden = !draft.other;
  view.choices.append(wrap);
}

function buildChoices(view) {
  const { question } = view;
  for (const option of question.options || []) view.choices.append(buildOption(view, option));
  if (question.allowOther || !question.options?.length) appendCustomAnswer(view);
  return view.choices;
}

function buildField(view) {
  const { card, entry, question, fieldId } = view;
  const field = el("fieldset", "question-field");
  field.disabled = !!entry.busy;
  const legend = el("legend", "question-title", question.question);
  legend.id = `${fieldId}-title`;
  card.setAttribute("aria-labelledby", legend.id);
  field.append(legend);
  const hint = el("p", "question-hint", hintText(question));
  hint.id = `${fieldId}-hint`;
  field.setAttribute("aria-describedby", hint.id);
  field.append(hint);
  field.append(buildChoices(view));
  return field;
}

function buildError(view) {
  const error = el(
    "p",
    "question-error",
    view.entry.error || (!view.ctx.connected ? "Reconnecting… Your choices are kept." : ""),
  );
  error.hidden = !error.textContent;
  error.setAttribute("role", "status");
  return error;
}

function buildSkipButton(view) {
  const skip = el("button", "btn ghost question-skip", "Skip");
  skip.type = "button";
  skip.disabled = view.entry.busy || !view.ctx.connected;
  skip.title = "Skip this request and let the agent continue";
  skip.onclick = () => submit(view.ctx, view.entry, true);
  return skip;
}

function buildBackButton(view) {
  const { ctx, card, entry } = view;
  const back = el("button", "btn ghost question-back", "Back");
  back.type = "button";
  back.disabled = !!entry.busy;
  back.onclick = () => {
    entry.step--;
    saveEntry(entry);
    redraw(ctx, entry);
    card.querySelector("legend")?.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" });
    card.querySelector("input,textarea")?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  };
  return back;
}

function nextLabel(view) {
  if (view.entry.busy) return "Sending…";
  return view.entry.step < view.questions.length - 1 ? "Continue" : "Send answer";
}

function buildFooter(view) {
  const footer = el("div", "question-footer"),
    nav = el("div", "question-nav");
  footer.append(buildSkipButton(view));
  if (view.entry.step > 0) nav.append(buildBackButton(view));
  view.next = el("button", "btn primary question-next", nextLabel(view));
  view.next.type = "submit";
  nav.append(view.next);
  footer.append(nav);
  return footer;
}

function finishQuestions(view) {
  const { ctx, entry, questions } = view;
  const missing = questions.findIndex((item) => !answerValues(entry, item).length);
  if (missing >= 0) {
    entry.step = missing;
    redraw(ctx, entry);
  } else submit(ctx, entry);
}

function handleFormSubmit(view, event) {
  const { ctx, card, entry, questions } = view;
  event.preventDefault();
  if (view.next.disabled) return;
  if (entry.step < questions.length - 1) {
    entry.step++;
    saveEntry(entry);
    redraw(ctx, entry);
    card.querySelector("input,textarea")?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  } else finishQuestions(view);
}

function renderForm(ctx, card, entry, questions, question) {
  const draft = (entry.drafts[question.id] ||= {
    selected: [],
    other: !question.options?.length,
  });
  draft.selected ||= [];
  const view = {
    ctx,
    card,
    entry,
    questions,
    question,
    draft,
    fieldId: `question-${++ctx.serial}`,
    choices: el("div", "question-choices"),
    otherControls: [],
    next: null,
  };
  card.append(buildHead(entry, questions));
  const form = el("form", "question-form");
  form.append(buildField(view), buildError(view));
  form.append(buildFooter(view));
  form.onsubmit = (event) => handleFormSubmit(view, event);
  card.append(form);
  updateForm(view);
}

function render(ctx, card, entry) {
  card.replaceChildren();
  card.classList.toggle("question-summary", !entry.active);
  card.setAttribute("aria-busy", String(!!entry.busy));
  const questions = entry.event.questions || [];
  if (!entry.active) {
    renderSummary(card, entry, questions);
    return;
  }
  entry.step = Math.max(0, Math.min(entry.step, questions.length - 1));
  const question = questions[entry.step];
  if (question) renderForm(ctx, card, entry, questions, question);
}

function mount(ctx, thread, event) {
  const entry = remember(ctx, thread, event),
    card = el("section", "msg question-card");
  card.dataset.questionId = event.id;
  let version = -1;
  card.refresh = () => {
    if (version === entry.revision) return;
    version = entry.revision;
    render(ctx, card, entry);
  };
  card.refresh();
  return card;
}

function ensure(ctx, thread, host = ctx.root()) {
  const ids = new Set(
    [...host.querySelectorAll(".question-card")].map((card) => card.dataset.questionId),
  );
  for (const questionId of ctx.pending.get(thread) || [])
    if (!ids.has(questionId)) {
      host.querySelector(":scope > .empty")?.remove();
      host.append(mount(ctx, thread, ctx.entries.get(entryKey(thread, questionId)).event));
    }
  refreshCards(ctx, host);
}

export function find(host, id) {
  return host?.querySelector(`.question-card[data-question-id="${CSS.escape(id)}"]`) || null;
}

export function createQuestions({ send, root, changed }) {
  const ctx = {
    entries: new Map(),
    pending: new Map(),
    connected: false,
    serial: 0,
    send,
    root,
    changed,
  };
  return {
    mount: (thread, event) => mount(ctx, thread, event),
    receive: (thread, event, live) => receive(ctx, thread, event, live),
    sync: (thread, events) => sync(ctx, thread, events),
    refresh: (host) => refreshCards(ctx, host),
    ensure: (thread, host) => ensure(ctx, thread, host),
    reply: (message) => reply(ctx, message),
    setConnected: (value) => setConnected(ctx, value),
    count: (thread) => ctx.pending.get(thread)?.size || 0,
  };
}

File: public/render.js
