// Rendering helpers shared by the app (and importable on their own): DOM helpers, icons, copy-to-clipboard,
// markdown -> sanitised HTML -> enhanced DOM (code block headers, table wrappers). DOMPurify sanitises ALL markdown output;
// highlight.js only ever produces escaped <span class="hljs-*"> markup and the sanitiser runs over it anyway.
// Media (images, video, ```chart and ```arch blocks) is built from the sanitised DOM: local files go through the authenticated,
// path-scoped /api/file-image and /api/file-media routes, Chart.js is imported lazily on the first chart and /arch.js on the first
// valid ```arch JSON spec (invalid or still-streaming specs stay plain code blocks).

import hljs from "/vendor/hljs/core.js";
import bash from "/vendor/hljs/languages/bash.js";
import css from "/vendor/hljs/languages/css.js";
import diff from "/vendor/hljs/languages/diff.js";
import go from "/vendor/hljs/languages/go.js";
import javascript from "/vendor/hljs/languages/javascript.js";
import json from "/vendor/hljs/languages/json.js";
import markdown from "/vendor/hljs/languages/markdown.js";
import python from "/vendor/hljs/languages/python.js";
import rust from "/vendor/hljs/languages/rust.js";
import sql from "/vendor/hljs/languages/sql.js";
import typescript from "/vendor/hljs/languages/typescript.js";
import xml from "/vendor/hljs/languages/xml.js";
import yaml from "/vendor/hljs/languages/yaml.js";
import { Marked } from "/vendor/marked.js";
import DOMPurify from "/vendor/purify.js";
import { frontmatterToMarkdown } from "./frontmatter.js";

for (const [n, l] of Object.entries({
  bash,
  javascript,
  typescript,
  json,
  python,
  css,
  xml,
  markdown,
  diff,
  yaml,
  sql,
  go,
  rust,
}))
  hljs.registerLanguage(n, l);

// Raw <video>/<source> from message HTML may only point at http(s) or the media route; nothing else is a valid media source.
const SAFE_MEDIA_SRC = /^(?:https?:\/\/|\/api\/file-media\?)/i;
DOMPurify.addHook("afterSanitizeAttributes", (n) => {
  if (
    (n.tagName === "VIDEO" || n.tagName === "SOURCE") &&
    n.hasAttribute("src") &&
    !SAFE_MEDIA_SRC.test(n.getAttribute("src").trim())
  )
    n.removeAttribute("src");
  if (n.tagName === "A") {
    if (n.hasAttribute("data-file-ref")) n.removeAttribute("target");
    else n.setAttribute("target", "_blank");
    n.setAttribute("rel", "noopener noreferrer");
  }
});

// ---------- DOM helpers ----------
export const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
export const el = (tag, cls, text) => {
  const e = document.createElement(tag);
  if (cls) e.className = cls;
  if (text != null) e.textContent = text;
  return e;
};
const SVG = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
/** 16px stroke icon from the sprite in index.html. */
export function icon(name, cls = "") {
  const s = document.createElementNS(SVG, "svg");
  s.setAttribute("class", cls ? `ico ${cls}` : "ico");
  s.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  const u = document.createElementNS(SVG, "use");
  u.setAttribute("href", `#i-${name}`);
  s.append(u);
  return s;
}
/** Icon-only button: aria-label + title carry the tooltip. */
export function iconButton(name, label, cls = "btn icon sm ghost") {
  const b = el("button", cls);
  b.type = "button";
  b.title = label;
  b.setAttribute("aria-label", label);
  b.append(icon(name));
  return b;
}

// ---------- clipboard ----------
export async function copyText(text) {
  try {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    return true;
  } catch {}
  const ta = document.createElement("textarea");
  ta.value = text;
  ta.setAttribute("readonly", "");
  ta.style.cssText = "position:fixed;top:0;left:0;opacity:0;pointer-events:none";
  document.body.append(ta);
  ta.select();
  let ok = false;
  try {
    ok = document.execCommand("copy");
  } catch {}
  ta.remove();
  return ok;
}
/** Copy `getText()` and show a "Copied" state on `btn` for 1.5 s (button keeps its label for icon-only ones via title/aria-label). */
export function wireCopy(btn, getText, label = "Copy") {
  const lab = btn.querySelector(".lab");
  let t = 0;
  btn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
    const ok = await copyText(getText());
    const done = ok ? "Copied" : "Copy failed";
    clearTimeout(t);
    btn.replaceChildren(icon(ok ? "check" : "alert"));
    if (lab) btn.append(el("span", "lab", done));
    btn.setAttribute("aria-label", done);
    btn.title = done;
    btn.dataset.copied = ok ? "1" : "0";
    const live = $("announce");
    if (live) live.textContent = done;
    t = setTimeout(() => {
      btn.replaceChildren(icon("copy"));
      if (lab) btn.append(el("span", "lab", label));
      btn.setAttribute("aria-label", label);
      btn.title = label;
      delete btn.dataset.copied;
      if (live) live.textContent = "";
    }, 1500);
  });
}

// ---------- highlighting ----------
const esc = (s) =>
  s.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;");

function stripLineHash(ref) {
  const hash = ref.indexOf("#");
  if (hash === -1) return { ref, line: null, endLine: null };
  const range = /^#L(\d+)(?:-L?(\d+))?$/i.exec(ref.slice(hash));
  return {
    ref: ref.slice(0, hash),
    line: range ? Number(range[1]) : null,
    endLine: range ? Number(range[2] || range[1]) : null,
  };
}
function fileUrlPath(ref) {
  try {
    const url = new URL(ref);
    if (url.protocol !== "file:" || (url.hostname && url.hostname !== "localhost") || url.search)
      return null;
    return url.pathname;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
function decodeRef(ref) {
  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(ref);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
function stripPosition(ref, where) {
  const position = /:(\d+)(?REDACTED_IP(\d+))?$/.exec(ref);
  if (!position) return ref;
  if (where.line == null) {
    where.line = Number(position[1]);
    where.endLine = where.line;
    where.column = position[2] ? Number(position[2]) : null;
  }
  return ref.slice(0, position.index);
}
function hasForbiddenRefShape(ref) {
  return (
    !ref ||
    /[\u0000-\u001f\u007f\\?]/.test(ref) ||
    /^[a-z][\w+.-]*:/i.test(ref) ||
    ref.startsWith("//")
  );
}
function isReservedRoute(ref) {
  return (
    /^\/(?:api|uploads|export|vendor|fonts)(?:\/|$)/.test(ref) ||
    /^\/(?:dashboard|login|restart|manifest\.webmanifest|sw\.js|icon\.svg)\/?$/.test(ref)
  );
}
// Bare names with no extension are ambiguous; common repository documents remain useful file links.
function isAmbiguousBareName(ref) {
  return (
    !ref.includes("/") &&
    !/\.[\w-]+$/.test(ref) &&
    !/^(?:README|LICENSE|LICENCE|COPYING|NOTICE|Dockerfile|Makefile|AGENTS|CLAUDE)$/i.test(ref)
  );
}
function hasValidLocation(where) {
  for (const position of [where.line, where.endLine, where.column])
    if (position != null && (!Number.isSafeInteger(position) || position < 1)) return false;
  return !(where.endLine != null && where.endLine < where.line);
}
/** A local file reference, never an HTTP URL, app route, or executable scheme. Paths stay relative until the server resolves them. */
export function parseFileRef(value) {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !value || /[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/.test(value)) return null;
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (!trimmed || trimmed.startsWith("#") || trimmed.startsWith("//")) return null;
  const hashed = stripLineHash(trimmed);
  const where = { line: hashed.line, endLine: hashed.endLine, column: null };
  let ref = hashed.ref;
  if (/^file:/i.test(ref)) ref = fileUrlPath(ref);
  if (ref !== null) ref = decodeRef(ref);
  if (ref === null) return null;
  ref = stripPosition(ref, where);
  if (hasForbiddenRefShape(ref) || isReservedRoute(ref) || isAmbiguousBareName(ref)) return null;
  if (!hasValidLocation(where)) return null;
  return { path: ref, line: where.line, endLine: where.endLine, column: where.column };
}
const cache = new Map();
/** Highlighted HTML for `text` (escaped plain text when the language is unknown). Cached: streaming re-renders the same blocks. */
export function highlight(text, lang) {
  const key = `${lang}\0${text}`;
  let html = cache.get(key);
  if (html != null) return html;
  try {
    if (lang && hljs.getLanguage(lang))
      html = hljs.highlight(text, { language: lang, ignoreIllegals: true }).value;
  } catch {}
  html ??= esc(text);
  if (cache.size > 300) cache.delete(cache.keys().next().value);
  cache.set(key, html);
  return html;
}
const LABELS = {
  xml: "HTML",
  html: "HTML",
  js: "JavaScript",
  javascript: "JavaScript",
  jsx: "JavaScript",
  ts: "TypeScript",
  typescript: "TypeScript",
  tsx: "TypeScript",
  py: "Python",
  python: "Python",
  sh: "Shell",
  bash: "Bash",
  zsh: "Shell",
  shell: "Shell",
  json: "JSON",
  css: "CSS",
  md: "Markdown",
  markdown: "Markdown",
  yml: "YAML",
  yaml: "YAML",
  sql: "SQL",
  go: "Go",
  rust: "Rust",
  rs: "Rust",
  diff: "Diff",
  patch: "Diff",
};
export const langLabel = (l) => (l ? LABELS[l] || l : "Text");

const md = new Marked({
  gfm: true,
  breaks: false,
  renderer: {
    link({ href, title, tokens }) {
      if (!parseFileRef(href)) return false;
      // DOMPurify deliberately blocks file://. Carry a validated reference as data, with an inert keyboard-accessible link.
      return `<a href="#" data-file-ref="${esc(href)}"${title ? ` title="${esc(title)}"` : ""}>${this.parser.parseInline(tokens)}</a>`;
    },
    // Local images/videos carry only a data attribute here; enhance() turns it into a scoped URL after sanitising (nothing loads
    // from the inert fragment). Remote ones keep their http(s) src. Anything else (data: images...) uses marked's default.
    image({ href, title, text }) {
      const t = title ? ` title="${esc(title)}"` : "",
        alt = ` alt="${esc(text || "")}"`;
      const video = VIDEO_EXT.test(String(href).split(/[?#]/)[0]);
      if (HTTP_URL.test(href))
        return video
          ? `<video src="${esc(href)}"${alt}${t}></video>`
          : `<img src="${esc(href)}"${alt}${t}>`;
      const ref = parseFileRef(href);
      if (!ref) return false;
      return `<${video ? "video" : "img"} data-local-path="${esc(href)}"${alt}${t}${video ? "></video>" : ">"}`;
    },
    code({ text, lang }) {
      const l = (lang || "")
        .trim()
        .split(/\s+/)[0]
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9+#_-]/g, "");
      if (l === "chart") {
        const key = chartKey(text);
        if (key) return `<div class="chart" data-chart="${key}"></div>\n`;
      }
      if (l === "arch") {
        const key = archKey(text);
        if (key) return `<div class="arch" data-arch="${key}"></div>\n`;
      }
      return `<pre><code class="hljs${l ? ` language-${l}` : ""}">${highlight(text, l)}</code></pre>\n`;
    },
  },
});

/** Wrap a <pre><code> in a block with a header row (language, wrap toggle, copy). */
export function codeBlock(pre) {
  const code = pre.querySelector("code");
  const lang = (/language-([\w+#-]+)/.exec(code?.className || "") || [])[1] || "";
  const box = el("div", "code");
  const head = el("div", "code-head");
  const wrap = iconButton("wrap", "Wrap lines", "btn icon sm ghost");
  wrap.setAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
  wrap.onclick = () => wrap.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(box.classList.toggle("wrapped")));
  const copy = el("button", "btn sm ghost");
  copy.type = "button";
  copy.title = "Copy code";
  copy.setAttribute("aria-label", "Copy");
  copy.append(icon("copy"), el("span", "lab", "Copy"));
  wireCopy(copy, () => (code || pre).textContent.replace(/\n$/, ""));
  head.append(el("span", "lang", langLabel(lang)), wrap, copy);
  pre.replaceWith(box);
  box.append(head, pre);
  pre.tabIndex = 0; // keyboard-scrollable
  pre.setAttribute("role", "region");
  pre.setAttribute("aria-label", `${langLabel(lang)} code`);
}

// ---------- media: images, video, charts ----------
const VIDEO_EXT = /\.(?:mp4|webm|mov|m4v)$/i;
const HTTP_URL = /^https?:\/\/[^\s<>"'`]{1,2000}$/i;
let threadOf = () => null;
/** app.js registers how to find the thread whose files local references are relative to. */
export const setFileScope = (fn) => {
  threadOf = fn;
};
const basename = (p) => String(p).split(/[\\/]/).filter(Boolean).at(-1) || String(p);
/** Authenticated, path-scoped URL for a local file. `repos` widens to the fixed ~/repos root (same scope as the Files sidebar). */
function localUrl(kind, ref, repos) {
  const thread = threadOf();
  if (!repos && !thread) return null;
  const q = new URLSearchParams(
    repos ? { scope: "repos", path: ref.path } : { thread, path: ref.path },
  );
  return `/api/${kind === "video" ? "file-media" : "file-image"}?${q}`;
}
const absolute = (p) => p.startsWith("/") || p.startsWith("~/");

// Streaming re-renders the same message many times a second: reuse media nodes (and live charts) keyed by content instead of
// rebuilding them. `kept` is only set while renderMarkdown runs.
let kept = null;
const keep = (key, build) => {
  const node = kept?.get(key)?.shift() || build();
  node.dataset.keep = key;
  return node;
};

function failure(node, kind, label, open) {
  const box = el("span", "md-fail");
  box.append(
    icon("alert"),
    el("span", "md-fail-text", label || (kind === "video" ? "Video" : "Image")),
  );
  const a = el("a", "md-fail-open", "Open");
  if (open.local) {
    a.href = "#";
    a.dataset.fileRef = open.local;
  } else {
    a.href = open.url;
    a.target = "_blank";
    a.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
  }
  box.append(a);
  box.dataset.keep = node.dataset.keep || "";
  node.replaceWith(box);
}

/** Lazy, zoomable image. Local refs load via /api/file-image; an absolute path outside the thread folder is retried once in the repos scope. */
function buildImage({ alt, title, src, local }) {
  const a = el("a", "md-img");
  const img = el("img", "md-img-el");
  img.alt = alt;
  img.loading = "lazy";
  img.decoding = "async";
  img.referrerPolicy = "no-referrer";
  const label = alt || basename(local?.path || src);
  a.title = title || label;
  const set = (url) => {
    img.src = url;
    if (local) a.href = url;
  };
  let retried = false;
  if (local) {
    const url = localUrl("image", local, false);
    if (!url) {
      queueMicrotask(() => failure(a, "image", label, { local: local.raw }));
      return a;
    }
    a.dataset.viewerImage = "true"; // chat click handler opens the shared viewer (blob preview, Escape to close)
    set(url);
  } else {
    a.href = src;
    a.target = "_blank";
    a.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
    img.src = src;
  }
  img.addEventListener("load", () => a.classList.add("loaded"));
  img.addEventListener("error", () => {
    if (local && !retried && absolute(local.path)) {
      retried = true;
      set(localUrl("image", local, true));
      return;
    }
    failure(a, "image", label, local ? { local: local.raw } : { url: src });
  });
  a.append(img);
  return a;
}

/** Zoomable preview of a local image file by path, for tool cards (a Read of a screenshot). */
export const localImage = (file) =>
  buildImage({ alt: basename(file), src: file, local: { path: file, raw: file } });
export const isImageFile = (file) =>
  typeof file === "string" && /\.(?:png|jpe?g|gif|webp)$/i.test(file);

/** Inline player. preload=metadata keeps phones from downloading whole files; the route supports Range so seeking works. */
function buildVideo({ alt, title, src, local }) {
  const box = el("span", "md-media");
  const video = el("video", "md-video");
  video.controls = true;
  video.playsInline = true;
  video.preload = "metadata";
  video.setAttribute("aria-label", alt || basename(local?.path || src));
  if (title) video.title = title;
  let retried = false;
  const url = local ? localUrl("video", local, false) : src;
  if (!url) {
    queueMicrotask(() => failure(box, "video", alt, { local: local.raw }));
    return box;
  }
  video.src = url;
  video.addEventListener("error", () => {
    if (local && !retried && absolute(local.path)) {
      retried = true;
      video.src = localUrl("video", local, true);
      return;
    }
    failure(box, "video", alt || basename(local?.path || src), { url: video.currentSrc || url });
  });
  box.append(video);
  return box;
}

// -- charts --
const CHART_TYPES = new Set([
  "bar",
  "line",
  "pie",
  "doughnut",
  "scatter",
  "radar",
  "polarArea",
  "bubble",
]);
const RADIAL = new Set(["pie", "doughnut", "polarArea"]);
const MAX = { labels: 200, datasets: 8, points: 500, total: 2000 };
const COLOR = /^(?:#[0-9a-f]{3,8}|(?:rgb|hsl)a?\([\d\s.,%/-]+\))$/i;
const chartConfigs = new Map(); // key -> normalised config (or null) for the code renderer and the mount step
const str = (v, n) => (typeof v === "string" || typeof v === "number" ? String(v).slice(0, n) : "");
const num = (v) => (typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v) ? v : null);
const color = (v) => (typeof v === "string" && COLOR.test(v.trim()) ? v.trim() : null);
function point(v, type) {
  if (v == null) return null;
  if (typeof v === "number") return Number.isFinite(v) ? v : null;
  if (typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v) && (type === "scatter" || type === "bubble")) {
    const x = num(v.x) ?? (typeof v.x === "string" ? v.x.slice(0, 80) : null),
      y = num(v.y);
    if (x == null || y == null) return undefined;
    return type === "bubble" ? { x, y, r: Math.min(Math.max(num(v.r) ?? 5, 1), 40) } : { x, y };
  }
  return undefined; // invalid
}
function axis(v) {
  if (!v || typeof v !== "object") return {};
  const out = {};
  const title = str(v.title?.text ?? v.title, 80);
  if (title) out.title = title;
  if (typeof v.stacked === "boolean") out.stacked = v.stacked;
  if (typeof v.beginAtZero === "boolean") out.beginAtZero = v.beginAtZero;
  if (num(v.min) != null) out.min = v.min;
  if (num(v.max) != null) out.max = v.max;
  if (v.type === "linear" || v.type === "logarithmic" || v.type === "category") out.type = v.type;
  return out;
}
function parseChartJson(src) {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(src);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
const paintColors = (value) =>
  Array.isArray(value) ? value.slice(0, MAX.labels).map(color) : color(value);
function normaliseDataset(raw, type) {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object" || !Array.isArray(raw.data) || raw.data.length > MAX.points)
    return null;
  const data = [];
  for (const value of raw.data) {
    const pointValue = point(value, type);
    if (pointValue === undefined) return null;
    data.push(pointValue);
  }
  const fillColors = paintColors(raw.backgroundColor),
    edgeColors = paintColors(raw.borderColor);
  return {
    label: str(raw.label, 80),
    data,
    bg: fillColors && [].concat(fillColors).every(Boolean) ? fillColors : null,
    border: edgeColors && [].concat(edgeColors).every(Boolean) ? edgeColors : null,
    fill: raw.fill === true,
    tension: Math.min(Math.max(num(raw.tension) ?? 0.25, 0), 1),
    stack: str(raw.stack, 40) || undefined,
  };
}
function normaliseDatasets(rawSets, type) {
  let total = 0;
  const datasets = [];
  for (const raw of rawSets) {
    const dataset = normaliseDataset(raw, type);
    if (!dataset) return null;
    total += dataset.data.length;
    datasets.push(dataset);
  }
  return total > MAX.total || !total ? null : datasets;
}
/** Strict JSON -> a small, validated config (never the raw object), or null while the JSON is incomplete or invalid. */
function normaliseChart(src) {
  const json = parseChartJson(src);
  if (!json || typeof json !== "object" || Array.isArray(json) || !CHART_TYPES.has(json.type))
    return null;
  const rawSets = json.data?.datasets;
  if (!Array.isArray(rawSets) || !rawSets.length || rawSets.length > MAX.datasets) return null;
  const labels = Array.isArray(json.data.labels)
    ? json.data.labels.slice(0, MAX.labels).map((label) => str(label, 60))
    : [];
  const datasets = normaliseDatasets(rawSets, json.type);
  if (!datasets) return null;
  const opts = json.options && typeof json.options === "object" ? json.options : {};
  const plugins = opts.plugins && typeof opts.plugins === "object" ? opts.plugins : {};
  return {
    type: json.type,
    labels,
    datasets,
    title: str(json.title ?? plugins.title?.text, 120),
    legend: plugins.legend?.display !== false,
    horizontal: opts.indexAxis === "y",
    x: axis(opts.scales?.x),
    y: axis(opts.scales?.y),
  };
}
function chartKey(src) {
  let h = 5381;
  for (let i = 0; i < src.length; i++) h = ((h << 5) + h + src.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;
  const key = `${(h >>> 0).toString(36)}.${src.length}`;
  if (!chartConfigs.has(key)) {
    if (chartConfigs.size > 60) chartConfigs.delete(chartConfigs.keys().next().value);
    const cfg = normaliseChart(src);
    chartConfigs.set(key, cfg && { cfg, src });
  }
  return chartConfigs.get(key) ? key : null;
}

// -- architecture diagrams (```arch JSON, built by /arch.js) --
const archConfigs = new Map(); // key -> parsed spec (or null) for the data-arch holder
function archKey(src) {
  let h = 5381;
  for (let i = 0; i < src.length; i++) h = ((h << 5) + h + src.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;
  const key = `${(h >>> 0).toString(36)}.${src.length}`;
  if (!archConfigs.has(key)) {
    if (archConfigs.size > 60) archConfigs.delete(archConfigs.keys().next().value);
    archConfigs.set(key, parseArch(src));
  }
  return archConfigs.get(key) ? key : null;
}
function parseArch(src) {
  try {
    const spec = JSON.parse(src);
    return spec && typeof spec === "object" && Array.isArray(spec.items) ? spec : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
let archLib;
function archFigure(key) {
  const box = el("div", "arch");
  (archLib ||= import("/arch.js").catch((err) => {
    archLib = null;
    throw err;
  }))
    .then((lib) => {
      const spec = archConfigs.get(key);
      const errors = lib.validateArch(spec);
      if (errors.length) throw new Error(errors[0]);
      return lib.buildArch(spec, box);
    })
    .catch(() => {
      // Invalid spec or /arch.js unavailable: show it as an ordinary code block.
      const pre = el("pre");
      const code = el("code");
      highlightInto(code, JSON.stringify(archConfigs.get(key), null, 2), "json");
      pre.append(code);
      box.replaceChildren(pre);
      box.className = "";
      codeBlock(pre);
    });
  return box;
}

let chartLib;
const loadChart = () =>
  (chartLib ||= import("/vendor/chart.umd.min.js")
    .then(() => {
      if (!globalThis.Chart) throw new Error("Chart.js did not load");
      return globalThis.Chart;
    })
    .catch((e) => {
      chartLib = null;
      throw e;
    }));
const liveCharts = new Set();
const cssVar = (name) => getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(name).trim();
const alpha = (c, a) => {
  const m = /^#([0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(c);
  return m ? `rgba(${[0, 2, 4].map((i) => parseInt(m[1].slice(i, i + 2), 16)).join(",")},${a})` : c;
};

function chartOptions(cfg) {
  const t2 = cssVar("--t2"),
    edge = cssVar("--edge"),
    still = matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)").matches;
  const axisOpts = (a) => ({
    ...(a.stacked != null && { stacked: a.stacked }),
    ...(a.beginAtZero != null && { beginAtZero: a.beginAtZero }),
    ...(a.min != null && { min: a.min }),
    ...(a.max != null && { max: a.max }),
    ...(a.type && { type: a.type }),
    border: { color: edge },
    grid: { color: edge },
    ticks: { color: t2, maxRotation: 0, autoSkipPadding: 12 },
    title: a.title ? { display: true, text: a.title, color: t2 } : { display: false },
  });
  return {
    responsive: true,
    maintainAspectRatio: false,
    animation: still ? false : { duration: 240 },
    ...(cfg.horizontal && { indexAxis: "y" }),
    font: { family: cssVar("--font-ui") || "system-ui" },
    plugins: {
      legend: {
        display: cfg.legend && (cfg.datasets.length > 1 || RADIAL.has(cfg.type)),
        position: "bottom",
        labels: { color: t2, boxWidth: 10, boxHeight: 10, usePointStyle: true },
      },
      title: cfg.title
        ? {
            display: true,
            text: cfg.title,
            color: cssVar("--t1"),
            font: { size: 14, weight: 600 },
            padding: { bottom: 12 },
          }
        : { display: false },
      tooltip: {
        backgroundColor: cssVar("--pop"),
        titleColor: cssVar("--t1"),
        bodyColor: cssVar("--t2"),
        borderColor: cssVar("--edge-strong"),
        borderWidth: 1,
        padding: 10,
      },
    },
    ...(!RADIAL.has(cfg.type) &&
      cfg.type !== "radar" && { scales: { x: axisOpts(cfg.x), y: axisOpts(cfg.y) } }),
    ...(cfg.type === "radar" && {
      scales: {
        r: {
          angleLines: { color: edge },
          grid: { color: edge },
          pointLabels: { color: t2 },
          ticks: { color: t2, backdropColor: "transparent" },
        },
      },
    }),
  };
}
function datasetBackground(cfg, series, base, fills) {
  if (series.bg) return series.bg;
  if (fills) return fills;
  if (cfg.type === "line" || cfg.type === "radar") return alpha(base, 0.18);
  return cfg.type === "scatter" || cfg.type === "bubble" ? alpha(base, 0.7) : base;
}
function datasetBorderWidth(cfg, radial, line) {
  if (radial) return 2;
  return line || cfg.type === "scatter" ? 2 : 0;
}
function chartDataset(cfg, series, index, palette, surface) {
  const base = palette[index % palette.length];
  const radial = RADIAL.has(cfg.type);
  const cycle = cfg.labels.length ? cfg.labels : series.data;
  const fills = radial ? cycle.map((_label, slot) => palette[slot % palette.length]) : null;
  const line = cfg.type === "line" || cfg.type === "radar";
  return {
    label: series.label || `Series ${index + 1}`,
    data: series.data,
    stack: series.stack,
    tension: series.tension,
    fill: series.fill || cfg.type === "radar",
    backgroundColor: datasetBackground(cfg, series, base, fills),
    borderColor: series.border || (radial ? surface : base),
    borderWidth: datasetBorderWidth(cfg, radial, line),
    ...(cfg.type === "bar" && { borderRadius: 4, maxBarThickness: 48 }),
    ...(line && { pointRadius: series.data.length > 40 ? 0 : 3, pointHoverRadius: 5 }),
  };
}
function chartData(cfg) {
  const palette = Array.from(
    { length: 6 },
    (_, slot) => cssVar(`--viz-${slot + 1}`) || cssVar("--accent"),
  );
  const surface = cssVar("--s1");
  return {
    labels: cfg.labels,
    datasets: cfg.datasets.map((series, index) =>
      chartDataset(cfg, series, index, palette, surface),
    ),
  };
}
/** Fallback / accessible data table: rows = labels (or points), columns = datasets. */
function chartTable(cfg) {
  const t = el("table");
  const head = el("tr");
  const scatter = cfg.type === "scatter" || cfg.type === "bubble";
  for (const h of scatter
    ? ["Series", "x", "y"]
    : ["", ...cfg.datasets.map((d, i) => d.label || `Series ${i + 1}`)])
    head.append(el("th", "", h));
  const thead = el("thead"),
    body = el("tbody");
  thead.append(head);
  t.append(thead, body);
  const rows = [];
  if (scatter)
    cfg.datasets.forEach((d, i) => {
      d.data.forEach((p) => {
        if (p) rows.push([d.label || `Series ${i + 1}`, p.x, p.y]);
      });
    });
  else
    for (let r = 0; r < Math.max(cfg.labels.length, ...cfg.datasets.map((d) => d.data.length)); r++)
      rows.push([cfg.labels[r] ?? r + 1, ...cfg.datasets.map((d) => d.data[r] ?? "")]);
  for (const r of rows.slice(0, 60)) {
    const tr = el("tr");
    r.forEach((v) => {
      tr.append(el("td", "", String(v)));
    });
    body.append(tr);
  }
  const w = el("div", "table-wrap");
  w.append(t);
  numericColumns(t);
  return w;
}
function chartFigure(key) {
  const { cfg, src } = chartConfigs.get(key);
  const fig = el("figure", "chart");
  fig.setAttribute("aria-busy", "true");
  const label = `${cfg.title || "Chart"}: ${cfg.type} chart, ${cfg.datasets.length} ${cfg.datasets.length === 1 ? "series" : "series"}`;
  const stage = el("div", "chart-stage");
  stage.dataset.shape = RADIAL.has(cfg.type) || cfg.type === "radar" ? "round" : "wide";
  const canvas = el("canvas");
  canvas.setAttribute("role", "img");
  canvas.setAttribute("aria-label", label);
  stage.append(canvas);
  const data = el("details", "chart-data");
  data.append(el("summary", "", "Data table"), chartTable(cfg));
  fig.append(stage, data);
  const draw = async () => {
    try {
      const Chart = await loadChart();
      fig.__chart?.destroy();
      fig.__chart = new Chart(canvas, {
        type: cfg.type,
        data: chartData(cfg),
        options: chartOptions(cfg),
      });
      liveCharts.add(fig);
      fig.removeAttribute("aria-busy");
    } catch {
      fig.__chart = null;
      liveCharts.delete(fig);
      fig.removeAttribute("aria-busy");
      const pre = document.createElement("pre"),
        code = el("code");
      code.innerHTML = DOMPurify.sanitize(highlight(src, "json"));
      pre.append(code);
      stage.replaceWith(el("p", "chart-note", "Chart unavailable. Showing the data instead."));
      data.open = true;
      fig.append(pre);
      codeBlock(pre);
    }
  };
  fig.__draw = draw; // theme changes re-draw the same figure
  return fig;
}
globalThis.matchMedia?.("(prefers-color-scheme:dark)").addEventListener?.("change", () => {
  for (const f of liveCharts) f.__draw();
});
function pruneCharts() {
  if (liveCharts.size < 24) return;
  for (const f of liveCharts)
    if (!f.isConnected) {
      f.__chart?.destroy();
      liveCharts.delete(f);
    }
}

// -- tables --
const NUMERIC = /^[\s$€£¥+\-−~≈<>]*\d[\d,.\s]*(?:[kKmMbBx×%]|ms|s|MB|GB|KB)?\s*$/;
function numericColumns(table) {
  const rows = [...table.querySelectorAll("tbody tr")];
  if (!rows.length) return;
  const cols = table.querySelectorAll("thead th").length || rows[0].cells.length;
  for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
    const cells = rows.map((r) => r.cells[c]).filter(Boolean);
    if (
      cells.length &&
      cells.every((td) => !td.textContent.trim() || NUMERIC.test(td.textContent))
    ) {
      for (const td of [table.querySelector("thead tr")?.cells[c], ...cells])
        td?.classList.add("num");
    }
  }
}

// -- links in prose --
/** Bare URL or file path (with an extension) that is the whole content of an inline code span. */
function codeTarget(t) {
  if (!t || /\s/.test(t) || t.length > 2000 || t.startsWith("@")) return null; // `@AGENTS.md` is an import, not a path
  if (HTTP_URL.test(t)) {
    try {
      new URL(t);
      return { url: t };
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }
  if (
    !/^(?:file:\/\/|~\/|\.{1,2}\/|\/)?[^\s/][^\s]*\.[A-Za-z0-9]{1,10}(?REDACTED_IP\d+(?REDACTED_IP\d+)?)?(?:#L\d+(?:-L?\d+)?)?$/.test(
      t,
    ) &&
    !/^(?:file:\/\/|~\/|\.{1,2}\/|\/)[^\s]+\.[A-Za-z0-9]{1,10}(?REDACTED_IP\d+(?REDACTED_IP\d+)?)?$/.test(t)
  )
    return null;
  const ref = parseFileRef(t);
  return ref ? { local: t, ref } : null;
}
const HASH_REF = /(^|[\s(\[])#(\d{1,6})(?!\w)/g;
const HASH_SKIP = "pre, code, a, .md-media, .chart, .arch";
function hashRefNodes(text) {
  const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
  let last = 0;
  for (const match of text.matchAll(HASH_REF)) {
    const start = match.index + match[1].length;
    frag.append(text.slice(last, start));
    const link = el("a", "ref-link");
    link.href = "#";
    link.dataset.ghRef = match[2];
    link.textContent = `#${match[2]}`;
    frag.append(link);
    last = start + match[2].length + 1;
  }
  frag.append(text.slice(last));
  return frag;
}
function linkHashRefs(root) {
  const walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
  const hits = [];
  for (let node = walker.nextNode(); node; node = walker.nextNode()) {
    HASH_REF.lastIndex = 0;
    if (HASH_REF.test(node.nodeValue) && !node.parentElement?.closest(HASH_SKIP)) hits.push(node);
  }
  for (const node of hits) node.replaceWith(hashRefNodes(node.nodeValue));
}
function linkCodeSpans(root) {
  for (const code of root.querySelectorAll(":not(pre) > code")) {
    if (code.closest("a")) continue;
    const text = code.textContent.trim(),
      target = codeTarget(text);
    if (!target) continue;
    const link = el("a", "code-link");
    if (target.url) {
      link.href = target.url;
      link.target = "_blank";
      link.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
    } else {
      link.href = "#";
      link.dataset.fileRef = target.local;
    }
    code.replaceWith(link);
    link.append(code);
  }
}
function videoSpec(link) {
  const href = link.getAttribute("href") || "";
  if (link.dataset.fileRef) {
    const ref = parseFileRef(link.dataset.fileRef);
    if (ref && VIDEO_EXT.test(ref.path))
      return { local: { ...ref, raw: link.dataset.fileRef }, key: ref.path };
  } else if (HTTP_URL.test(href) && VIDEO_EXT.test(href.split(/[?#]/)[0]))
    return { src: href, key: href };
  return null;
}
// Links to video files get a player after their paragraph/item; the link stays as a tappable fallback.
function addVideoPlayers(root) {
  let players = 0;
  for (const link of root.querySelectorAll("a")) {
    if (players >= 8 || link.closest("pre, .md-media, .md-fail, .chart, .arch")) continue;
    const spec = videoSpec(link);
    if (!spec) continue;
    const block = link.closest("p, li, td, th, blockquote, h1, h2, h3, h4") || link.parentElement;
    if (
      !block ||
      [...block.querySelectorAll(".md-media")].some((m) => m.dataset.keep === `video:${spec.key}`)
    )
      continue;
    block.append(
      keep(`video:${spec.key}`, () => buildVideo({ alt: link.textContent.trim(), ...spec })),
    );
    players++;
  }
}
function linkify(root) {
  linkHashRefs(root);
  linkCodeSpans(root);
  addVideoPlayers(root);
}

// -- placeholders -> nodes --
function plainMediaFallback(node, isVideo, src) {
  if (isVideo && !src) node.remove();
  else if (isVideo) {
    node.controls = true;
    node.setAttribute("playsinline", "");
    node.setAttribute("preload", "metadata");
    node.classList.add("md-video");
  }
}
function mountMediaNode(node) {
  const alt = node.getAttribute("alt") || "",
    title = node.getAttribute("title") || "";
  const raw = node.dataset.localPath,
    ref = raw ? parseFileRef(raw) : null,
    src = node.getAttribute("src");
  const isVideo = node.tagName === "VIDEO";
  if (!ref && !(src && HTTP_URL.test(src))) {
    plainMediaFallback(node, isVideo, src);
    return; // data:/blob: images and same-origin URLs are left as sanitised
  }
  if (isVideo && node.closest(".md-media")) return;
  const spec = { alt, title, src: ref ? null : src, local: ref && { ...ref, raw } };
  const key = `${isVideo ? "video" : "img"}:${ref ? `${threadOf()}:${ref.path}` : src}`;
  node.replaceWith(keep(key, () => (isVideo ? buildVideo(spec) : buildImage(spec))));
}
function mountChartHolder(holder, mounts) {
  const key = holder.dataset.chart;
  if (!chartConfigs.get(key)) {
    holder.remove();
    return;
  }
  const figure = keep(`chart:${key}`, () => {
    const built = chartFigure(key);
    mounts.push(built);
    return built;
  });
  holder.replaceWith(figure);
}
function mountArchHolder(holder) {
  const key = holder.dataset.arch;
  if (!archConfigs.get(key)) {
    holder.remove();
    return;
  }
  holder.replaceWith(keep(`arch:${key}`, () => archFigure(key)));
}
function mountMedia(root, mounts) {
  for (const node of root.querySelectorAll("img, video")) mountMediaNode(node);
  for (const holder of root.querySelectorAll(".chart[data-chart]"))
    mountChartHolder(holder, mounts);
  for (const holder of root.querySelectorAll(".arch[data-arch]")) mountArchHolder(holder);
}
/** Post-sanitise structure: code headers, scrollable tables, media, linked paths. Operates on already-sanitised DOM (or a fragment of it). */
export function enhance(root, mounts = []) {
  for (const pre of root.querySelectorAll("pre")) if (!pre.closest(".code")) codeBlock(pre);
  for (const t of root.querySelectorAll("table")) {
    if (t.parentElement?.classList.contains("table-wrap")) continue;
    const w = el("div", "table-wrap");
    t.replaceWith(w);
    w.append(t);
    numericColumns(t);
  }
  linkify(root);
  mountMedia(root, mounts);
}

/** Markdown source -> sanitised, highlighted, enhanced DOM inside `target`. Media/chart nodes survive re-renders (streaming) when unchanged. */
export function renderMarkdown(src, target) {
  const old = new Map();
  for (const n of target.querySelectorAll("[data-keep]")) {
    if (!old.has(n.dataset.keep)) old.set(n.dataset.keep, []);
    old.get(n.dataset.keep).push(n);
  }
  const content = frontmatterToMarkdown(src || "");
  const frag = DOMPurify.sanitize(md.parse(content), {
    RETURN_DOM_FRAGMENT: true,
    FORBID_ATTR: ["autoplay"],
  });
  const mounts = [];
  kept = old;
  try {
    enhance(frag, mounts);
  } finally {
    kept = null;
  }
  target.replaceChildren(frag);
  for (const list of old.values())
    for (const n of list) {
      n.__chart?.destroy();
      liveCharts.delete(n);
    }
  pruneCharts();
  for (const f of mounts) f.__draw();
  return target;
}

/** Highlight plain code into an existing <code> element (used for tool-card bodies). */
export function highlightInto(codeEl, text, lang) {
  codeEl.className = `hljs${lang ? ` language-${lang}` : ""}`;
  codeEl.innerHTML = DOMPurify.sanitize(highlight(text, lang));
}
export const langFromPath = (p, content = "") => {
  const ext = /\.([A-Za-z0-9]+)$/.exec(String(p || ""))?.[1]?.toLowerCase();
  const shebang = /^#![^\n]*(?:\/|\s)(bash|sh|zsh|dash|ksh|python[\d.]*|node)(?:\s|$)/.exec(
    content.slice(0, 200),
  );
  if (shebang)
    return /^(bash|sh|zsh|dash|ksh)$/.test(shebang[1])
      ? "bash"
      : shebang[1].startsWith("python")
        ? "python"
        : "javascript";
  return (
    {
      js: "javascript",
      mjs: "javascript",
      cjs: "javascript",
      jsx: "javascript",
      ts: "typescript",
      tsx: "typescript",
      json: "json",
      py: "python",
      css: "css",
      html: "xml",
      xml: "xml",
      svg: "xml",
      md: "markdown",
      yml: "yaml",
      yaml: "yaml",
      sql: "sql",
      go: "go",
      rs: "rust",
      sh: "bash",
      bash: "bash",
      diff: "diff",
      patch: "diff",
    }[ext] || ""
  );
};

File: public/session-search.js

const form = document.getElementById("session-search-form");
const query = document.getElementById("session-query");
const provider = document.getElementById("session-provider");
const status = document.getElementById("search-status");
const results = document.getElementById("session-results");
let pending;
function heading(item, href) {
  const h = element("h2");
  const link = element("a", item.title || item.thread);
  link.href = href;
  h.append(link);
  if (item.archived) h.append(element("span", "Archived", "badge-archived"));
  return h;
}
function element(tag, text, className) {
  const node = document.createElement(tag);
  if (text !== undefined) node.textContent = text;
  if (className) node.className = className;
  return node;
}
// A GitHub PR/issue/comment URL, owner/repo#n or #n asks "which session raised this PR?" instead of searching text.
const PR_REF =
  /^(https:\/\/github\.com\/[\w.-]+\/[\w.-]+\/(pull|issues)\/\d+\S*|[\w.-]+\/[\w.-]+#\d+|#\d+)$/;
async function lookupPrs(request, ref) {
  const response = await fetch("/api/sessions/prs?" + new URLSearchParams({ ref }), {
    signal: request.signal,
    cache: "no-store",
  });
  if (response.status === 401) {
    location.assign("/login");
    return;
  }
  const body = await response.json();
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(body.error || "PR lookup failed. Try again.");
  if (request !== pending) return;
  status.textContent = body.prs.length
    ? `${body.prs.length} session${body.prs.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} raised this PR`
    : "No session raised this PR. It may have been opened by hand, by automation, or before logs were kept.";
  for (const pr of body.prs) {
    const row = element("li");
    const pull = element("a", `${pr.repo}#${pr.number}`);
    pull.href = pr.url;
    pull.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
    const meta = element("p", `${pr.provider} · line ${pr.line} · `, "search-meta");
    meta.append(pull);
    row.append(heading(pr, pr.sessionUrl), meta);
    results.append(row);
  }
}
function groupByThread(hits) {
  const groups = new Map();
  for (const hit of hits) {
    if (!groups.has(hit.thread)) groups.set(hit.thread, []);
    groups.get(hit.thread).push(hit);
  }
  return groups;
}

async function loadSurroundingMessages(details, hit) {
  if (!details.open || details.dataset.loaded) return;
  details.dataset.loaded = "true";
  const text = element("pre", "Loading messages…");
  details.append(text);
  try {
    const response = await fetch(
      `/api/sessions/${encodeURIComponent(hit.thread)}?start=${Math.max(1, hit.line - 2)}&limit=8`,
      { cache: "no-store" },
    );
    const body = await response.json();
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(body.error || "Could not load messages.");
    text.textContent = body.events
      .map((event) => `Line ${event.line} · ${event.role}\n${event.text}`)
      .join("\n\n");
  } catch (error) {
    text.textContent = error.message;
    delete details.dataset.loaded;
  }
}

function buildHitBox(hit) {
  const box = element("div", undefined, "hit");
  box.append(
    element("p", `${hit.role} · line ${hit.line}`, "search-meta"),
    element("p", hit.snippet || hit.text),
  );
  const details = element("details");
  details.append(element("summary", "Read surrounding messages"));
  details.addEventListener("toggle", () => loadSurroundingMessages(details, hit));
  box.append(details);
  return box;
}

function buildGroupRow(hits) {
  const first = hits[0];
  const row = element("li");
  row.append(
    heading(first, "/#thread=" + encodeURIComponent(first.thread)),
    element(
      "p",
      `${first.provider} · ${hits.length} matching message${hits.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`,
      "search-meta",
    ),
  );
  for (const hit of hits) row.append(buildHitBox(hit));
  return row;
}

async function runTextSearch(request, params) {
  const response = await fetch("/api/sessions/search?" + params, {
    signal: request.signal,
    cache: "no-store",
  });
  if (response.status === 401) {
    location.assign("/login");
    return;
  }
  const body = await response.json();
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(body.error || "Search failed. Try again.");
  if (request !== pending) return;
  status.textContent = body.results.length
    ? `${body.results.length} matching messages · ${body.indexedSessions} sessions searched`
    : "No matches. Try fewer words or another provider.";
  for (const hits of groupByThread(body.results).values()) results.append(buildGroupRow(hits));
}

async function search() {
  pending?.abort();
  const request = (pending = new AbortController());
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ q: query.value, limit: "30" });
  if (provider.value) params.set("provider", provider.value);
  history.replaceState(null, "", "/sessions?" + params);
  results.replaceChildren();
  status.textContent = "Searching sessions…";
  results.setAttribute("aria-busy", "true");
  try {
    if (PR_REF.test(query.value.trim())) await lookupPrs(request, query.value.trim());
    else await runTextSearch(request, params);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.name !== "AbortError" && request === pending) status.textContent = error.message;
  } finally {
    if (request === pending) results.setAttribute("aria-busy", "false");
  }
}
form.addEventListener("submit", (event) => {
  event.preventDefault();
  search();
});
provider.addEventListener("change", () => {
  if (query.value.trim()) search();
});
const initial = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
query.value = initial.get("q") || "";
if ([...provider.options].some((option) => option.value === initial.get("provider")))
  provider.value = initial.get("provider");
if (query.value.trim()) search();

/* Agent Console
   One token layer (this block) -> one button system -> components. Nothing below :root hard-codes a colour, size or duration.
   Surfaces are layered by tone (s0 canvas, s1 panel, s2 raised, s3 hover/pressed); lines are used only where they carry meaning.
   Red marks errors, destructive actions and near-exhausted usage meters. Bypass permissions is amber.
   Type: Newsreader (conversation text) + Inter (UI) + JetBrains Mono (code), all self-hosted variable fonts, SIL OFL 1.1. */

================
File: public/tool-display.js

// Legacy logs can contain WebSearch {query:""}; no metadata exists to reconstruct its action.
export const toolName = (name, input) => {
  if (
    name === "WebSearch" &&
    !input?.query?.trim() &&
    !input?.queries?.length &&
    !input?.description
  )
    return "WebBrowse";
  // MCP tools arrive as `mcp__<server>__<tool>`; show the readable `server/tool` instead.
  if (name.startsWith("mcp__")) {
    const parts = name.slice(5).split("__");
    const server = parts[0];
    const tool = parts.slice(1).join("__");
    return server && tool ? `${server}/${tool}` : name;
  }
  return name;
};
// Shorten an absolute file path to a readable, identifying summary. Keep the
// repo name onward when under a workspace, otherwise the last two segments, so
// the full absolute path is never clipped mid-segment in the UI.
const shortPath = (p) => {
  if (typeof p !== "string" || !p) return "";
  const segs = p.split(/[/\\]/).filter(Boolean);
  const i = segs.lastIndexOf("repos");
  if (i !== -1 && segs[i + 1]) return segs.slice(i + 1).join("/");
  return segs.slice(-2).join("/");
};

export function toolSummary(name, input = {}) {
  if (name === "WebSearch" && input.queries?.length) return input.queries.join(" · ");
  if (name === "WebFind")
    return [input.pattern || input.description, input.url].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
  if (toolName(name, input) === "WebBrowse")
    return input.description || "Web action details not reported by Codex";
  if (name === "Read") return shortPath(input.file_path) || "";
  return (
    input.command ||
    input.file_path ||
    input.path ||
    input.pattern ||
    input.url ||
    input.query ||
    input.description ||
    JSON.stringify(input).slice(0, 120)
  );
}

================
File: server/providers/claude-accounts.ts
================
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";

export type ClaudeAccount = "primary" | "secondary";

export function normalizeClaudeAccount(value?: string): ClaudeAccount {
  if (value === undefined || value === "primary") return "primary";
  if (value === "secondary") return "secondary";
  throw new Error("Unknown Claude account.");
}

export function claudeConfigDir(account?: string, env = process.env, home = os.homedir()): string {
  return normalizeClaudeAccount(account) === "secondary"
    ? path.join(home, ".claude-secondary")
    : env.CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR || path.join(home, ".claude");
}

export function claudeAccountReady(
  account?: string,
  env = process.env,
  home = os.homedir(),
): boolean {
  try {
    const selected = normalizeClaudeAccount(account);
    const config = claudeConfigDir(selected, env, home);
    const oauth = JSON.parse(
      fs.readFileSync(path.join(config, ".credentials.json"), "utf8"),
    ).claudeAiOauth;
    if (typeof oauth?.accessToken !== "string" || !oauth.accessToken.length) return false;
    const metadata = path.join(config, ".claude.json");
    const legacy =
      selected === "primary" && !env.CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR ? path.join(home, ".claude.json") : undefined;
    const file = fs.existsSync(metadata) ? metadata : legacy;
    const email =
      file && fs.existsSync(file)
        ? JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8")).oauthAccount?.emailAddress
        : undefined;
    const expected = selected === "secondary" ? "REDACTED_EMAIL" : "REDACTED_EMAIL";
    if (selected === "secondary" && typeof email !== "string") return false;
    if (typeof email === "string" && email.toLowerCase() !== expected) return false;
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export function requireClaudeAccount(account?: string): ClaudeAccount {
  const selected = normalizeClaudeAccount(account);
  if (selected === "secondary" && !claudeAccountReady(selected))
    throw new Error(
      "secondary Claude account is not signed in. Complete its separate Claude login first.",
    );
  return selected;
}

export const claudeUsageKey = (account?: string) =>
  normalizeClaudeAccount(account) === "secondary" ? "claude:secondary" : "claude";

export function claudeAccounts() {
  return [
    {
      id: "primary",
      label: "primary",
      email: "REDACTED_EMAIL",
      ready: claudeAccountReady("primary"),
    },
    {
      id: "secondary",
      label: "secondary",
      email: "REDACTED_EMAIL",
      ready: claudeAccountReady("secondary"),
    },
  ];
}

File: server/providers/claude.ts

import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { launch as sandboxLaunch } from "../automation/sandbox.ts";
import { headroom } from "../headroom.ts";
import { toolEnvFile } from "../tool-priority.ts";
import { threadDir } from "../uploads.ts";
import { personaFor, personaPath, stylePreamble } from "./brain.ts";
import { claudeConfigDir, requireClaudeAccount } from "./claude-accounts.ts";
import { consoleMcpServer } from "./console-mcp.mjs";
import { parseJson, spawnLines } from "./proc.ts";
import {
  type Agent,
  type AgentCtx,
  type Attachment,
  type Command,
  cleanQuestions,
  clip,
  type Provider,
  type QuestionReply,
  type Settings,
  type ThreadSpec,
} from "./types.ts";

// never let the subscription login be silently replaced by metered API billing
const SCRUBBED_ENV = [
  "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY",
  "ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN",
  "CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN",
  "CLAUDECODE",
];

// Lean context: every connected MCP server adds its tool names and usage instructions to every turn (about 4k tokens for the claude.ai
// connectors plus project servers). Threads load only the servers named in AGENT_CONSOLE_MCP (comma list from ~/.claude.json, default none:
// agents use the codegraph CLI, so no per-thread MCP server stays alive); AGENT_CONSOLE_MCP=all restores everything.
export function leanMcpArgs(): string[] {
  const keep = (process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_MCP ?? "")
    .split(",")
    .map((x) => x.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
  const consoleServer = consoleMcpServer();
  let servers: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  try {
    const all =
      JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(os.homedir(), ".claude.json"), "utf8")).mcpServers ?? {};
    servers = keep.includes("all")
      ? { ...all }
      : Object.fromEntries(keep.filter((k) => all[k]).map((k) => [k, all[k]]));
  } catch {}
  if (consoleServer) servers.agent_console = consoleServer;
  // always strict: a thread's tools array must not depend on account-level or project-level MCP config
  return ["--strict-mcp-config", "--mcp-config", JSON.stringify({ mcpServers: servers })];
}

export function normalizeCommands(raw: any[]): Command[] {
  return (raw ?? [])
    .map((c) => (typeof c === "string" ? { name: c } : c))
    .filter((c) => typeof c?.name === "string")
    .map((c) => ({
      name: c.name.replace(/^\/+/, ""),
      description: String(c.description ?? "").slice(0, 200),
      argumentHint: c.argumentHint || undefined,
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
}

// The SDK control protocol (`--permission-prompt-tool stdio`): the CLI asks its host before any tool that needs the user, as
//   {"type":"control_request","request_id":R,"request":{"subtype":"can_use_tool","tool_name":T,"input":{...},"tool_use_id":U}}
// and blocks until {"type":"control_response","response":{"subtype":"success","request_id":R,"response":{"behavior":"allow"|"deny",...}}}.
// Without a host, headless Claude drops AskUserQuestion from its tools altogether. AskUserQuestion becomes a question card; its
// answer goes back as updatedInput.answers {question text: "label, label"} (verified on CLI 2.1.301: the tool result reads
// `"Which color?"="Green"`). Every other prompt is denied, exactly as without a host, so the permission mode still decides.
interface PendingControl {
  input: any;
  questions: ReturnType<typeof cleanQuestions>;
}

interface ControlContext {
  pending: Map<string, PendingControl>;
  write: (msg: object) => void;
  emit: (event: any) => void;
  mode: string;
}

const AUTO_ALLOWED_TOOLS = [
  "mcp__agent_console__session_search",
  "mcp__agent_console__session_read",
  "mcp__agent_console__pi_research",
  "mcp__agent_console__cli_search",
  "mcp__agent_console__thread_list",
  "mcp__agent_console__thread_send",
];

function respondControl(ctx: ControlContext, requestId: string, response: object) {
  ctx.write({
    type: "control_response",
    response: { subtype: "success", request_id: requestId, response },
  });
}

function askControlQuestions(ctx: ControlContext, event: any, req: any) {
  const questions = cleanQuestions(
    req.input.questions.map((question: any, idx: number) => ({
      ...question,
      id: String(idx),
      allowOther: true,
    })),
  );
  if (!questions.length) {
    respondControl(ctx, event.request_id, {
      behavior: "deny",
      message: "No questions were provided. Continue with your best judgment.",
    });
    return;
  }
  ctx.pending.set(event.request_id, { input: req.input, questions });
  ctx.emit({
    type: "question",
    id: event.request_id,
    tool_use_id: req.tool_use_id,
    questions,
    ts: Date.now(),
  });
}

function handleControlRequest(ctx: ControlContext, event: any) {
  const req = event.request ?? {};
  if (req.subtype !== "can_use_tool") {
    ctx.write({
      type: "control_response",
      response: {
        subtype: "error",
        request_id: event.request_id,
        error: `Unsupported control request: ${req.subtype}`,
      },
    });
    return;
  }
  // Console tools are authenticated by the bridge; thread sends also require a live root tool call.
  // Allow only these exact names in Plan/acceptEdits; all other permission prompts retain their policy.
  const redactionAction = req.input?.action ?? "scan";
  const redactionAllowed =
    req.tool_name === "mcp__agent_console__session_redact" &&
    (["scan", "verify", "status"].includes(redactionAction) ||
      (redactionAction === "apply" && ctx.mode === "bypassPermissions"));
  if (AUTO_ALLOWED_TOOLS.includes(req.tool_name) || redactionAllowed) {
    respondControl(ctx, event.request_id, { behavior: "allow", updatedInput: req.input ?? {} });
    return;
  }
  if (req.tool_name === "AskUserQuestion" && Array.isArray(req.input?.questions)) {
    askControlQuestions(ctx, event, req);
    return;
  }
  respondControl(ctx, event.request_id, {
    behavior: "deny",
    message: `Permission for ${req.tool_name} was not granted: this console does not approve tool prompts in ${ctx.mode} mode. Continue without it or ask the user to switch the thread to Bypass.`,
  });
}

function handleControlMessage(ctx: ControlContext, event: any): boolean {
  if (event?.type === "control_cancel_request") {
    if (ctx.pending.delete(event.request_id))
      ctx.emit({ type: "question_answer", id: event.request_id, cancelled: true });
    return true;
  }
  if (event?.type === "control_response") return true; // replies to host requests; we send none
  if (event?.type !== "control_request") return false;
  handleControlRequest(ctx, event);
  return true;
}

function answerControl(ctx: ControlContext, questionId: string, reply: QuestionReply): boolean {
  const entry = ctx.pending.get(questionId);
  if (!entry) throw new Error("This question is no longer waiting for an answer.");
  if (reply.skip || reply.note !== undefined) {
    const message =
      reply.note !== undefined
        ? `The user did not pick an option and replied in chat instead: ${reply.note}`
        : "The user skipped these questions without answering. Continue with your best judgment and do not ask the same questions again.";
    respondControl(ctx, questionId, { behavior: "deny", message });
    ctx.pending.delete(questionId);
    return reply.note !== undefined;
  }
  const answers: Record<string, string> = {};
  entry.questions.forEach((question, idx) => {
    const picked = (reply.answers[question.id] ?? [])
      .map((part) => String(part).trim())
      .filter(Boolean);
    if (picked.length)
      answers[String(entry.input.questions[idx]?.question ?? question.question)] =
        picked.join(", ");
  });
  respondControl(ctx, questionId, { behavior: "allow", updatedInput: { ...entry.input, answers } });
  ctx.pending.delete(questionId);
  return false;
}

// The SDK control protocol (`--permission-prompt-tool stdio`): the CLI asks its host before any tool that needs the user, as
//   {"type":"control_request","request_id":R,"request":{"subtype":"can_use_tool","tool_name":T,"input":{...},"tool_use_id":U}}
// and blocks until {"type":"control_response","response":{"subtype":"success","request_id":R,"response":{"behavior":"allow"|"deny",...}}}.
// Without a host, headless Claude drops AskUserQuestion from its tools altogether. AskUserQuestion becomes a question card; its
// answer goes back as updatedInput.answers {question text: "label, label"} (verified on CLI 2.1.301: the tool result reads
// `"Which color?"="Green"`). Every other prompt is denied, exactly as without a host, so the permission mode still decides.
export function claudeControl(
  write: (msg: object) => void,
  emit: (event: any) => void,
  mode: string,
) {
  const ctx: ControlContext = { pending: new Map(), write, emit, mode };
  return {
    pending: ctx.pending,
    cancelAll() {
      for (const questionId of ctx.pending.keys())
        emit({ type: "question_answer", id: questionId, cancelled: true });
      ctx.pending.clear();
    },
    // true when the line was a control message (never shown or logged)
    handle(event: any): boolean {
      return handleControlMessage(ctx, event);
    },
    answer(questionId: string, reply: QuestionReply): boolean {
      return answerControl(ctx, questionId, reply);
    },
  };
}

// Empty string disables the advisor in the installed CLI settings schema; omitted inherits user/project settings.
export function claudeAdvisorArgs(advisor?: string): string[] {
  if (advisor === "off") return ["--settings", JSON.stringify({ advisorModel: "" })];
  return advisor && advisor !== "default" ? ["--advisor", advisor] : [];
}

// Console schedules idle compaction itself. Keep the CLI's independent idle timer off,
// including when a thread opts out, while preserving native context-limit compaction.
export function claudeConsoleSettingsArgs(advisor?: string): string[] {
  const args = claudeAdvisorArgs(advisor);
  if (args[0] === "--settings") {
    return ["--settings", JSON.stringify({ ...JSON.parse(args[1]), idleCompaction: false })];
  }
  return ["--settings", JSON.stringify({ idleCompaction: false }), ...args];
}

// A nested console can inherit its parent's local proxy URL. Never keep that
// managed URL when this console has disabled the proxy or cannot reach it.
export function claudeRoutingEnv(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv): NodeJS.ProcessEnv {
  const routed = { ...env };
  if (routed.ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL?.replace(/\/+$/, "") === headroom.state().url)
    delete routed.ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL;
  const proxy = headroom.url();
  if (proxy && !routed.ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL) routed.ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL = proxy;
  return routed;
}

interface ClaudeRun {
  proc: ReturnType<typeof spawnLines> | undefined;
  requests: number;
}

export function claudeStartArgs(thread: ThreadSpec): string[] {
  const args = [
    "-p",
    "--input-format",
    "stream-json",
    "--output-format",
    "stream-json",
    "--verbose",
    "--include-partial-messages",
    "--permission-mode",
    thread.mode,
    "--permission-prompt-tool",
    "stdio", // host answers prompts over stdin/stdout: enables AskUserQuestion (see claudeControl)
    "--add-dir",
    threadDir(thread.id), // attachments live outside the project; without this plan/accept-edits could not read them
    "--model",
    thread.model,
  ];
  if (thread.effort && thread.effort !== "default") args.push("--effort", thread.effort);
  args.push(...claudeConsoleSettingsArgs(thread.advisor));
  args.push(...leanMcpArgs());
  const persona = personaFor(thread);
  if (fs.existsSync(personaPath(persona))) args.push("--agent", persona); // replaces the default system prompt; the persona carries the role rules
  const style = stylePreamble();
  if (style) args.push("--append-system-prompt", style); // brain index arrives via the user hook
  fs.mkdirSync(threadDir(thread.id), { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  if (thread.sessionId) args.push("--resume", thread.sessionId);
  else if (thread.forkFrom) args.push("--resume", thread.forkFrom, "--fork-session"); // a forked thread: continue a copy of the parent session
  return args;
}

function claudeStartEnv(thread: ThreadSpec, account: ReturnType<typeof requireClaudeAccount>) {
  const env = claudeRoutingEnv(process.env);
  env.AGENT_CONSOLE_THREAD = thread.id;
  env.ENABLE_TOOL_SEARCH = "false"; // deferred tool loading changes the tools array mid-thread and busts the prompt cache
  const toolEnv = toolEnvFile();
  if (toolEnv && !env.CLAUDE_ENV_FILE) env.CLAUDE_ENV_FILE = toolEnv; // Bash commands start at the lower tool priority
  if (account === "secondary") env.CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR = claudeConfigDir(account);
  for (const name of SCRUBBED_ENV) delete env[name];
  return env;
}

function clipToolResults(event: any) {
  delete event.tool_use_result; // duplicate of message.content, can be huge
  for (const block of event.message?.content ?? []) {
    if (block?.type === "tool_result" && typeof block.content === "string")
      block.content = clip(block.content);
  }
}

// How full the context is: the last main-thread request's whole input (cached + new) plus what it produced. Subagents have their own.
function emitContextUsage(ctx: AgentCtx, event: any) {
  const usage = event.message?.usage;
  const used =
    (usage?.input_tokens ?? 0) +
    (usage?.cache_creation_input_tokens ?? 0) +
    (usage?.cache_read_input_tokens ?? 0) +
    (usage?.output_tokens ?? 0);
  if (used > 0) ctx.event({ type: "context_usage", used });
}

function emitContextWindow(ctx: AgentCtx, event: any, model: string) {
  const wins = Object.entries<any>(event.modelUsage);
  const mine = wins.find(([name]) => name.includes(model)) ?? wins[0];
  if (mine?.[1]?.contextWindow) ctx.event({ type: "context_usage", max: mine[1].contextWindow });
}

function handleClaudeLine(
  thread: ThreadSpec,
  ctx: AgentCtx,
  control: ReturnType<typeof claudeControl>,
  line: string,
) {
  const event = parseJson(line);
  if (!event) return;
  if (control.handle(event)) return;
  if (event.type === "result") control.cancelAll();
  if (event.type === "user") clipToolResults(event);
  if (event.type === "system" && event.subtype === "init" && Array.isArray(event.slash_commands))
    ctx.commands(normalizeCommands(event.slash_commands));
  if (event.type === "system" && event.subtype === "commands_changed")
    return ctx.commands(normalizeCommands(event.commands));
  if (event.type === "assistant" && !event.parent_tool_use_id) emitContextUsage(ctx, event);
  if (event.type === "result" && event.modelUsage) emitContextWindow(ctx, event, thread.model);
  ctx.event(event);
}

// plain string when there is nothing to attach; otherwise the Messages API block form
function writeClaudeUser(proc: NonNullable<ClaudeRun["proc"]>, text: string, images: Attachment[]) {
  const content = images.length
    ? [
        ...images.map((image) => ({
          type: "image",
          source: {
            type: "base64",
            media_type: image.media_type,
            data: fs.readFileSync(image.path).toString("base64"),
          },
        })),
        ...(text.trim() ? [{ type: "text", text }] : []),
      ]
    : text;
  proc.write(JSON.stringify({ type: "user", message: { role: "user", content } }));
}

function claudeEffortLevel(effort?: string) {
  return !!effort && effort !== "default" && effort !== "ultracode"; // default/ultracode change flags, not a level
}

function configureClaude(run: ClaudeRun, from: Settings, target: Settings): boolean {
  // the CLI refuses to enter bypassPermissions unless launched in it ("bypass_not_launched"): restart for that
  if (target.mode === "bypassPermissions" && from.mode !== target.mode) return false;
  if (
    from.advisor !== target.advisor ||
    (from.effort !== target.effort &&
      !(claudeEffortLevel(from.effort) && claudeEffortLevel(target.effort)))
  )
    return false;
  const ask = (request: object) =>
    run.proc!.write(
      JSON.stringify({
        type: "control_request",
        request_id: `cfg-${++run.requests}`,
        request,
      }),
    );
  if (from.model !== target.model) ask({ subtype: "set_model", model: target.model });
  if (from.mode !== target.mode) ask({ subtype: "set_permission_mode", mode: target.mode });
  if (from.effort !== target.effort)
    ask({ subtype: "apply_flag_settings", settings: { effortLevel: target.effort } });
  return true;
}

export const claude: Provider = {
  id: "claude",
  label: "Claude Code",
  modes: ["plan", "acceptEdits", "bypassPermissions"],
  defaultMode: "acceptEdits",
  advisors: ["off", "opus", "fable"],
  steer: true,
  models: () => ["sonnet", "opus", "haiku"],
  defaultModel: () => "sonnet",
  modelDetails: () =>
    Object.fromEntries(
      ["sonnet", "opus", "haiku"].map((id) => [
        id,
        {
          label: id[0].toUpperCase() + id.slice(1),
          efforts: id === "haiku" ? [] : ["low", "medium", "high", "xhigh", "max", "ultracode"],
        },
      ]),
    ),
  status: () => ({ ok: true }),
  start(t: ThreadSpec, ctx: AgentCtx): Agent {
    const account = requireClaudeAccount(t.claudeAccount);
    const args = claudeStartArgs(t);
    const env = claudeStartEnv(t, account);
    const run: ClaudeRun = { proc: undefined, requests: 0 };
    const control = claudeControl((msg) => run.proc!.write(JSON.stringify(msg)), ctx.event, t.mode);
    const launch = sandboxLaunch(t, "claude", args, env);
    const proc = spawnLines(
      "claude",
      launch.command,
      launch.args,
      { cwd: t.cwd, env: launch.env },
      {
        ...ctx,
        exited(info) {
          control.cancelAll();
          ctx.exited(info);
        },
      },
      (line) => handleClaudeLine(t, ctx, control, line),
    );
    run.proc = proc;
    return {
      get alive() {
        return proc.alive();
      },
      send: (text, images = []) => writeClaudeUser(proc, text, images),
      compact: (instructions = "") =>
        writeClaudeUser(proc, `/compact${instructions ? " " + instructions : ""}`, []),
      // Claude takes a user message mid-turn on stdin and folds it in after the running tool call (verified: it dropped the
      // remaining commands and answered the new message within the same turn).
      steer: (text, images = []) => writeClaudeUser(proc, text, images),
      answer: (id, reply) => control.answer(id, reply),
      // Verified on CLI 2.1.302: `interrupt` ends the turn with a zero-usage error result and the process stays up;
      // set_model, set_permission_mode (except into bypass) and apply_flag_settings {effortLevel} change the next request, no model call.
      interrupt: () => {
        control.cancelAll();
        proc.write(
          JSON.stringify({
            type: "control_request",
            request_id: `int-${++run.requests}`,
            request: { subtype: "interrupt" },
          }),
        );
      },
      configure: (from, target) => configureClaude(run, from, target),
      abort: () => {
        control.cancelAll();
        proc.abort();
      },
      kill: () => {
        control.cancelAll();
        proc.kill();
      },
    };
  },
};

const ITEM_TYPES: Record<string, string> = {
  agentMessage: "agent_message",
  commandExecution: "command_execution",
  fileChange: "file_change",
  mcpToolCall: "mcp_tool_call",
  webSearch: "web_search",
};

// App-server thread item -> the exec-style row codexTranslator understands; undefined when the item has no visible row.
export function adaptItem(item: any, done: boolean): any {
  if (item.type === "reasoning")
    return done
      ? {
          type: "item.completed",
          item: { ...item, text: [...(item.summary || []), ...(item.content || [])].join("\n") },
        }
      : undefined;
  if (!ITEM_TYPES[item.type]) return undefined;
  return {
    type: done ? "item.completed" : "item.started",
    item: {
      ...item,
      type: ITEM_TYPES[item.type],
      aggregated_output: item.aggregatedOutput,
      exit_code: item.exitCode,
      ...(item.changes
        ? {
            changes: item.changes.map((change: any) => ({
              ...change,
              kind: change.kind?.type || change.kind,
            })),
          }
        : {}),
    },
  };
}

================
File: server/providers/codex-app-questions.ts
================
import { cleanQuestions, type Question, type QuestionReply } from "./types.ts";

export interface CodexQuestionCard {
  questions: Question[];
  consumesNote: boolean;
  reply(answer: QuestionReply): any;
}

const FORM_FIELD_TYPES = ["string", "boolean", "number", "integer", "array"];

// Codex asks the user through app-server server requests (exec mode refuses: "request_user_input not supported in exec mode"):
//   item/tool/requestUserInput   {questions:[{id,header,question,isOther,isSecret,options:[{label,description}]|null}]}
//                                -> {answers:{[id]:{answers:[...]}}}; no answers = "continue with best judgment"
//   mcpServer/elicitation/request (mode "form") an MCP server's JSON-schema form -> {action:"accept"|"decline", content}
// request_user_input is offered to the model in Plan collaboration mode, or in default mode behind the
// default_mode_request_user_input feature, which we enable (AGENT_CONSOLE_CODEX_ASK=0 turns it off). Returns undefined for
// requests that cannot be shown as a question card.
export function codexQuestion(method: string, params: any): CodexQuestionCard | undefined {
  if (method === "item/tool/requestUserInput" && Array.isArray(params?.questions))
    return userInputCard(params.questions);
  if (
    method === "mcpServer/elicitation/request" &&
    ["form", "openai/form"].includes(params?.mode) &&
    params.requestedSchema?.properties
  )
    return elicitationCard(params);
  return undefined;
}

function userInputCard(rawQuestions: any[]): CodexQuestionCard {
  const questions = cleanQuestions(
    rawQuestions.map((raw: any) => ({
      ...raw,
      options: raw.options ?? [],
      allowOther: raw.isOther || !raw.options?.length,
      secret: raw.isSecret,
    })),
  );
  return {
    questions,
    consumesNote: true,
    reply: (answer) => userInputReply(questions, answer),
  };
}

function userInputReply(questions: Question[], reply: QuestionReply) {
  if (reply.note !== undefined)
    return { answers: questions[0] ? { [questions[0].id]: { answers: [reply.note] } } : {} };
  if (reply.skip) return { answers: {} };
  return {
    answers: Object.fromEntries(
      questions
        .map((question) => [
          question.id,
          { answers: (reply.answers[question.id] ?? []).map(String).filter(Boolean) },
        ])
        .filter(([, entry]: any) => entry.answers.length),
    ),
  };
}

function enumValues(schema: any): string[] {
  return (
    schema?.enum ??
    schema?.oneOf?.map((option: any) => option.const) ??
    schema?.anyOf?.map((option: any) => option.const) ??
    []
  );
}

// Nested objects and large forms need a dedicated form renderer. Decline
// unsupported schemas instead of silently truncating or changing their types.
function unsupportedField(schema: any): boolean {
  if (!schema || !FORM_FIELD_TYPES.includes(schema.type)) return true;
  const isArray = schema.type === "array";
  if (isArray && schema.items?.type !== "string" && !Array.isArray(schema.items?.enum)) return true;
  return enumValues(isArray ? schema.items : schema).length > 20;
}

function elicitationCard(params: any): CodexQuestionCard | undefined {
  const fields = Object.entries<any>(params.requestedSchema.properties);
  const required = new Set<string>(params.requestedSchema.required ?? []);
  if (!fields.length || fields.length > 10 || fields.some(([, schema]) => unsupportedField(schema)))
    return undefined;
  const questions = cleanQuestions(
    fields.map(([key, schema], index) =>
      fieldQuestion(params, key, schema, index, required.has(key)),
    ),
  );
  return {
    questions,
    consumesNote: false,
    reply: (answer) => elicitationReply(fields, required, answer),
  };
}

function fieldQuestion(params: any, key: string, schema: any, index: number, isRequired: boolean) {
  const multi = schema.type === "array";
  const values =
    schema.type === "boolean" ? ["Yes", "No"] : enumValues(multi ? schema.items : schema);
  const first = index === 0;
  return {
    id: key,
    header: first ? params.serverName : undefined,
    question:
      [first ? params.message : "", schema.title || schema.description || key]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join("\n\n") + (isRequired ? "" : " (optional)"),
    options: values.map((value: string) => ({ label: String(value) })),
    multiSelect: multi,
    allowOther: !values.length,
  };
}

function elicitationReply(fields: [string, any][], required: Set<string>, reply: QuestionReply) {
  if (reply.skip || reply.note !== undefined)
    return { action: "decline", content: null, _meta: null };
  const content: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (const [key, schema] of fields) {
    const values = (reply.answers[key] ?? []).map(String).filter(Boolean);
    if (!values.length) {
      if (required.has(key)) throw new Error(`Please answer ${schema.title || key}.`);
      continue;
    }
    validateField(key, schema, values[0]);
    content[key] = fieldValue(schema, values);
  }
  return { action: "accept", content, _meta: null };
}

function validateField(key: string, schema: any, first: string): void {
  const label = schema.title || key;
  const numeric = schema.type === "number" || schema.type === "integer";
  if (
    numeric &&
    (!Number.isFinite(Number(first)) ||
      (schema.type === "integer" && !Number.isInteger(Number(first))))
  )
    throw new Error(`Enter a valid ${schema.type} for ${label}.`);
  if (schema.type === "boolean" && !["Yes", "No"].includes(first))
    throw new Error(`Choose Yes or No for ${label}.`);
}

function fieldValue(schema: any, values: string[]): unknown {
  if (schema.type === "array") return values;
  if (schema.type === "boolean") return values[0] === "Yes";
  if (schema.type === "number" || schema.type === "integer") return Number(values[0]);
  return values[0];
}

================
File: server/providers/codex-app-rpc.ts
================
import type { ExitInfo } from "./types.ts";

interface PendingRequest {
  resolve: (value: any) => void;
  reject: (error: Error) => void;
  timer: NodeJS.Timeout;
}

// JSON-RPC client half of the app-server connection: numbered requests, their pending replies and the fixed timeout.
export class CodexRpc {
  open = true;
  private serial = 0;
  private readonly pending = new Map<number, PendingRequest>();
  private readonly writeLine: (line: string) => void;

  constructor(writeLine: (line: string) => void) {
    this.writeLine = writeLine;
  }

  request(method: string, params: object): Promise<any> {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      if (!this.open) {
        reject(new Error("Codex connection closed."));
        return;
      }
      const requestId = ++this.serial;
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.pending.delete(requestId);
        reject(new Error(`Codex ${method} timed out.`));
      }, 30_000);
      this.pending.set(requestId, { resolve, reject, timer });
      this.writeLine(JSON.stringify({ id: requestId, method, params }));
    });
  }

  // A response line (id, no method) settles its waiting request; unknown ids are ignored.
  settle(response: any): void {
    const wait = this.pending.get(response.id);
    if (!wait) return;
    this.pending.delete(response.id);
    clearTimeout(wait.timer);
    if (response.error) wait.reject(new Error(response.error.message || "Codex request rejected."));
    else wait.resolve(response.result);
  }

  rejectAll(info: ExitInfo): void {
    for (const wait of this.pending.values()) {
      clearTimeout(wait.timer);
      wait.reject(new Error(`Codex connection closed (${info.code ?? info.signal}).`));
    }
    this.pending.clear();
  }
}

File: server/providers/codex-app-session.ts
