import type { Command } from "./providers/types.ts";

export const CONSOLE_COMMANDS: Command[] = [
  { name: "compact", description: "Compact the agent's context; keep the visible conversation." },
  { name: "help", description: "Show the commands available for this agent." },
  { name: "status", description: "Show the agent, model, effort and current context." },
  { name: "model", description: "Choose a model for this thread.", argumentHint: "[model]" },
  {
    name: "effort",
    description: "Choose reasoning effort for this thread.",
    argumentHint: "[level]",
  },
  { name: "stop", description: "Stop the running operation and clear queued messages." },
];
export function parseCommand(text: string) {
  const m = /^\/([a-z][\w:-]*)(?:\s+([\s\S]*))?$/i.exec(text.trim());
  return m ? { name: m[1].toLowerCase(), args: (m[2] || "").trim() } : undefined;
}
export function commandList(native: Command[] = []): Command[] {
  return [
    ...new Map(
      [...native.map((c) => ({ ...c, name: c.name.replace(/^\/+/, "") })), ...CONSOLE_COMMANDS].map(
        (c) => [c.name, c],
      ),
    ).values(),
  ];
}

================
File: server/console-cli-input.ts
================
import type { TaskInput } from "./schedules.ts";

const TEXT_FIELDS = [
  "title",
  "prompt",
  "provider",
  "model",
  "effort",
  "mode",
  "cwd",
  "repeat",
  "time",
  "tz",
];
const BOOLEAN_FIELDS = ["enabled", "saveOutput"];
const PATCH_FIELDS = [...TEXT_FIELDS, ...BOOLEAN_FIELDS, "day", "limitMin"];

export function positiveInteger(value: unknown, label: string, maximum: number): number {
  const number = typeof value === "string" && /^\d+$/.test(value) ? Number(value) : value;
  if (typeof number !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(number) || number < 1 || number > maximum)
    throw new Error(`${label} must be an integer from 1 to ${maximum}.`);
  return number;
}

function validatePatchField(key: string, value: unknown): void {
  if (!PATCH_FIELDS.includes(key)) throw new Error(`Unknown config field: ${key}.`);
  if (TEXT_FIELDS.includes(key) && (typeof value !== "string" || !value.trim()))
    throw new Error(`${key} must be a nonempty string.`);
  if (BOOLEAN_FIELDS.includes(key) && typeof value !== "boolean")
    throw new Error(`${key} must be a boolean.`);
  if (key === "limitMin") {
    if (typeof value !== "number") throw new Error("limitMin must be a number.");
    positiveInteger(value, "limitMin", 1440);
  }
  if (
    key === "day" &&
    !(
      typeof value === "string" ||
      (Number.isInteger(value) && Number(value) >= 0 && Number(value) <= 6)
    )
  )
    throw new Error("day must be a weekday name or an integer from 0 to 6.");
}

function validateScheduleFields(patch: Record<string, unknown>): void {
  if (
    patch.mode !== undefined &&
    !["plan", "acceptEdits", "bypassPermissions"].includes(String(patch.mode))
  )
    throw new Error("mode must be plan, acceptEdits or bypassPermissions.");
  if (
    patch.repeat !== undefined &&
    !["manual", "hourly", "daily", "weekdays", "weekly"].includes(String(patch.repeat))
  )
    throw new Error("repeat must be manual, hourly, daily, weekdays or weekly.");
  if (patch.time !== undefined && !/^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/.test(String(patch.time)))
    throw new Error("time must be HH:MM, 24-hour.");
  if (
    typeof patch.day === "string" &&
    !/^(?:[0-6]|sun(?:day)?|mon(?:day)?|tue(?:sday)?|wed(?:nesday)?|thu(?:rsday)?|fri(?:day)?|sat(?:urday)?)$/i.test(
      patch.day,
    )
  )
    throw new Error("day must be a weekday name or 0–6.");
  if (patch.tz !== undefined) {
    try {
      new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en", { timeZone: String(patch.tz) });
    } catch {
      throw new Error("tz must be a valid IANA timezone.");
    }
  }
}

export function configPatch(value: unknown): TaskInput {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value))
    throw new Error("Config patch must be a JSON object.");
  const patch = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (!Object.keys(patch).length) throw new Error("Config patch must contain at least one field.");
  for (const [key, field] of Object.entries(patch)) validatePatchField(key, field);
  validateScheduleFields(patch);
  return patch as TaskInput;
}

================
File: server/console-cli-tools.ts
================
import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { configPatch, positiveInteger } from "./console-cli-input.ts";
import type { Ideas } from "./ideas.ts";
import { PROVIDERS } from "./providers/index.ts";
import type { Run, Schedules, Task, TaskInput } from "./schedules.ts";
import type { SessionSearch } from "./session-search.ts";

const OPERATIONS = new Set([
  "idea_list",
  "idea_read",
  "schedule_config_list",
  "schedule_get",
  "schedule_runs",
  "schedule_report",
  "schedule_transcript",
  "schedule_config_update",
]);
export const isConsoleCliTool = (name: string): boolean => OPERATIONS.has(name);
type Services = {
  ideas: Pick<Ideas, "list" | "read">;
  schedules: Pick<Schedules, "list" | "get" | "update" | "outputs">;
  sessionSearch: Pick<SessionSearch, "read">;
};

function pickTask(schedules: Services["schedules"], identifier: unknown): Task {
  if (typeof identifier !== "string" || !identifier.trim())
    throw new Error("Give a task id or exact title.");
  const exact = schedules.get(identifier);
  if (exact) return exact;
  const matches = schedules
    .list()
    .filter((task) => task.title.toLowerCase() === identifier.toLowerCase());
  if (matches.length > 1)
    throw new Error("Ambiguous task title. Use its id from scheduled-tasks list.");
  if (!matches.length) throw new Error("Unknown scheduled task. Use scheduled-tasks list.");
  return matches[0];
}

function taskConfig(task: Task) {
  const { runs: _runs, ...configuration } = task;
  return configuration;
}

function pickRun(task: Task, identifier: unknown, report: boolean): Run {
  if (identifier !== undefined && typeof identifier !== "string")
    throw new Error("run must be a run id.");
  const run =
    identifier === undefined
      ? task.runs.findLast((candidate) => (report ? !!candidate.output : !!candidate.thread))
      : task.runs.find((candidate) => candidate.id === identifier);
  if (!run)
    throw new Error(identifier ? "Unknown run for this task." : "No matching run for this task.");
  return run;
}

function readReport(outputs: string, run: Run): string {
  if (!run.output) throw new Error("This run has no saved report.");
  const relative = path.relative(outputs, run.output.path);
  if (!relative || relative.startsWith("..") || path.isAbsolute(relative))
    throw new Error("Report path is outside the outputs repository.");
  if (run.output.commit) {
    if (!/^[a-f0-9]{7,40}$/.test(run.output.commit))
      throw new Error("Invalid saved report commit.");
    try {
      return execFileSync(
        "git",
        ["-C", outputs, "show", `${run.output.commit}:${relative.split(path.sep).join("/")}`],
        {
          encoding: "utf8",
          maxBuffer: 600_000,
          timeout: 10_000,
          stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"],
        },
      );
    } catch {
      throw new Error("Saved report commit is unavailable.");
    }
  }
  if (fs.statSync(run.output.path).size > 600_000) throw new Error("Saved report is too large.");
  return fs.readFileSync(run.output.path, "utf8");
}

function validateAgentPatch(patch: TaskInput, task: Task): void {
  const provider = PROVIDERS.get(patch.provider ?? task.provider);
  if (!provider) throw new Error("Unknown agent.");
  if (patch.model !== undefined && !provider.models().includes(patch.model))
    throw new Error("Unknown model for this agent.");
  if (patch.mode !== undefined && !provider.modes.includes(patch.mode))
    throw new Error("Unknown mode for this agent.");
  const model =
    patch.model ??
    (patch.provider && patch.provider !== task.provider ? provider.defaultModel() : task.model);
  if (
    patch.effort !== undefined &&
    patch.effort !== "default" &&
    !provider.modelDetails?.()[model]?.efforts.includes(patch.effort)
  )
    throw new Error("Unknown effort for this model.");
}

export async function consoleCliTool(
  name: string,
  input: Record<string, unknown>,
  services: Services,
): Promise<unknown> {
  if (!input || typeof input !== "object" || Array.isArray(input))
    throw new Error("Input must be an object.");
  if (name === "idea_list") return services.ideas.list();
  if (name === "idea_read") {
    if (typeof input.slug !== "string") throw new Error("Give an idea slug.");
    return services.ideas.read(input.slug);
  }
  if (name === "schedule_config_list") return { tasks: services.schedules.list().map(taskConfig) };
  const task = pickTask(services.schedules, input.id);
  return taskOperation(name, input, task, services);
}

async function taskOperation(
  name: string,
  input: Record<string, unknown>,
  task: Task,
  services: Services,
): Promise<unknown> {
  switch (name) {
    case "schedule_get":
      return { task: taskConfig(task) };
    case "schedule_runs":
      return { task: task.id, runs: task.runs };
    case "schedule_config_update": {
      const patch = configPatch(input.patch);
      validateAgentPatch(patch, task);
      return { updated: taskConfig(services.schedules.update(task.id, patch)) };
    }
    case "schedule_report": {
      const run = pickRun(task, input.run, true);
      return {
        task: task.id,
        run: run.id,
        output: run.output,
        markdown: readReport(services.schedules.outputs, run),
      };
    }
    case "schedule_transcript": {
      const run = pickRun(task, input.run, false);
      if (!run.thread) throw new Error("This run has no transcript.");
      const start =
        input.start === undefined
          ? 1
          : positiveInteger(input.start, "start", Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER);
      const limit = input.limit === undefined ? 30 : positiveInteger(input.limit, "limit", 100);
      return {
        task: task.id,
        run: run.id,
        ...(await services.sessionSearch.read({ thread: run.thread, start, limit })),
      };
    }
    default:
      throw new Error(`Unknown CLI operation ${name}.`);
  }
}

================
File: server/cwd-hints.ts
================
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { DATA_DIR } from "./auth.ts";

const TAIL = 512 * 1024;
const within = (root: string, dir: string) => dir === root || dir.startsWith(root + path.sep);
const expand = (p: string) =>
  p === "~" ? os.homedir() : p.startsWith("~/") ? path.join(os.homedir(), p.slice(2)) : p;

/** Last 512 KiB of a thread's event log; `offset` > 0 means the first line may be cut. */
export function readLogTail(threadId: string): { text: string; offset: number } | null {
  try {
    const file = path.join(DATA_DIR, "logs", `${threadId}.jsonl`);
    const size = fs.statSync(file).size,
      fd = fs.openSync(file, "r");
    try {
      const buf = Buffer.alloc(Math.min(size, TAIL));
      const offset = size - buf.length;
      fs.readSync(fd, buf, 0, buf.length, offset);
      return { text: buf.toString("utf8"), offset };
    } finally {
      fs.closeSync(fd);
    }
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

const TEXT_PATH = /(?<![\w.~/-])(?:~|\/(?:home|tmp)\b)[^\s"'`;|&()<>[\]*]*\.\w+/g;
const CD_CMD = /\bcd\s+(?:"([^"]+)"|'([^']+)'|([^\s;&|`()]+))/g;
const ABS_IN_CMD = /(?:~|\/(?:home|tmp)\b)\/[^\s"'`;|&)<>]+/g;

function parseLine(line: string): any {
  if (!line.includes('"tool_use"') && !line.includes('"text"')) return null;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(line);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function commandDirs(command: string, base: string, add: (target: string) => void) {
  let cur = base;
  for (const match of command.matchAll(CD_CMD)) {
    cur = path.resolve(cur, expand(match[1] ?? match[2] ?? match[3]));
    add(cur);
  }
  for (const match of command.matchAll(ABS_IN_CMD)) add(expand(match[0]));
}

function toolInputDirs(input: any, cwd: string, add: (target: string) => void) {
  const workdir = input.workdir ?? input.cwd;
  const base = typeof workdir === "string" ? path.resolve(cwd, expand(workdir)) : cwd;
  if (base !== cwd) add(base);
  for (const key of ["file_path", "path", "notebook_path"])
    if (typeof input[key] === "string" && path.isAbsolute(expand(input[key])))
      add(expand(input[key]));
  for (const change of Array.isArray(input.changes) ? input.changes : []) {
    if (typeof change?.path === "string") add(path.resolve(base, expand(change.path)));
  }
  const command = input.command ?? input.cmd;
  if (typeof command === "string") commandDirs(command, base, add);
}

function blockDirs(event: any, block: any, cwd: string, add: (target: string) => void) {
  if (block?.type === "text" && event.type === "assistant" && typeof block.text === "string") {
    for (const match of block.text.matchAll(TEXT_PATH)) add(expand(match[0]));
    return;
  }
  if (block?.type === "tool_use" && block.input) toolInputDirs(block.input, cwd, add);
}

/**
 * Folders a thread has been working in, newest first, read from its shared Claude/Codex/Pi event log:
 * shell working directories and `cd` targets in Bash calls,
 * plus every folder from the thread cwd down to any absolute path a tool call touched. Lets the viewer resolve
 * a relative citation like `public/render.js` that the agent wrote after `cd agent-console`.
 * Folders outside cwd (a screenshot in /tmp/shots) count only themselves, and paths quoted in replies count too,
 * so a bare `after-open.png` finds the file the agent named earlier. The viewer still applies its own scope rules.
 */
export function contextDirs(threadId: string, cwd: string): string[] {
  const tail = readLogTail(threadId);
  if (!tail) return [];
  const found: string[] = [];
  const add = (target: string) => {
    let dir = path.resolve(target);
    if (/\.[\w-]+$/.test(path.basename(dir))) dir = path.dirname(dir);
    if (!within(cwd, dir)) return void found.push(dir);
    for (; within(cwd, dir) && dir !== cwd; dir = path.dirname(dir)) found.push(dir);
  };
  // discard only a potentially cut line
  for (const line of tail.text.split("\n").slice(tail.offset ? 1 : 0)) {
    const event = parseLine(line);
    for (const block of event?.message?.content || []) blockDirs(event, block, cwd, add);
  }
  return [...new Set(found.reverse())].slice(0, 60);
}

================
File: server/export.ts
================
import { toolName, toolSummary } from "../public/tool-display.js";
// Turns a thread's event log into readable text: for Download / Share (Markdown) and for seeding a forked thread whose agent has
// no native fork (the earlier conversation is replayed as a transcript in its first prompt).

export interface Turn {
  role: "user" | "agent";
  text: string;
  tool?: boolean; // a tool call or error line, not words the agent said
}

const oneLine = (s: string, n = 160) => {
  const t = s.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  return t.length > n ? t.slice(0, n - 1) + "…" : t;
};

type TurnContext = {
  labels: Map<string, string>;
  updates: Map<string, any>;
  out: Turn[];
  tools: boolean;
};
function pushTurn(context: TurnContext, role: Turn["role"], text: string, tool = false) {
  if (text.trim()) context.out.push({ role, text, ...(tool ? { tool: true } : {}) });
}
function userInputText(event: any) {
  const names = (event.attachments ?? []).map((attachment: any) => attachment.name);
  return [event.text ?? "", ...(names.length ? [`(attached: ${names.join(", ")})`] : [])]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join("\n");
}
function answerText(event: any) {
  if (event.skip) return "Skipped these questions; continue with your best judgment.";
  return (
    event.note ??
    Object.values<string[]>(event.answers ?? {})
      .map((answer) => answer.join(", "))
      .join("\n")
  );
}
function toolCallText(context: TurnContext, block: any) {
  const what =
    context.labels.get(block.id) ||
    block.input?.description ||
    toolSummary(block.name, block.input || {});
  return `${toolName(block.name, block.input)}${what && what !== "{}" ? `: ${oneLine(String(what))}` : ""}`;
}
function pushAssistantTurns(context: TurnContext, event: any) {
  for (const original of event.message?.content ?? []) {
    const update = original.type === "tool_use" && context.updates.get(original.id);
    const block = update ? { ...original, name: update.name, input: update.input } : original;
    if (block.type === "text") pushTurn(context, "agent", block.text);
    else if (block.type === "tool_use" && context.tools)
      pushTurn(context, "agent", toolCallText(context, block), true);
  }
}
function pushEventTurns(context: TurnContext, event: any) {
  switch (event?.type) {
    case "user_input":
      if (!event.auto && !event.questionAnswer) pushTurn(context, "user", userInputText(event));
      return;
    case "question":
      for (const question of event.questions ?? []) pushTurn(context, "agent", question.question);
      return;
    case "question_answer":
      if (!event.cancelled) pushTurn(context, "user", answerText(event));
      return;
    case "assistant":
      pushAssistantTurns(context, event);
      return;
    case "console_error":
      if (context.tools)
        pushTurn(context, "agent", `Error: ${oneLine(String(event.text), 300)}`, true);
  }
}

/** user_input / assistant text / tool calls, in order. `tools:false` keeps only what was said. */
export function turnsOf(events: any[], opts: { tools?: boolean } = {}): Turn[] {
  const context: TurnContext = {
    labels: new Map<string, string>(),
    updates: new Map<string, any>(),
    out: [],
    tools: opts.tools !== false,
  };
  for (const event of events)
    if (event?.type === "tool_label") context.labels.set(event.tool_use_id, event.label);
  for (const event of events)
    if (event?.type === "tool_update") context.updates.set(event.tool_use_id, event);
  for (const event of events) pushEventTurns(context, event);
  return context.out;
}

export function threadToMarkdown(
  t: { title: string; provider: string; model: string; cwd: string; createdAt: number },
  label: string,
  events: any[],
): string {
  const lines = [
    `# ${t.title}`,
    "",
    `*${label} · ${t.model} · ${t.cwd} · started ${new Date(t.createdAt).toISOString().slice(0, 16).replace("T", " ")} UTC*`,
    "",
  ];
  let last: Turn["role"] | undefined;
  for (const turn of turnsOf(events)) {
    if (turn.role !== last) lines.push(turn.role === "user" ? "## You" : "## Agent", "");
    last = turn.role;
    lines.push(turn.tool ? `> ${turn.text.replace(/\n/g, " ")}` : turn.text, ""); // tool lines are quoted so they read as activity
  }
  return (
    lines
      .join("\n")
      .replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")
      .trimEnd() + "\n"
  );
}

/** Context for an agent that cannot fork natively: the earlier conversation as plain text, newest part kept if it is long. */
export function replayPreamble(events: any[], maxChars = 24_000): string {
  const body = turnsOf(events, { tools: false })
    .map((t) => `${t.role === "user" ? "User" : "Assistant"}: ${t.text}`)
    .join("\n\n");
  const clipped =
    body.length > maxChars
      ? "… (earlier part omitted)\n\n" + body.slice(body.length - maxChars)
      : body;
  return `This conversation was forked from an earlier one. Here is what was said so far (tool output omitted); continue from it.\n\n--- earlier conversation ---\n${clipped}\n--- end of earlier conversation ---\n\n`;
}

/** A bounded, local handoff: no summarizer call and no native session history carried into the new run. */
export function recentPreamble(events: any[], maxChars = 6000): string {
  const limit = Math.max(0, Math.floor(maxChars));
  const header =
    "This is a fresh session with a short handoff: the initial request and recent conversation only. Earlier constraints may be omitted; the full transcript remains available in Console. Inspect the working tree before continuing.\n\n";
  if (limit <= header.length) return header.slice(0, limit);
  const turns = turnsOf(
    events.filter((e) => !e?.parent_tool_use_id),
    { tools: false },
  );
  const recent = turns.slice(-6);
  const first = turns.find((t) => t.role === "user");
  const excerpt = (s: string, n: number) =>
    s.length <= n
      ? s
      : n < 40
        ? s.slice(0, n)
        : s.slice(0, Math.floor((n - 25) / 2)) +
          "\n… (middle omitted) …\n" +
          s.slice(-Math.floor((n - 25) / 2));
  let budget = limit - header.length;
  let initial = "";
  if (first && !recent.includes(first)) {
    initial =
      "Initial request: " +
      excerpt(first.text, Math.max(0, Math.min(1200, Math.floor(budget / 4))));
    budget -= initial.length + 2;
  }
  const parts: string[] = [];
  for (const turn of recent.toReversed()) {
    const label = turn.role === "user" ? "User: " : "Assistant: ";
    const available = Math.min(1200, budget - label.length - 2);
    if (available <= 0) break;
    const text = label + excerpt(turn.text, available);
    parts.unshift(text);
    budget -= text.length + 2;
  }
  return (header + [initial, ...parts].filter(Boolean).join("\n\n") + "\n\n").slice(0, limit);
}

================
File: server/files.ts
================
import fs from "node:fs";
import type { FileHandle } from "node:fs/promises";
import {
  access,
  lstat,
  open,
  opendir,
  readdir,
  readlink,
  realpath,
  stat as statPath,
} from "node:fs/promises";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { performance } from "node:perf_hooks";
import type { RequestHandler } from "express";
import { contextDirs } from "./cwd-hints.ts";

export const MAX_VIEW_BYTES = 1 << 20;
export const MAX_PREVIEW_IMAGE_BYTES = 5 << 20;
export const TREE_PAGE_SIZE = 200;
const TREE_SCAN_SIZE = 1000;
const MAX_TREE_OFFSET = 100_000;

export class ViewError extends Error {
  status: number;
  constructor(status: number, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
  }
}

function inside(root: string, file: string): boolean {
  const relative = path.relative(root, file);
  return (
    relative === "" ||
    (!relative.startsWith(`..${path.sep}`) && relative !== ".." && !path.isAbsolute(relative))
  );
}

// This endpoint displays project text, not credential stores. Check both the requested
// name and the real target so a friendly symlink cannot expose a hidden secret.
export function sensitive(file: string): boolean {
  const parts = file.split(path.sep).map((part) => part.toLowerCase());
  if (
    parts.some((part) =>
      [".git", ".ssh", ".gnupg", ".aws", ".azure", ".kube", ".docker"].includes(part),
    )
  )
    return true;
  const name = parts.at(-1) || "";
  if (
    /^(?:\.env(?:\..*)?|\.npmrc|\.netrc|\.pgpass|\.pypirc|\.git-credentials|\.?credentials(?:\..*)?|\.claude\.json|auth\.json|password|secret|id_(?:rsa|dsa|ecdsa|ed25519)(?:\..*)?|\.\w*_history)$/.test(
      name,
    )
  )
    return true;
  if (/\.(?:pem|key|p12|pfx|keystore)$/.test(name)) return true;
  const home = os.homedir();
  return [".config", ".local/share/keyrings"].some((dir) => inside(path.join(home, dir), file));
}

// Beyond the thread folder the viewer reaches the rest of home and temp (screenshots, notes);
// system paths such as /proc stay closed because they can expose the server's environment.
export function reachable(root: string, file: string): boolean {
  return (
    !sensitive(file) && [root, os.homedir(), os.tmpdir(), "/tmp"].some((dir) => inside(dir, file))
  );
}

const DENIED = "This file is outside your home folder or contains private credentials.";

function pathExists(file: string) {
  return access(file).then(
    () => true,
    () => false,
  );
}

// Folders the thread's tool calls worked in (cd targets, edited files), newest first.
async function contextMatch(cwd: string, requested: string, threadId?: string) {
  if (!threadId) return undefined;
  for (const dir of contextDirs(threadId, cwd)) {
    const child = path.join(dir, requested);
    if (!sensitive(child) && (await pathExists(child))) return child;
  }
  return undefined;
}

async function subfolderHits(cwd: string, requested: string) {
  const hits: string[] = [];
  for (const entry of await readdir(cwd, { withFileTypes: true }).catch(() => [])) {
    if (!entry.isDirectory() || entry.name.startsWith(".") || entry.name === "node_modules")
      continue;
    const child = path.join(cwd, entry.name, requested);
    if (!sensitive(child) && (await pathExists(child))) hits.push(child);
  }
  return hits;
}

// Agents often cite paths relative to a subproject they cd'd into (public/render.js while the thread cwd is ~/repos).
// When a relative path is missing under cwd, accept it if exactly one immediate subfolder holds it.
async function resolveRelative(cwd: string, requested: string, threadId?: string) {
  const direct = path.resolve(cwd, requested);
  if (await pathExists(direct)) return direct;
  const fromContext = await contextMatch(cwd, requested, threadId);
  if (fromContext) return fromContext;
  const hits = await subfolderHits(cwd, requested);
  return hits.length === 1 ? hits[0] : direct;
}

export async function scopedPath(cwd: string, requested: string, threadId?: string) {
  const root = await realpath(cwd);
  const expanded = requested.startsWith("~/")
    ? path.join(os.homedir(), requested.slice(2))
    : requested;
  const candidate =
    path.isAbsolute(expanded) || expanded.split("/").includes("..")
      ? path.resolve(cwd, expanded)
      : await resolveRelative(cwd, expanded, threadId);
  if (!reachable(path.resolve(cwd), candidate) && !reachable(root, candidate))
    throw new ViewError(403, DENIED);
  const target = await realpath(candidate);
  if (!reachable(root, target)) throw new ViewError(403, DENIED);
  return { root, target, candidate };
}

// Link chain for a requested path (max 8 hops) so the viewer can show what a symlink points to.
async function linkChain(root: string, candidate: string) {
  const chain: { path: string; to: string; inside: boolean }[] = [];
  let current = candidate;
  for (let hop = 0; hop < 8; hop++) {
    const info = await lstat(current).catch(() => undefined);
    if (!info?.isSymbolicLink()) break;
    const to = path.resolve(path.dirname(current), await readlink(current));
    chain.push({ path: current, to, inside: inside(root, to) });
    current = to;
  }
  return chain;
}

function fileMeta(
  stat: { mode: number; uid: number; mtimeMs: number; size: number; nlink: number },
  chain: { path: string; to: string; inside: boolean }[],
) {
  return {
    size: stat.size,
    mode: (stat.mode & 0o777).toString(8).padStart(3, "0"),
    uid: stat.uid,
    mtime: Math.round(stat.mtimeMs),
    nlink: stat.nlink,
    symlink: chain.length ? chain : undefined,
  };
}

async function openScoped(root: string, target: string, directory = false): Promise<FileHandle> {
  // Nonblocking avoids waiting on pipes; no-follow prevents a swapped final symlink.
  const handle = await open(
    target,
    fs.constants.O_RDONLY |
      fs.constants.O_NOFOLLOW |
      fs.constants.O_NONBLOCK |
      (directory ? fs.constants.O_DIRECTORY : 0),
  );
  try {
    // Verify the opened inode too, closing parent-directory symlink races on Linux.
    if (process.platform === "linux") {
      const opened = await realpath(`/proc/self/fd/${handle.fd}`);
      if (!reachable(root, opened)) throw new ViewError(403, DENIED);
    }
    return handle;
  } catch (error) {
    await handle.close();
    throw error;
  }
}

function imageType(bytes: Buffer): string | null {
  if (
    bytes.length >= 8 &&
    bytes.subarray(0, 8).equals(Buffer.from([137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10]))
  )
    return "image/png";
  if (bytes.length >= 3 && bytes[0] === 255 && bytes[1] === 216 && bytes[2] === 255)
    return "image/jpeg";
  if (["GIF87a", "GIF89a"].includes(bytes.subarray(0, 6).toString("latin1"))) return "image/gif";
  if (
    bytes.length >= 12 &&
    bytes.subarray(0, 4).toString("latin1") === "RIFF" &&
    bytes.subarray(8, 12).toString("latin1") === "WEBP"
  )
    return "image/webp";
  return null;
}

const MEDIA_TYPES: Record<string, string> = {
  ".mp4": "video/mp4",
  ".m4v": "video/mp4",
  ".mov": "video/quicktime",
  ".webm": "video/webm",
  ".mp3": "audio/mpeg",
  ".wav": "audio/wav",
  ".m4a": "audio/mp4",
  ".aac": "audio/aac",
  ".ogg": "audio/ogg",
  ".oga": "audio/ogg",
  ".opus": "audio/ogg",
  ".flac": "audio/flac",
};
const ISO_BOXES = ["ftyp", "moov", "mdat", "wide", "free", "skip"];

// Extension picks the type; magic bytes must agree so a renamed text file is never served as media.
function mediaMagic(ext: string, head: Buffer): boolean {
  const ascii = (from: number, to: number) => head.subarray(from, to).toString("latin1");
  switch (ext) {
    case ".webm":
      return head.length >= 4 && head.readUInt32BE(0) === 0x1a45dfa3;
    case ".wav":
      return head.length >= 12 && ascii(0, 4) === "RIFF" && ascii(8, 12) === "WAVE";
    case ".mp3":
      return (
        ascii(0, 3) === "ID3" || (head.length >= 2 && head[0] === 0xff && (head[1] & 0xe0) === 0xe0)
      );
    case ".aac":
      return (
        ascii(0, 3) === "ID3" || (head.length >= 2 && head[0] === 0xff && (head[1] & 0xf6) === 0xf0)
      );
    case ".ogg":
    case ".oga":
    case ".opus":
      return ascii(0, 4) === "OggS";
    case ".flac":
      return ascii(0, 4) === "fLaC";
    default:
      return head.length >= 12 && ISO_BOXES.includes(ascii(4, 8));
  }
}

function mediaType(file: string, head: Buffer): string | null {
  const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
  const type = MEDIA_TYPES[ext];
  return type && mediaMagic(ext, head) ? type : null;
}

const identity = (root: string, target: string) => ({
  root,
  path: target,
  name: path.basename(target),
});

function tooLarge(limit: number) {
  return new ViewError(413, `The file is too large to preview (maximum ${limit >> 20} MB).`);
}

async function readWhole(handle: FileHandle, statSize: number, limit: number) {
  const buffer = Buffer.alloc(Math.min(statSize + 1, limit + 1));
  let size = 0;
  while (size < buffer.length) {
    const { bytesRead } = await handle.read(buffer, size, buffer.length - size, size);
    if (!bytesRead) break;
    size += bytesRead;
  }
  if (size > limit) throw tooLarge(limit);
  if (size > statSize) throw new ViewError(409, "This file changed while opening. Open it again.");
  return { buffer, size };
}

function decodeText(bytes: Buffer): string {
  let content: string;
  try {
    content = new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(bytes);
  } catch {
    throw new ViewError(415, "Only UTF-8 text files can be viewed.");
  }
  if (/[\u0000-\u0008\u000b\u000e-\u001f\u007f]/.test(content))
    throw new ViewError(415, "Only text files can be viewed.");
  // Needs key body on the next line, so code that only mentions the header (tests, docs) stays viewable.
  if (/-----BEGIN (?:[A-Z]+ )*PRIVATE KEY-----\r?\n[A-Za-z0-9+/=]{40}/.test(content))
    throw new ViewError(403, "Private keys cannot be previewed.");
  return content;
}

async function sniffFile(handle: FileHandle, target: string) {
  const head = Buffer.alloc(16);
  const peek = await handle.read(head, 0, head.length, 0);
  const media_type = imageType(head.subarray(0, peek.bytesRead));
  // Audio and video stream from /api/file-media with Range, so they have no preview size limit.
  const stream_type = media_type ? null : mediaType(target, head.subarray(0, peek.bytesRead));
  return { media_type, stream_type };
}

async function readProjectFile(cwd: string, requested: string, binary = false, threadId?: string) {
  const { root, target, candidate } = await scopedPath(cwd, requested, threadId);
  const handle = await openScoped(root, target);
  try {
    const stat = await handle.stat();
    const meta = fileMeta(stat, await linkChain(root, candidate));
    if (!stat.isFile())
      throw new ViewError(415, "Only regular text and image files can be viewed.");
    const { media_type, stream_type } = await sniffFile(handle, target);
    if (stream_type && !binary)
      return {
        kind: "media" as const,
        ...identity(root, target),
        media_type: stream_type,
        bytes: stat.size,
        meta,
      };
    const limit = media_type ? MAX_PREVIEW_IMAGE_BYTES : MAX_VIEW_BYTES;
    if (stat.size > limit) throw tooLarge(limit);
    if (binary && !media_type)
      throw new ViewError(415, "Only PNG, JPEG, GIF and WebP images can be previewed.");
    // Metadata does not read the whole image; the authenticated image request does.
    if (media_type && !binary)
      return {
        kind: "image" as const,
        ...identity(root, target),
        media_type,
        bytes: stat.size,
        meta,
      };
    const { buffer, size } = await readWhole(handle, stat.size, limit);
    if (media_type)
      return {
        kind: "image" as const,
        ...identity(root, target),
        media_type,
        bytes: size,
        meta,
        data: buffer.subarray(0, size),
      };
    const content = decodeText(buffer.subarray(0, size));
    return {
      kind: "text" as const,
      ...identity(root, target),
      content,
      bytes: size,
      meta,
    };
  } finally {
    await handle.close();
  }
}

function requestPath(requested: unknown): string {
  if (
    typeof requested !== "string" ||
    !requested ||
    requested.length > 8192 ||
    requested.includes("\0")
  )
    throw new ViewError(400, "Choose a valid file path.");
  return requested;
}

function failure(error: any) {
  if (error instanceof ViewError) return { status: error.status, error: error.message };
  if (["ENOENT", "ENOTDIR"].includes(error?.code))
    return { status: 404, error: "The file or folder no longer exists." };
  if (["EACCES", "EPERM", "ELOOP"].includes(error?.code))
    return { status: 403, error: "This file or folder cannot be previewed." };
  return { status: 415, error: "This file or folder cannot be previewed." };
}

function timing(res: Parameters<RequestHandler>[1], start: number, kind: "file" | "tree") {
  const ms = Math.round((performance.now() - start) * 100) / 100;
  res.setHeader("Server-Timing", `${kind};dur=${ms}`);
  return ms;
}

export type Lookup = (id: string) => { cwd: string; id?: string } | undefined;

// These routes mount after authentication. Every request resolves scope afresh;
// image URLs stay within the selected thread's real cwd or the fixed repos root.
export function viewerScope(query: Record<string, unknown>, lookup: Lookup) {
  if (query.scope !== undefined) {
    if (query.scope !== "repos" || query.thread !== undefined)
      throw new ViewError(400, "Choose a valid file scope.");
    return {
      cwd: path.join(os.homedir(), "repos"),
      threadId: undefined as string | undefined,
      params: { scope: "repos" },
    };
  }
  const thread = typeof query.thread === "string" ? lookup(query.thread) : undefined;
  if (!thread) throw new ViewError(404, "Unknown thread.");
  return { cwd: thread.cwd, threadId: thread.id, params: { thread: String(query.thread) } };
}

export function fileViewer(lookup: Lookup, binary = false): RequestHandler {
  return async (req, res) => {
    const start = performance.now();
    res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
    res.setHeader("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
    try {
      const scope = viewerScope(req.query, lookup);
      const file = await readProjectFile(
        scope.cwd,
        requestPath(req.query.path),
        binary,
        scope.threadId,
      );
      const read_ms = timing(res, start, "file");
      if (binary && file.kind === "image" && file.data) {
        res.setHeader("Content-Type", file.media_type!);
        res.setHeader("Content-Disposition", "inline");
        res.setHeader("Content-Security-Policy", "default-src 'none'; sandbox");
        return void res.send(file.data);
      }
      const route =
        file.kind === "image" ? "file-image" : file.kind === "media" ? "file-media" : undefined;
      const url = route
        ? `/api/${route}?${new URLSearchParams({ ...scope.params, path: file.path })}`
        : undefined;
      res.json({ ...file, url, read_ms });
    } catch (error) {
      timing(res, start, "file");
      const result = failure(error);
      res.status(result.status).json({ error: result.error });
    }
  };
}

/** Parse a single `Range: bytes=` spec against `size`. null = ignore the header (serve 200), "bad" = 416. */
export function parseRange(
  header: unknown,
  size: number,
): { start: number; end: number } | null | "bad" {
  if (typeof header !== "string" || !header.startsWith("bytes=")) return null;
  const spec = header.slice(6).trim();
  if (spec.includes(",")) return null;
  const m = /^(\d*)-(\d*)$/.exec(spec);
  if (!m || (m[1] === "" && m[2] === "")) return "bad";
  let start: number, end: number;
  if (m[1] === "") {
    const n = Number(m[2]);
    if (!n) return "bad";
    start = Math.max(0, size - n);
    end = size - 1;
  } else {
    start = Number(m[1]);
    end = m[2] === "" ? size - 1 : Math.min(Number(m[2]), size - 1);
  }
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(start) || start >= size || end < start) return "bad";
  return { start, end };
}

function assertMediaExtension(target: string) {
  if (!MEDIA_TYPES[path.extname(target).toLowerCase()])
    throw new ViewError(
      415,
      "Only MP4, WebM and MOV video or MP3, WAV, M4A, AAC, Ogg and FLAC audio can be played.",
    );
}

async function sniffMedia(handle: FileHandle, target: string): Promise<string> {
  const head = Buffer.alloc(16);
  const peek = await handle.read(head, 0, head.length, 0);
  const type = mediaType(target, head.subarray(0, peek.bytesRead));
  if (!type) throw new ViewError(415, "This file is not playable media.");
  return type;
}

function applyMediaHeaders(
  res: Parameters<RequestHandler>[1],
  type: string,
  range: { start: number; end: number } | null,
  size: number,
) {
  const [start, end] = range ? [range.start, range.end] : [0, size - 1];
  res.status(range ? 206 : 200);
  if (range) res.setHeader("Content-Range", `bytes ${start}-${end}/${size}`);
  res.setHeader("Accept-Ranges", "bytes");
  res.setHeader("Content-Type", type);
  res.setHeader("Content-Disposition", "inline");
  res.setHeader("Content-Security-Policy", "default-src 'none'; sandbox");
  return { start, end };
}

// Audio and video for inline players. Same scope and secret rules as the viewer; streamed from the verified descriptor with HTTP Range
// so phones can seek. Only known media containers (extension + magic bytes) are served, always inline under a locked-down CSP.
export function fileMedia(lookup: Lookup): RequestHandler {
  return async (req, res) => {
    res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "private, no-cache");
    res.setHeader("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
    let handle: FileHandle | undefined;
    try {
      const scope = viewerScope(req.query, lookup);
      const { root, target } = await scopedPath(
        scope.cwd,
        requestPath(req.query.path),
        scope.threadId,
      );
      assertMediaExtension(target);
      handle = await openScoped(root, target);
      const stat = await handle.stat();
      if (!stat.isFile()) throw new ViewError(415, "Only regular media files can be played.");
      const type = await sniffMedia(handle, target);
      const range = parseRange(req.headers.range, stat.size);
      if (range === "bad") {
        res.setHeader("Content-Range", `bytes */${stat.size}`);
        throw new ViewError(416, "Invalid byte range.");
      }
      const { start, end } = applyMediaHeaders(res, type, range, stat.size);
      if (stat.size === 0 || req.method === "HEAD") {
        res.setHeader("Content-Length", stat.size === 0 ? 0 : end - start + 1);
        await handle.close();
        return void res.end();
      }
      res.setHeader("Content-Length", end - start + 1);
      const stream = handle.createReadStream({ start, end }); // owns the descriptor from here (autoClose)
      handle = undefined;
      res.on("close", () => stream.destroy());
      stream.on("error", () => res.destroy());
      stream.pipe(res);
    } catch (error) {
      await handle?.close().catch(() => {});
      const result = failure(error);
      res.status(result.status).json({ error: result.error });
    }
  };
}

type TreeEntry = { name: string; path: string; kind: "directory" | "file"; link?: string };

function treeOffset(rawCursor: unknown): number {
  const offset =
    rawCursor === undefined
      ? 0
      : typeof rawCursor === "string" && /^\d{1,6}$/.test(rawCursor)
        ? Number(rawCursor)
        : -1;
  if (offset < 0 || offset > MAX_TREE_OFFSET) throw new ViewError(400, "Invalid folder cursor.");
  return offset;
}

async function entryKind(entry: fs.Dirent, child: string, root: string) {
  let kind: "directory" | "file" | undefined = entry.isDirectory()
    ? "directory"
    : entry.isFile()
      ? "file"
      : undefined;
  // Symlinks (CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, .mcp.json in ~/repos) list as their target kind; opening still enforces realpath scope.
  if (!kind && entry.isSymbolicLink()) {
    const real = await realpath(child).catch(() => undefined);
    if (real && reachable(root, real)) {
      const info = await statPath(real).catch(() => undefined);
      kind = info?.isDirectory() ? "directory" : info?.isFile() ? "file" : undefined;
    }
  }
  return kind;
}

async function treeEntry(entry: fs.Dirent, child: string, kind: "directory" | "file") {
  return {
    name: entry.name,
    path: child,
    kind,
    ...(entry.isSymbolicLink() ? { link: await readlink(child).catch(() => undefined) } : {}),
  } as TreeEntry;
}

async function scanTree(dir: fs.Dir, target: string, root: string, offset: number) {
  const entries: TreeEntry[] = [];
  let index = 0,
    done = false;
  while (index < offset) {
    if (!(await dir.read())) {
      done = true;
      break;
    }
    index++;
  }
  while (
    !done &&
    index < offset + TREE_SCAN_SIZE &&
    index < MAX_TREE_OFFSET &&
    entries.length < TREE_PAGE_SIZE
  ) {
    const entry = await dir.read();
    if (!entry) {
      done = true;
      break;
    }
    index++;
    const child = path.join(target, entry.name);
    if (sensitive(child)) continue;
    const kind = await entryKind(entry, child, root);
    if (kind) entries.push(await treeEntry(entry, child, kind));
  }
  return { entries, index, done };
}

async function listTree(root: string, target: string, offset: number) {
  const handle = await openScoped(root, target, true);
  try {
    const dir = await opendir(
      process.platform === "linux" ? `/proc/self/fd/${handle.fd}` : target,
      {
        bufferSize: 64,
      },
    );
    try {
      return await scanTree(dir, target, root, offset);
    } finally {
      await dir.close();
    }
  } finally {
    await handle.close();
  }
}

// Include the complete decoded JSON payload size (including this field).
function encodeSized(result: { bytes: number }) {
  let encoded = JSON.stringify(result);
  while (result.bytes !== Buffer.byteLength(encoded)) {
    result.bytes = Buffer.byteLength(encoded);
    encoded = JSON.stringify(result);
  }
  return encoded;
}

// One level only, using Dirent types (stat only for symlinks). Special files
// are omitted; opening a symlink still enforces realpath scope.
// The cursor counts raw directory entries so filtered entries cannot stall paging.
export function fileTree(lookup: Lookup): RequestHandler {
  return async (req, res) => {
    const start = performance.now();
    res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
    try {
      const scope = viewerScope(req.query, lookup);
      const { root, target } = await scopedPath(
        scope.cwd,
        requestPath(req.query.path ?? "."),
        scope.threadId,
      );
      const offset = treeOffset(req.query.cursor);
      const { entries, index, done } = await listTree(root, target, offset);
      const read_ms = timing(res, start, "tree");
      const result = {
        root,
        path: target,
        parent: target === root ? null : path.dirname(target),
        entries,
        cursor: done || index >= MAX_TREE_OFFSET ? null : String(index),
        truncated: !done,
        limit_reached: !done && index >= MAX_TREE_OFFSET,
        bytes: 0,
        read_ms,
      };
      res.type("json").send(encodeSized(result));
    } catch (error) {
      timing(res, start, "tree");
      const result = failure(error);
      res.status(result.status).json({ error: result.error });
    }
  };
}

================
File: server/history-wire.ts
================
// History is a view, not a harness replay. Keep the fields rendered by public/app.js
// without repeatedly transferring provider diagnostics, signed reasoning, or tool catalogs.
// Logs, exports, and live events retain their full original data.
const fields = (source: any, names: string[]) =>
  Object.fromEntries(
    names.filter((name) => source[name] !== undefined).map((name) => [name, source[name]]),
  );

function blockView(block: any) {
  if (!block || typeof block !== "object") return block;
  switch (block.type) {
    case "text":
      return fields(block, ["type", "text"]);
    case "thinking":
      return fields(block, ["type", "thinking"]);
    case "redacted_thinking":
      return { type: block.type };
    case "tool_use":
      return fields(block, ["type", "id", "name", "input"]);
    case "tool_result": {
      const view = fields(block, ["type", "tool_use_id", "content", "is_error"]);
      // The tool card renders only text, never embedded image data or provider metadata.
      if (Array.isArray(block.content))
        view.content = block.content.map((part: any) => fields(part, ["type", "text"]));
      return view;
    }
    default:
      return block; // Unknown future content stays intact until its display contract is known.
  }
}

export function historyWireEvent(event: any) {
  if (!event || typeof event !== "object") return event;
  switch (event.type) {
    case "assistant":
    case "user": {
      const view = fields(event, ["type", "parent_tool_use_id", "ts"]); // ts: replayed rows show step durations
      if (event.message)
        view.message = {
          ...fields(event.message, ["role"]),
          ...(Array.isArray(event.message.content)
            ? { content: event.message.content.map(blockView) }
            : {}),
        };
      return view;
    }
    case "result": {
      const view = fields(event, ["type", "subtype", "is_error", "duration_ms", "total_cost_usd"]);
      // Error text is short and lets the reply footer offer a fix, such as signing in after an expired Claude login.
      if (event.is_error && typeof event.result === "string")
        view.result = event.result.slice(0, 500);
      return view;
    }
    case "system":
      return fields(
        event,
        event.subtype?.startsWith("task_")
          ? [
              "type",
              "subtype",
              "tool_use_id",
              "usage",
              "description",
              "is_backgrounded",
              "status",
              "summary",
            ]
          : ["type", "subtype"],
      );
    // Quotas already arrive in hello/usage; historical quota events do not update the UI.
    case "rate_limit_event":
      return { type: event.type };
    default:
      return event;
  }
}

================
File: server/history.ts
================
import fs from "node:fs";

// Lines of a JSONL buffer that contain any needle, in file order. Decoding and splitting whole logs costs seconds at boot;
// a native byte search finds the few init and result lines among megabytes of tool output.
export function linesWith(buf: Buffer, needles: string[]): string[] {
  const starts = new Set<number>();
  for (const needle of needles) {
    for (let at = buf.indexOf(needle); at !== -1; ) {
      const start = buf.lastIndexOf(10, at) + 1;
      starts.add(start);
      const end = buf.indexOf(10, at);
      if (end === -1) break;
      at = buf.indexOf(needle, end);
    }
  }
  return [...starts]
    .sort((a, b) => a - b)
    .map((start) => {
      const end = buf.indexOf(10, start);
      return buf.toString("utf8", start, end === -1 ? buf.length : end);
    });
}

// Byte offsets, not parsed transcripts: retain a small index and read only the requested page.
// Extend indexes as JSONL grows; replacing/truncating a log invalidates its index.
export class HistoryIndex {
  private files = new Map<string, { ino: number; size: number; offsets: number[] }>();
  private extend(file: string, fd: number) {
    const stat = fs.fstatSync(fd);
    let index = this.files.get(file);
    if (!index || index.ino !== stat.ino || index.size > stat.size)
      index = { ino: stat.ino, size: 0, offsets: [0] };
    if (index.size < stat.size) {
      const chunk = Buffer.alloc(64 * 1024);
      let pos = index.size;
      while (pos < stat.size) {
        const n = fs.readSync(fd, chunk, 0, Math.min(chunk.length, stat.size - pos), pos);
        if (!n) break;
        for (let i = chunk.indexOf(10); i !== -1 && i < n; i = chunk.indexOf(10, i + 1))
          index.offsets.push(pos + i + 1);
        pos += n;
      }
      index.size = pos;
    }
    this.files.delete(file);
    this.files.set(file, index);
    if (this.files.size > 64) this.files.delete(this.files.keys().next().value!);
    return index;
  }
  private withFile<T>(file: string, empty: T, fn: (fd: number, offsets: number[]) => T): T {
    let fd: number;
    try {
      fd = fs.openSync(file, "r");
    } catch {
      return empty;
    }
    try {
      return fn(fd, this.extend(file, fd).offsets);
    } finally {
      fs.closeSync(fd);
    }
  }
  private lines(fd: number, offsets: number[], start: number, end: number) {
    const buf = Buffer.alloc(offsets[end] - offsets[start]);
    fs.readSync(fd, buf, 0, buf.length, offsets[start]);
    return {
      events: buf
        .toString("utf8")
        .split("\n")
        .filter(Boolean)
        .map((l) => JSON.parse(l)),
      bytes: buf.length,
    };
  }
  // Lines written so far: live events carry it so a cached view can later ask for only what follows.
  count(file: string) {
    return this.withFile(file, 0, (_, offsets) => offsets.length - 1);
  }
  // Everything after line `since`, or null when the gap is wider than `limit` (or the log shrank): the caller sends a full page instead.
  after(file: string, since: number, limit: number) {
    return this.withFile(file, null, (fd, offsets) => {
      const total = offsets.length - 1;
      if (since > total || total - since > limit) return null;
      return { ...this.lines(fd, offsets, since, total), start: since, end: total, total };
    });
  }
  read(file: string, before: number | undefined, limit: number) {
    return this.withFile(
      file,
      { events: [] as any[], start: 0, end: 0, total: 0, bytes: 0 },
      (fd, offsets) => {
        const total = offsets.length - 1;
        const end = Math.max(0, Math.min(before ?? total, total));
        const candidate = Math.max(0, end - limit - 150);
        let start = Math.max(0, end - limit);
        let scanned = 0;
        const header = Buffer.alloc(256);
        for (let i = Math.min(start, end - 1); i >= candidate; i--) {
          const size = offsets[i + 1] - offsets[i];
          const n = fs.readSync(fd, header, 0, Math.min(size, header.length), offsets[i]);
          scanned += n;
          // Logs put type first. Inspect that tiny header instead of parsing discarded megabyte tool results.
          let type = header
            .subarray(0, n)
            .toString("utf8")
            .match(/^\s*\{\s*"type"\s*:\s*"([^"]+)"/)?.[1];
          if (!type) {
            const line = Buffer.alloc(size);
            fs.readSync(fd, line, 0, size, offsets[i]);
            scanned += size;
            type = JSON.parse(line.toString("utf8")).type;
          }
          if (type === "user_input") {
            start = i;
            break;
          }
        }
        const { events, bytes } = this.lines(fd, offsets, start, end);
        return { events, start, end, total, bytes: bytes + scanned };
      },
    );
  }
}

  markReady = resolve;

const clients = new Set<WebSocket>();
const subscriptions = new WeakMap<WebSocket, string>();
const broadcast = (msg: unknown) => {
  const s = JSON.stringify(msg);
  const event = msg as { t?: string; thread?: string };
  const invalidation =
    event.t === "event" ? JSON.stringify({ t: "invalidate", thread: event.thread }) : null;
  for (const c of clients)
    if (c.readyState === c.OPEN) {
      const selected = subscriptions.get(c);
      c.send(invalidation && selected && selected !== event.thread ? invalidation : s);
    }
};

// Plan-only until ~/.config/agent-console/window-dispatch.json sets enabled (see docs/window-dispatch.md).
if (process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_MOCK !== "1") {
  const activeRuns = () =>
    threads
      .list()
      .filter((t) => t.issueRun && t.status === "running")
      .map((t) => ({ provider: t.provider, role: t.issueRun!.role }));
  const dispatcher = createDispatcher({
    usage: () => usage.all(),
    events: automationLog,
    active: () => activeRuns(),
    start: (d) =>
      startIssue(d, {
        create: (o) => threads.create(o),
        send: (id, text) => threads.send(id, text),
      }),
  });
  const reviewer = createReviewer({
    threads: () => threads.list(),
    dataDir: DATA_DIR,
    canReview: (t) => !assignedOwner(t),
    rerouteWorker: (req) => threads.rerouteIssueWorker(req),
    premium: async (req) => {
      const cfg = loadDispatchConfig();
      if (!cfg.enabled || cfg.dryRun || cfg.routing.mode !== "live") return;
      // A persisted assignment also acts as a receipt if the process died before the worker was marked.
      const previous = threads
        .list()
        .find(
          (t) =>
            t.issueRun?.premiumAssignment && canonicalIssue(t.issueRun) === canonicalIssue(req),
        );
      if (previous) return { thread: previous.id };
      const answer = routeWork({
        role: "work",
        effort: "deep",
        premium: true,
        exclude: req.exclude,
        usage: usage.all(),
        active: activeRuns(),
        now: Date.now(),
      });
      if (answer.decision !== "route" || !answer.route || answer.route.provider === "pi") return;
      return assignPremium({ ...req, ...answer.route, mode: "bypassPermissions" });
    },
    canResume: (t) => {
      const live = threads.get(t.id);
      if (
        !live ||
        live.archived ||
        live.status === "running" ||
        live.background ||
        live.quotaPaused ||
        assignedOwner(live)
      )
        return false;
      const cfg = loadDispatchConfig();
      if (!cfg.enabled || cfg.dryRun || cfg.routing.mode !== "live") return false;
      const answer = routeWork({
        role: "work",
        effort: t.issueRun!.effort as any,
        premium: t.provider !== "pi" && t.issueRun!.effort !== "deep",
        usage: usage.all(),
        active: activeRuns(),
        now: Date.now(),
        flags: { bauOnPi: cfg.routing.bauOnPi },
      });
      return (
        answer.decision === "route" &&
        [answer.route, ...answer.alternatives.map((a) => a.route)].some(
          (route) => route?.provider === t.provider,
        )
      );
    },
    runner: {
      create: (o) => threads.create(o),
      send: (id, text) => threads.send(id, text),
      mark: (id, patch) => threads.setIssueRun(id, patch),
    },
    route: (effort) =>
      routeWork({
        role: "review",
        effort: effort as any,
        usage: usage.all(),
        active: activeRuns(),
        now: Date.now(),
      }),
    events: automationLog,
  });
  threads.onIssueTurnEnd = (t, ev) => {
    if (!assignedOwner(t)) return reviewer.turnEnded(t, ev);
  };
  // Threads stopped by an exhausted premium quota continue late in a later window, when Deep issues would also start.
  const resumeQuotaPaused = () => {
    const cfg = loadDispatchConfig();
    if (!cfg.enabled) return;
    for (const provider of resumeDue(usage.all(), Date.now(), cfg)) {
      const waiting = threads
        .list()
        .filter((t) => t.quotaPaused?.provider === provider && !t.archived).length;
      if (!waiting) continue;
      const resumed = cfg.dryRun
        ? 0
        : threads.resumeQuotaPaused(
            provider,
            undefined,
            (t) => !!t.issueRun?.premiumAssignment || !assignedOwner(t),
          );
      automationLog.record({
        step: "dispatcher",
        kind: "ok",
        msg: `${cfg.dryRun ? "dry run: would resume" : "resumed"} ${cfg.dryRun ? waiting : resumed} quota-paused ${provider} thread(s)`,
      });
    }
  };
  const archiver = createAutomationArchiver({ threads });
  let automationSweep: Promise<void> | undefined;
  const sweepAutomation = (archive = false) =>
    (automationSweep ??= (async () => {
      if (archive) await archiver.sweep();
      await reviewer.sweep(threads.list().filter((t) => !assignedOwner(t)));
      // Finished Auto threads keep only transcripts; deleted threads lose their runtime folder.
      const runtime = await sweepAutoRuntime(DATA_DIR, threads.list());
      if (runtime.pruned || runtime.removed || runtime.failed)
        automationLog.record({
          step: "worktree",
          kind: runtime.failed ? "fail" : "info",
          msg: `Auto runtime cleanup: ${runtime.pruned} pruned, ${runtime.removed} removed, ${runtime.failed} failed`,
        });
    })()
      .catch(() => {})
      .finally(() => {
        automationSweep = undefined;
      }));
  void sweepAutomation(true); // catches up on closures missed while the Console was down
  // Closed issues and merged PRs arrive as GitHub webhooks (the triage worker drops a marker here), so archiving is event-driven.
  watchClosed(
    path.join(DATA_DIR, "github-events"),
    () => archiver.sweep(),
    () =>
      automationLog.record({
        step: "triage",
        kind: "fail",
        msg: "archive sweep after GitHub closure failed",
      }),
  );
  setInterval(() => {
    void sweepAutomation();
    void dispatcher.tick().catch(() => {});
    resumeQuotaPaused();
  }, 5 * 60_000).unref();
  const opener = createOpener({
    usage: () => usage.all(),
    events: automationLog,
    refresh: readCodexUsage,
    claudeAccounts: () =>
      claudeAccounts()
        .filter((a) => a.ready)
        .map((a) => a.id),
    open: (provider, route, account) => {
      const t = threads.create({
        provider,
        ...route,
        ...(account ? { claudeAccount: account } : {}),
        cwd: path.join(os.homedir(), "repos"),
        mode: "bypassPermissions",
        title: `Window opener · ${provider}${account ? ":" + account : ""}`,
      });
      threads.send(t.id, "Reply with the single word: ok");
      threads.archive(t.id, true);
    },
  });
  setInterval(() => {
    opener.tick().catch(() => {});
  }, 5 * 60_000).unref();
}

const app = express();
app.disable("x-powered-by");
app.use((_req, res, next) => {
  res.setHeader("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
  res.setHeader("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer");
  res.setHeader("X-Frame-Options", "DENY");
  res.setHeader(
    "Content-Security-Policy",
    "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self' 'wasm-unsafe-eval'; img-src 'self' data: blob: https: http:; media-src 'self' blob: https: http:; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; connect-src 'self' wss: ws:; frame-ancestors 'none'",
  );
  next();
});

app.get("/login", (req, res) => (authed(req) ? res.redirect("/") : sendPublic(res, "login.html")));
// ---- attachments: upload (raw bytes), download, discard. State-changing, so cookie auth alone is not enough: same-origin only.
const sameOrigin: express.RequestHandler = (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    if (new URL(String(req.headers.origin)).host === req.headers.host) return next();
  } catch {}
  res.status(403).json({ error: "Cross-site request refused." });
};
app.post("/login", sameOrigin, express.json({ limit: "1kb" }), (req, res) => {
  if (limited()) return void res.status(429).json({ error: "Too many attempts. Try again later." });
  if (!passwordOk(req.body?.password)) {
    recordFail();
    return void res.status(401).json({ error: "Wrong password." });
  }
  res.setHeader("Set-Cookie", setCookie(makeToken()));
  res.json({ ok: true });
});

// Loopback broker for the single model queue. Pi, the triage worker and the label helpers all admit through here.
const MODEL_RATE_TOKEN_HEADER = "x-agent-console-tool";
app.post("/api/model-request", express.json({ limit: "4kb" }), async (req, res) => {
  const given = Buffer.from(String(req.headers[MODEL_RATE_TOKEN_HEADER] || ""));
  const expected = Buffer.from(String(process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_RATE_TOKEN || ""));
  if (
    !expected.length ||
    given.length !== expected.length ||
    !crypto.timingSafeEqual(given, expected)
  )
    return void res.status(401).json({ error: "Not an Agent Console model request." });
  const provider =
    req.body?.provider === "nvidia" || req.body?.provider === "openrouter"
      ? req.body.provider
      : "other";
  const priority =
    typeof req.body?.priority === "string" && req.body.priority in MODEL_REQUEST_PRIORITIES
      ? req.body.priority
      : "interactive";
  const abort = new AbortController();
  const disconnected = () => abort.abort();
  res.once("close", disconnected);
  try {
    if (req.body?.action === "acquire")
      await acquireModelRequest({ provider, priority, signal: abort.signal });
    else if (req.body?.action === "cooldown")
      await reportModel429(
        typeof req.body.retryAfter === "string" || typeof req.body.retryAfter === "number"
          ? req.body.retryAfter
          : null,
        provider,
      );
    else return void res.status(400).json({ error: "Unknown model admission action." });
    if (!res.destroyed) res.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (error: any) {
    const message =
      error?.code === "MODEL_PROVIDER_QUOTA_EXHAUSTED"
        ? error.message
        : "Model admission unavailable.";
    if (!res.destroyed)
      res.status(503).json({
        error: message,
        message,
        code: error?.code,
        retryAt: error?.retryAt,
        provider: error?.provider,
      });
  } finally {
    res.removeListener("close", disconnected);
  }
});

// Claude/Codex delegate research to a read-only Pi subagent; the report comes back as the tool result.
async function piResearch(input: any, thread?: string): Promise<string> {
  const caller = thread ? threads.get(thread) : undefined;
  const cwd = typeof input.folder === "string" && input.folder ? input.folder : caller?.cwd;
  const res = await runPiResearch({
    version: 1,
    task: input.task,
    cwd: cwd as string,
    caller: { thread, provider: caller?.provider },
  });
  if (res.phase === "failed") throw new Error(`Pi research failed: ${res.error}`);
  return researchToolText(res);
}

const cliSearch = new CliSearch();
app.post("/api/agent-tools/:name", express.json({ limit: "1mb" }), async (req, res) => {
  const header = req.headers["x-agent-console-thread"];
  const gate = gateToolCall(
    TOOL_TOKEN,
    String(req.headers["x-agent-console-tool"] || ""),
    typeof header === "string" ? header : undefined,
    req.params.name,
    (threadId) => !!threads.get(threadId)?.issueRun,
  );
  if (gate.status === 401) return void res.status(401).json({ error: gate.error });
  const thread = gate.threadId;
  try {
    if (gate.error) throw new Error(gate.error);
    const input = req.body ?? {};
    const result = ["thread_list", "thread_send"].includes(req.params.name)
      ? await threads.threadTool(req.params.name, thread, input)
      : req.params.name === "session_redact"
        ? transcriptRedaction.tool(input, thread ? threads.get(thread) : undefined)
        : req.params.name === "cli_search"
          ? cliSearch.search(input)
          : req.params.name === "session_search"
            ? await sessionSearch.search(input)
            : req.params.name === "session_read"
              ? await sessionSearch.read(input)
              : req.params.name === "pi_research"
                ? await piResearch(input, thread)
                : isConsoleCliTool(req.params.name)
                  ? await consoleCliTool(req.params.name, input, {
                      ideas,
                      schedules,
                      sessionSearch,
                    })
                  : schedules.tool(req.params.name, thread, input);
    res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
    res.json(result);
  } catch (e: any) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: e?.message || String(e) });
  }
});

  if (authed(req)) return next();

    // Keep the Android share-target query so a share survives signing in.
    const query = req.path === "/" ? new URL(req.originalUrl, "http://local").search : "";
    return res.redirect(`/login${query}`);
  }
  res.status(401).end();
});

// Restart button: runs `bin/agent-console restart-when-idle` (waits for running turns, then replaces this process);
// its idle-restart lock keeps two restarts from overlapping.
app.post("/restart", sameOrigin, (_req, res) => {
  spawn(path.join(ROOT, "bin", "agent-console"), ["restart-when-idle"], {
    cwd: ROOT,
    detached: true,
    stdio: "ignore",
    env: { ...process.env, AGENT_CONSOLE_RUN: RUN_DIR },
  })
    .on("error", () => console.warn("The restart could not be scheduled."))
    .unref();
  res.json({ ok: true });
});
const MAX_SHARE_BYTES = 100 << 20;
const shareNoteName = (provider: string) =>
  provider === "instagram" ? "instagram.txt" : "post.md";
function attachShare(
  link: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof resolveShare>>,
  result: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof downloadShare>>["result"],
  target: unknown,
) {
  // an existing thread is never removed on failure; only a thread created here is
  const existing = typeof target === "string";
  const thread = existing
    ? threads.get(target)!
    : threads.create({
        provider: "claude",
        claudeAccount: "primary",
        cwd: path.join(os.homedir(), "repos"),
        title: shareTitle(link),
      });
  // the note carries the post's caption, which is as untrusted as the media
  const sources = [...result.items, { path: result.notePath, name: shareNoteName(link.provider) }];
  try {
    const untrusted = isSandboxedShareProvider(link.provider);
    const files = adoptShareFiles(thread.id, sources, untrusted, MAX_SHARE_BYTES);
    return { thread: thread.id, threadProvider: thread.provider, files };
  } catch (error) {
    if (!existing) threads.remove(thread.id);
    throw error;
  }
}
async function handleShare(req: express.Request, res: express.Response, only?: string) {
  let link: ReturnType<typeof resolveShare>;
  try {
    link = resolveShare(req.body?.text, req.body?.url);
  } catch (failure: any) {
    return void res.status(422).json({ error: failure?.message ?? "Share failed." });
  }
  if (!link || (only && link.provider !== only))
    return void res.status(422).json({
      error: only ? "No Instagram reel link found in that share." : "No link found in that share.",
    });
  const target: unknown = req.body?.thread;
  if (target !== undefined && (typeof target !== "string" || !isId(target) || !threads.get(target)))
    return void res.status(404).json({ error: "Unknown thread." });
  const abort = new AbortController();
  res.on("close", () => {
    if (!res.writableEnded) abort.abort();
  });
  let download: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof downloadShare>> | undefined;
  try {
    download = await downloadShare(link, abort.signal);
    const { result } = download;
    const saved = attachShare(link, result, target);
    // the client fills the composer with the prompt and these uploads; nothing is sent until the user taps Send
    res.json({
      thread: saved.thread,
      prompt: sharePrompt(link, result, saved.threadProvider),
      attachments: saved.files.map(({ id: uploadId, name, kind, media_type, size }) => ({
        id: uploadId,
        name,
        kind,
        media_type,
        size,
      })),
    });
  } catch (failure: any) {
    res.status(422).json({
      error:
        failure instanceof ShareError ? failure.message : (failure?.message ?? "Share failed."),
    });
  } finally {
    download?.cleanup();
  }
}
const shareBody = [sameOrigin, express.json({ limit: "4kb" })] as const;
app.post("/api/share", ...shareBody, (req, res) => void handleShare(req, res));
app.post(
  "/api/share/instagram",
  ...shareBody,
  (req, res) => void handleShare(req, res, "instagram"),
);
app.post(
  "/uploads/:thread",
  sameOrigin,
  express.raw({ type: () => true, limit: MAX_FILE_BYTES }),
  (req, res) => {
    const { thread } = req.params;
    if (!isId(thread) || !threads.get(thread))
      return void res.status(404).json({ error: "Unknown thread." });
    try {
      const name = (() => {
        try {
          return decodeURIComponent(String(req.headers["x-name"] ?? ""));
        } catch {
          return "";
        }
      })();
      res.json(
        saveUpload(
          thread,
          name,
          req.headers["content-type"],
          Buffer.isBuffer(req.body) ? req.body : Buffer.alloc(0),
        ),
      );
    } catch (e: any) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: e?.message ?? "Upload failed." });
    }
  },
);

app.get("/export/:thread", async (req, res) => {

  if (!t) return void res.status(404).end();

  res
    .attachment(
      `${
        t.title
          .replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|\u0000-\u001f]+/g, " ")
          .trim()
          .slice(0, 60) || "conversation"
      }.md`,
    )
    .type("text/markdown");
  res.send(threadToMarkdown(t, label, await threads.historyAsync(t.id)));
});

// Turn timing stats for agents and the dashboard: metadata only (durations, tool names, counts, error classes).
const turnLatency = (from: number, to: number, thread?: string) => {
  const store = turnTiming();
  if (!store) return { available: false };
  let phone: { provider: string; total_ms: number }[] = [];
  try {
    if (!thread) phone = latency?.samples("first_token", from, to) ?? [];
  } catch {} // device samples are per provider, not per thread
  return { available: true, ...store.summary(from, to, { thread, phone }) };
};
app.get("/api/boot-timing", (_req, res) => {
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  res.json({ boots: boot.recent(20) });
});
app.get("/api/turn-latency", (req, res) => {
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  const to = Number(req.query.to) || Date.now();
  const from = Number(req.query.from) || to - 7 * 86_400_000;
  const thread =
    typeof req.query.thread === "string" && isId(req.query.thread) ? req.query.thread : undefined;
  // ?thread=&detail=N: an existing thread's last N turns (max 20) with their full timelines, for Run details' Full breakdown.
  const known =
    typeof req.query.thread === "string" && threads.get(req.query.thread)
      ? req.query.thread
      : undefined;
  if (req.query.detail != null) {
    const store = turnTiming();
    if (!known) return void res.status(404).json({ error: "Unknown thread." });
    try {
      return void res.json(
        store
          ? { available: true, turns: store.recent(known, Number(req.query.detail) || 10) }
          : { available: false },
      );
    } catch {
      return void res.status(503).json({ available: false });
    }
  }
  if (!(from < to) || to - from > 400 * 86_400_000)
    return void res.status(400).json({ error: "Choose a range of up to 400 days." });
  try {
    res.json(turnLatency(from, to, thread));
  } catch {
    res.status(503).json({ available: false });
  }
});

const EFFORT_TOGGLES = ["effort_schedule", ...EFFORT_STEPS.map((step) => `effort_${step}`)];
app.post("/api/automation/toggle", sameOrigin, express.json({ limit: "1kb" }), (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { target, enabled } = req.body ?? {};
    if (typeof enabled !== "boolean") throw new Error("Expected a boolean enabled.");
    if (EFFORT_TOGGLES.includes(target)) {
      // effort_schedule is the weekly auto-start; every other effort_* target is the step of that name
      const effort = readEffortConfig();
      if (target === "effort_schedule") effort.enabled = enabled;
      else effort.steps[target.slice("effort_".length) as EffortStep] = enabled;
      writeEffortConfig(effort);
      return void res.json(automation.snapshot());
    }
    if (!["dispatcher", "opener"].includes(target))
      throw new Error(
        "Expected target dispatcher, opener or an effort_ step and a boolean enabled.",
      );
    const file = path.join(DATA_DIR, "window-dispatch.json");
    let raw: any = {};
    try {
      raw = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8"));
    } catch {}
    if (target === "dispatcher") raw.enabled = enabled;
    else raw.opener = { ...raw.opener, enabled };
    fs.writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify(raw, null, 2) + "\n");
    res.json(automation.snapshot());
  } catch (e: any) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: String(e?.message ?? e) });
  }
});
// Run now: the Effort model retrain starts in the triage worker's script, detached, so a Console restart does not stop it.
app.post("/api/automation/run", sameOrigin, express.json({ limit: "1kb" }), (req, res) => {
  if (req.body?.pipeline !== "effort")
    return void res.status(400).json({ error: 'Expected pipeline "effort".' });
  const mock = process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_MOCK === "1";
  automationLog.record({
    step: "effort_schedule",
    kind: "info",
    msg: mock ? "Run now requested (mock: not started)" : "Run now requested",
  });
  if (!mock) {
    const child = spawn(
      process.execPath,
      [path.join(ROOT, "scripts", "github-triage.ts"), "calibrate", "--background"],
      { cwd: ROOT, detached: true, stdio: "ignore" },
    );
    child.on("error", (e) =>
      automationLog.record({
        step: "effort_schedule",
        kind: "fail",
        msg: `Run now could not start: ${e.message}`,
      }),
    );
    child.unref();
  }
  res.status(202).json({ v: 1, pipeline: "effort", started: !mock });
});

app.post("/api/automation/assign", sameOrigin, express.json({ limit: "4kb" }), async (req, res) => {
  const { v, repo, number, provider, model, effort, mode, claudeAccount } = req.body ?? {};
  const choice = { v, repo, number, provider, model, effort, mode, claudeAccount };
  // The dialog asks for NDJSON to draw each step live; the last line is the thread or the error.
  if (!String(req.headers.accept).includes("application/x-ndjson")) {
    try {
      return void res.json(await assignPremium(choice));
    } catch (e: any) {
      return void res.status(400).json({ error: String(e?.message ?? e) });
    }
  }
  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "application/x-ndjson");
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  res.setHeader("X-Accel-Buffering", "no");
  res.flushHeaders();
  const line = (msg: unknown) => {
    if (!res.writableEnded)
      res.write(JSON.stringify({ ...(msg as object), at: Date.now() }) + "\n");
  };
  try {
    line({ t: "done", thread: (await assignPremium(choice, line)).thread });
  } catch (e: any) {
    line({ t: "error", error: String(e?.message ?? e) });
  }
  res.end();
});

app.get("/api/dashboard", async (req, res) => {
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  const to = Number(req.query.to) || Date.now();
  const from = Number(req.query.from) || to - 7 * 86_400_000;
  if (!(from < to) || to - from > 400 * 86_400_000)
    return void res.status(400).json({ error: "Choose a range of up to 400 days." });
  try {
    const d = await dashboard(from, to);
    const titles: Record<string, string> = {};
    const cwds: Record<string, string | null> = {};
    for (const t of d.turns) {
      titles[t.thread] ??= threads.get(t.thread)?.title ?? "Deleted thread";
      cwds[t.thread] ??= threads.get(t.thread)?.cwd ?? null;
    }
    let turnLatencyStats: any = { available: false };
    try {
      turnLatencyStats = turnLatency(from, to);
    } catch {}
    res.json({ ...d, titles, cwds, latency: turnLatencyStats });
  } catch {
    res.status(503).json({ error: "Dashboard data is unavailable." });
  }
});
