app.get("/uploads/:thread/:id/:name", (req, res) => {
  const { thread, id, name } = req.params;
  if (!isId(thread) || !isId(id) || !SAFE_NAME.test(name)) return void res.status(404).end();
  const type = sniffImageFile(path.join(UPLOAD_DIR, thread, id, name));
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "private, max-age=86400, immutable");
  // only verified images render inline; anything else (html, svg, ...) is a download, so an upload can never run as a page on this origin
  if (type) res.type(type);
  else res.attachment(name).type("application/octet-stream");
  // `root` matters: without it send() 404s any absolute path with a dot segment, and the data dir lives under ~/.config
  res.sendFile(
    `${thread}/${id}/${name}`,
    { root: UPLOAD_DIR },
    (err) => err && !res.headersSent && res.status(404).end(),
  );
});
app.use((err: any, req: express.Request, res: express.Response, next: express.NextFunction) => {
  if (!req.path.startsWith("/uploads/")) return next(err);
  res.status(err?.status ?? 400).json({
    error:
      err?.type === "entity.too.large"
        ? `Files must be under ${MAX_FILE_BYTES >> 20} MB.`
        : "Upload failed.",
  });
});
// The ```arch route worker runs libavoid's embind glue, which needs eval. Only the worker script (and the module it
// imports) gets a CSP allowing it; Chromium applies a dedicated worker's own response CSP, the page CSP stays strict.
const ARCH_WORKER_SCRIPTS = new Set(["/arch-route-worker.js", "/vendor/libavoid/index.js"]);
app.use((req, res, next) => {
  if (ARCH_WORKER_SCRIPTS.has(req.path))
    res.setHeader(
      "Content-Security-Policy",
      "default-src 'none'; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-eval' 'wasm-unsafe-eval'; connect-src 'self'",
    );
  next();
});
app.use(express.static(path.join(ROOT, "public"), { index: "index.html", extensions: ["html"] }));

boot.mark("routes");
const wss = new WebSocketServer({
  noServer: true,
  maxPayload: 1 << 20,
  // Browser-supported compression cuts history and tool traffic on remote/mobile links.
  // Independent messages bound memory and avoid retaining prior message dictionaries.
  perMessageDeflate: {
    serverNoContextTakeover: true,
    clientNoContextTakeover: true,
    concurrencyLimit: 2,
    threshold: 1024,
    zlibDeflateOptions: { level: 3 },
  },
});

function upgrade(req: http.IncomingMessage, socket: Duplex, head: Buffer) {
  const okAuth = authed(req);
  // block cross-site websocket hijacking: Origin host must match the Host header
  let okOrigin = false;
  try {
    okOrigin = new URL(String(req.headers.origin)).host === req.headers.host;
  } catch {}
  if (req.url !== "/ws" || !okAuth || !okOrigin) {
    socket.write("HTTP/1.1 403 Forbidden\r\n\r\n");
    return void socket.destroy();
  }
  wss.handleUpgrade(req, socket, head, (ws) => wss.emit("connection", ws));
}

function safeCwd(p: unknown): string {
  if (typeof p !== "string") throw new Error("Invalid directory.");
  const r = path.resolve(p);
  if (r !== HOME && !r.startsWith(HOME + path.sep))
    throw new Error("Directory must be inside your home folder.");
  if (!fs.statSync(r, { throwIfNoEntry: false })?.isDirectory())
    throw new Error("Directory does not exist.");
  return r;
}

function dirs(): string[] {
  const out = [REPOS];
  try {
    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(REPOS, { withFileTypes: true })) {
      if (e.isDirectory() && !e.name.startsWith(".")) out.push(path.join(REPOS, e.name));
    }
  } catch {}
  return out;
}

// Client message ids already handled: a message resent after a reconnect (its ack was lost) is acknowledged, not run twice.
const seenCids = new Map<string, number>();
const firstDelivery = (cid: string) => {
  if (seenCids.has(cid)) return false;
  seenCids.set(cid, Date.now());
  if (seenCids.size > 2000) seenCids.delete(seenCids.keys().next().value!);
  return true;
};

wss.on("connection", (ws) => {
  clients.add(ws);
  void refreshRouterUsage(); // quota refresh only on demand; no idle network polling
  void refreshDeepSeekUsage();
  const reply = (m: unknown) => ws.readyState === ws.OPEN && ws.send(JSON.stringify(m));
  reply({
    t: "hello",
    build: BUILD,
    features: {
      piRateLimit: 1,
      autoIsolation: 1,
      acks: true,
      queueEditing: true,
      questions: true,
      latency: !!latency,
      selectiveEvents: true,
    },
    questions: threads.getQuestions(),
    tasks: schedules.list(),
    usage: usage.all(),
    commands: threads.getCommands(),
    threads: threads.list(),
    queues: threads.getQueues(),
    dirs: dirs(),
    providers: providerInfo(),
  });
  let metricsWindow = Date.now(),
    metricsCount = 0;
  // A socket that misses a whole ping interval is dead (phone slept, network changed): drop it so its client reconnects cleanly.
  let isAlive = true;
  ws.on("pong", () => (isAlive = true));
  const ping = setInterval(() => {
    if (!isAlive) return void ws.terminate();
    isAlive = false;
    if (ws.readyState === ws.OPEN) ws.ping();
  }, Number(process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_WS_PING_MS) || 25_000);
  ws.on("close", () => {
    clearInterval(ping);
    clients.delete(ws);
  });
  ws.on("message", (raw) => {
    isAlive = true;
    let m: any;
    try {
      m = JSON.parse(String(raw));
      if (m?.thread != null && !safeId(m.thread)) throw new Error("Invalid thread id.");
      if (m?.t === "read" && m.id != null && !safeId(m.id)) throw new Error("Invalid thread id.");
      switch (m.t) {
        case "ping":
          return reply({ t: "pong" });
        case "telemetry": {
          if (Date.now() - metricsWindow > 60_000) {
            metricsWindow = Date.now();
            metricsCount = 0;
          }
          if (
            !Array.isArray(m.samples) ||
            m.samples.length > 20 ||
            (metricsCount += m.samples.length) > 200
          )
            return;
          try {
            latency?.record(m.samples, (id) => threads.get(id));
          } catch {
            console.warn("Local latency sample write failed.");
          }
          return;
        }
        case "refresh_usage":
          void refreshUsage();
          return;
        case "answer": {
          try {
            threads.answer(m.thread, m.id, m.answers, m.skip === true);
            return reply({ t: "answer_done", thread: m.thread, id: m.id, request: m.request });
          } catch (e: any) {
            return reply({
              t: "answer_error",
              thread: m.thread,
              id: m.id,
              request: m.request,
              message: e?.message || "Could not send your answer.",
            });
          }
        }
        case "create_issue": {
          const cid = typeof m.cid === "string" ? m.cid : undefined;
          createIssueDraft(threads.get(m.thread)?.cwd, m.draft).then(
            (draft) => reply({ t: "create_issue_done", cid, ...draft }),
            (e: any) =>
              reply({
                t: "create_issue_error",
                cid,
                message: e?.message || "Could not create the issue draft.",
              }),
          );
          return;
        }
        case "queue_edit":
        case "queue_save":
        case "queue_cancel_edit": {
          try {
            if (m.t === "queue_edit")
              return reply({
                t: "queue_edit",
                request: m.request,
                thread: m.thread,
                ...threads.editQueued(m.thread, m.id),
              });
            if (m.t === "queue_save") threads.saveQueued(m.thread, m.id, m.revision, m.text);
            else threads.cancelQueuedEdit(m.thread, m.id, m.revision);
            return reply({ t: "queue_edit_done", request: m.request, thread: m.thread, id: m.id });
          } catch (e: any) {
            return reply({
              t: "queue_edit_error",
              request: m.request,
              message: e?.message || "Could not update this queued message.",
            });
          }
        }
        case "subscribe":
          if (threads.get(m.thread)) subscriptions.set(ws, m.thread);
          return;
        case "open":
          if (m.foregroundOnly === true && threads.get(m.thread)) subscriptions.set(ws, m.thread);
          return reply({
            t: "history",
            thread: m.thread,
            request: m.request,
            before: Number.isInteger(m.before) ? m.before : null,
            ...threads.page(
              m.thread,
              Number.isInteger(m.before) ? m.before : undefined,
              Number.isInteger(m.since) && m.since >= 0 ? m.since : undefined,
              m.small === true,
            ),
          });
        case "create": {
          const t = threads.create({
            provider: m.provider,
            claudeAccount: typeof m.claudeAccount === "string" ? m.claudeAccount : undefined,
            cwd: safeCwd(m.cwd),
            mode: m.mode,
            model: m.model,
            effort: m.effort,
            title: m.title,
          });
          return reply({ t: "created", thread: t });
        }
        case "send":
          if (typeof m.cid === "string" && !firstDelivery(m.cid))
            return reply({ t: "ack", cid: m.cid });
          if (
            typeof m.text === "string" &&
            (m.text.trim() || (Array.isArray(m.attachments) && m.attachments.length))
          ) {
            threads.send(m.thread, m.text.slice(0, 100_000), {
              attachments: m.attachments,
              mode: m.mode === "steer" ? "steer" : "queue",
            });
          }
          return typeof m.cid === "string" && reply({ t: "ack", cid: m.cid });
        case "steer_queued":
          return threads.steerQueued(m.thread, m.id);
        case "compact":
          return threads.compact(m.thread);
        case "unqueue":
          return threads.unqueue(m.thread, String(m.id));
        case "fork":
          return reply({
            t: "created",
            thread: threads.fork(
              m.thread,
              typeof m.provider === "string" ? m.provider : undefined,
              m.context === "recent",
              {
                ...Object.fromEntries(
                  ["model", "effort", "mode", "claudeAccount"]
                    .filter((k) => typeof m[k] === "string")
                    .map((k) => [k, m[k]]),
                ),
              },
            ),
          });
        case "abort":
          if (typeof m.cid === "string" && !firstDelivery(m.cid))
            return reply({ t: "ack", cid: m.cid });
          threads.abort(m.thread);
          return typeof m.cid === "string" && reply({ t: "ack", cid: m.cid });
        case "warm":
          if (typeof m.thread === "string") threads.warm(m.thread);
          return;
        case "read":
          if (typeof m.id === "string") threads.markRead(m.id);
          return;
        case "update": {
          return threads.update(m.thread, {
            mode: typeof m.mode === "string" ? m.mode : undefined,
            model: typeof m.model === "string" ? m.model : undefined,
            effort: typeof m.effort === "string" ? m.effort : undefined,
            claudeAccount: typeof m.claudeAccount === "string" ? m.claudeAccount : undefined,
            advisor: typeof m.advisor === "string" ? m.advisor : undefined,
            idleCompaction: typeof m.idleCompaction === "boolean" ? m.idleCompaction : undefined,
            title: typeof m.title === "string" ? m.title : undefined,
          });
        }
        case "task_save": {
          const input = {
            title: m.title,
            prompt: m.prompt,
            provider: m.provider,
            model: m.model,
            effort: m.effort,
            mode: m.mode,
            cwd: m.cwd === undefined ? undefined : safeCwd(m.cwd),
            repeat: m.repeat,
            time: m.time,
            day: m.day,
            tz: m.tz,
            enabled: typeof m.enabled === "boolean" ? m.enabled : undefined,
            saveOutput: typeof m.saveOutput === "boolean" ? m.saveOutput : undefined,
          };
          const task =
            typeof m.id === "string" ? schedules.update(m.id, input) : schedules.create(input);
          return reply({ t: "task_saved", request: m.request, task });
        }
        case "task_delete":
          return schedules.remove(String(m.id));
        case "task_run": {
          const run = schedules.run(String(m.id), "manual");
          if (run.status === "skipped") return reply({ t: "error", message: run.reason });
          if (run.status === "failed") return reply({ t: "error", message: run.reason });
          return run.thread && reply({ t: "created", thread: threads.get(run.thread) });
        }
        case "archive":
          return threads.archive(m.thread, m.archived !== false);
        case "delete":
          return threads.remove(m.thread);
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      reply({
        t: "error",
        message: e?.message ?? String(e),
        ...(typeof m?.cid === "string" ? { cid: m.cid } : {}),
      });
    }
  });
});

if (acodeSettings) {
  const bridgeServer = acodePublicApp(acode, ROOT).listen(acodeSettings.port, HOST);
  // a scratch server reads the same env file as the live one: exiting here made agents hunt down the port's owner, the live server
  bridgeServer.on("error", () =>
    console.error(
      `Acode bridge port ${acodeSettings.port} is in use (normally by the live server); Acode Edit is off in this one.`,
    ),
  );
}

// Runs once the state and routes exist; the port is already bound, so this is the old listen callback.
function startServing() {
  const defaultDataDir = path.join(os.homedir(), ".config", "agent-console");
  repairUsageHistory({
    pidFile: PID_FILE || (DATA_DIR === defaultDataDir ? path.join(RUN_DIR, "pid") : undefined),
  });
  if (PID_FILE) fs.writeFileSync(PID_FILE, String(process.pid)); // only the process that holds the port may claim it
  serving = true;
  markReady(); // queued requests and upgrades run once this synchronous block yields
  startModelRateBroker(PORT); // same rule: a scratch server that lost the port must not repoint the live broker
  startHostSampler(); // 5 s host samples for the Host page's history charts
  workspaceMonitor?.start(); // reads those samples; alerts and escalation (docs/monitoring.md)
  boot.mark("listening");
  boot.flush("listen");
  console.log(`agent-console listening on http://${HOST}:${PORT}`);
  console.log(
    CODER_HOSTS.length
      ? `auth: Coder SSO for ${CODER_HOSTS.join(", ")} (password cookie still works elsewhere); proxy proof ${PROXY_PROOF_ON ? "on" : "OFF (SSO disabled, fail closed)"}`
      : "auth: password only",
  );
  if (process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_SHOW_PASSWORD) console.log(`password: ${PASSWORD}`);
  // Connected clients may have received the previous process's cached model list.
  void refreshPiModels()
    .then(() => broadcast({ t: "providers", providers: providerInfo() }))
    .catch(() => console.warn("Pi model refresh failed."));
  // Boot work nothing waits on runs once the first page has had its turn on the event loop.
  setTimeout(() => {
    // Never wait for the proxy: a cold Headroom launch takes up to 10 s, and turns go direct until it is healthy.
    // A proxy the previous server left exiting must be gone first, or it would look like an external one.
    void headroom
      .reapOrphan(ORPHAN_FILE)
      .then(() => headroom.start())
      .catch(() => console.warn("Headroom proxy failed to start."));
    boot.mark("headroom-launched");
    threads.restoreTelemetry();
    boot.mark("telemetry-restored");
    threads.restoreIdleCompaction();
    boot.mark("idle-compaction-restored");
    threads.warmAccounting();
    boot.mark("accounting-warm");
    void sessionSearch
      .warm()
      .catch(() => console.warn("Session search index will retry on the next query."));
    boot.flush("deferred");
  }, 1000).unref();
}

// A hash of the code this process loaded. Open tabs compare it on reconnect and reload themselves when the server was replaced
// (`bin/agent-console restart-when-idle` -> scripts/idle-restart.mjs).
const sources = (dir: string): string[] =>
  fs
    .readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })
    .flatMap((e) =>
      e.name === "fonts" || e.name === "node_modules"
        ? []
        : e.isDirectory()
          ? sources(path.join(dir, e.name))
          : [path.join(dir, e.name)],
    );
// The release sha from RELEASE.json, when a release wrote one. Missing or unreadable file: null.
function releaseSha(): string | null {
  try {
    const sha = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, "RELEASE.json"), "utf8"))?.sha;
    return typeof sha === "string" && sha.length > 0 ? sha : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
function buildId() {
  const sha = releaseSha();
  if (sha) return sha.slice(0, 12);
  const h = crypto.createHash("sha1");
  for (const f of [
    ...sources(path.join(ROOT, "server")),
    ...sources(path.join(ROOT, "public")),
  ].sort()) {
    const st = fs.statSync(f);
    h.update(`${f}:${st.mtimeMs}:${st.size}`);
  }
  return h.digest("hex").slice(0, 12);
}
const BUILD = buildId();

// Standby (#348): announce readiness with our pid, then block until the supervisor writes `go` (stdin EOF first: give up).
function waitForGo(runDir: string): Promise<void> {
  fs.mkdirSync(runDir, { recursive: true });
  const readyFile = path.join(runDir, "standby-ready");
  fs.writeFileSync(`${readyFile}.tmp`, String(process.pid));
  fs.renameSync(`${readyFile}.tmp`, readyFile); // the supervisor never reads a half-written pid
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    let pending = "";
    let started = false;
    const onData = (chunk: string) => {
      pending += chunk;
      const lines = pending.split("\n");
      pending = lines.pop() ?? "";
      if (started || !lines.some((line) => line.trim() === "go")) return;
      started = true;
      process.stdin.off("data", onData).pause();
      resolve();
    };
    const onEnd = () => {
      if (started) return;
      console.error("standby: stdin closed before go; exiting");
      process.exit(1);
    };
    process.stdin.setEncoding("utf8").on("data", onData).on("end", onEnd).on("error", onEnd);
  });
}

const ORPHAN_FILE = path.join(RUN_DIR, "headroom-orphan.pid");
const RESTART_CLOSE_CODE = 1012; // WebSocket "service restart": tabs reconnect to the replacement server
const settled = (work: Promise<unknown>, ms: number) =>
  Promise.race([work.catch(() => {}), new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms))]);

// Node cannot tell who sent the signal; the sender is usually among the newest processes, so log those
function newestProcesses(): string {
  try {
    return execFileSync("ps", ["-eo", "pid,ppid,etime,args", "--sort=-start_time"], {
      encoding: "utf8",
    })
      .split("\n")
      .slice(0, 16)
      .map((line) => line.slice(0, 240))
      .join("\n");
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
}

// Stop accepting first, then tell every tab to reconnect; resolves when the tabs answered the close.
function closeClients(): Promise<unknown> {
  server.close();
  const closed = [...clients].map(
    (socket) =>
      new Promise((resolve) => {
        socket.once("close", resolve);
        socket.close(RESTART_CLOSE_CODE, "server restart");
      }),
  );
  server.closeAllConnections();
  return Promise.all(closed);
}

// Fast exit for a restart handoff: persist, release the port and children, never wait on Headroom's slow exit.
async function shutdown(sig: string) {
  const answered = closeClients();
  const handoff = fs.existsSync(path.join(RUN_DIR, "handoff"));
  const newest = handoff ? "(restart handoff)" : newestProcesses();
  console.log(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] stopping on ${sig}; newest processes:\n${newest}`);
  schedules.shutdown();
  threads.shutdown();
  headroom.release(ORPHAN_FILE); // the next server waits for it, or kills it 5 s after this file was written
  benchmarks.close();
  workspaceMonitor?.stop();
  await Promise.all([settled(transcriptRedaction.close(), 200), settled(answered, 100)]);
  latency?.close();
  process.exit(0);
}

let shuttingDown = false;
for (const sig of ["SIGINT", "SIGTERM"] as const) {
  process.on(sig, () => {
    if (shuttingDown) return;
    shuttingDown = true;
    void shutdown(sig);
  });
}
startServing();

File: server/latency.ts
================
import fs from "node:fs";
import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";

// first_token: send tap -> first reply token painted on this device (wait_ms = attachment uploads still in flight at the tap)
const KINDS = new Set([
  "open",
  "page",
  "cached",
  "refresh",
  "cancelled",
  "viewer_open",
  "viewer_tree",
  "viewer_module",
  "first_token",
]);
const STAGES = [
  "server_ms",
  "render_ms",
  "wait_ms",
  "prepare_ms",
  "roundtrip_ms",
  "decode_ms",
  "module_ms",
];
const duration = (n: unknown) =>
  typeof n === "number" && Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= 300_000;
const size = (n: unknown, max: number) =>
  typeof n === "number" && Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 0 && n <= max;

function validLatencyShape(sample: any) {
  if (!size(sample.width, 16384) || !sample.width || !size(sample.height, 16384) || !sample.height)
    return false;
  if (STAGES.some((stage) => sample[stage] != null && !duration(sample[stage]))) return false;
  if (sample.events != null && !size(sample.events, 100_000)) return false;
  return !["bytes", "wire_bytes"].some(
    (field) => sample[field] != null && !size(sample[field], 64 * 1024 * 1024),
  );
}

function latencyRow(
  sample: any,
  thread: (id: string) => { provider: string } | undefined,
  now: number,
): any[] | undefined {
  if (
    !sample ||
    !KINDS.has(sample.kind) ||
    typeof sample.thread !== "string" ||
    !duration(sample.total_ms)
  )
    return undefined;
  const owner =
    sample.thread === "viewer:repos" && sample.kind.startsWith("viewer_")
      ? { provider: "workspace" }
      : thread(sample.thread);
  if (!owner || !validLatencyShape(sample)) return undefined;
  return [
    now,
    sample.thread,
    owner.provider,
    sample.kind,
    sample.total_ms,
    sample.server_ms ?? null,
    sample.render_ms ?? null,
    sample.wait_ms ?? null,
    sample.events ?? null,
    sample.bytes ?? null,
    sample.wire_bytes ?? null,
    sample.prepare_ms ?? null,
    sample.roundtrip_ms ?? null,
    sample.decode_ms ?? null,
    sample.module_ms ?? null,
    sample.width,
    sample.height,
    sample.touch === true ? 1 : 0,
    sample.hidden === true ? 1 : 0,
  ];
}

export const RETENTION_DAYS = 7;
const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;

// Local, bounded measurements only. Never accept arbitrary metadata, URLs, prompts or browser fingerprints.
export class LatencyStore {
  private db: DatabaseSync;
  private writes = 0;
  private maxSamples: number;
  constructor(file: string, maxSamples = 20_000) {
    this.maxSamples = maxSamples;
    fs.closeSync(fs.openSync(file, "a", 0o600));
    fs.chmodSync(file, 0o600);
    this.db = new DatabaseSync(file);
    this.db.exec(`PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL; CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS latency (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, at INTEGER NOT NULL, thread TEXT NOT NULL, provider TEXT NOT NULL,
      kind TEXT NOT NULL, total_ms REAL NOT NULL, server_ms REAL, render_ms REAL, wait_ms REAL,
      events INTEGER, bytes INTEGER, wire_bytes INTEGER, prepare_ms REAL, roundtrip_ms REAL, decode_ms REAL, module_ms REAL, width INTEGER NOT NULL, height INTEGER NOT NULL,
      touch INTEGER NOT NULL, hidden INTEGER NOT NULL
    ); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS latency_at ON latency(at);`);
    const columns = new Set(
      this.db
        .prepare("PRAGMA table_info(latency)")
        .all()
        .map((c: any) => c.name),
    );
    for (const name of ["wire_bytes", "prepare_ms", "roundtrip_ms", "decode_ms", "module_ms"])
      if (!columns.has(name)) this.db.exec(`ALTER TABLE latency ADD COLUMN ${name} REAL`);
    this.prune();
  }
  record(
    samples: unknown,
    thread: (id: string) => { provider: string } | undefined,
    now = Date.now(),
  ) {
    if (!Array.isArray(samples) || samples.length > 20) return 0;
    const rows: any[][] = [];
    for (const sample of samples) {
      const row = latencyRow(sample, thread, now);
      if (row) rows.push(row);
    }
    if (!rows.length) return 0;
    const insert = this.db.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO latency(at,thread,provider,kind,total_ms,server_ms,render_ms,wait_ms,events,bytes,wire_bytes,prepare_ms,roundtrip_ms,decode_ms,module_ms,width,height,touch,hidden) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
    );
    this.db.exec("BEGIN");
    try {
      for (const row of rows) insert.run(...row);
      this.db.exec("COMMIT");
    } catch (error) {
      this.db.exec("ROLLBACK");
      throw error;
    }
    this.writes += rows.length;
    if (this.writes >= 100) {
      this.writes = 0;
      this.prune(now);
    }
    return rows.length;
  }
  private prune(now = Date.now()) {
    this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM latency WHERE at < ?").run(now - RETENTION_DAYS * DAY_MS);
    this.db
      .prepare(
        "DELETE FROM latency WHERE id <= (SELECT id FROM latency ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1 OFFSET ?)",
      )
      .run(this.maxSamples);
  }
  summary(now = Date.now()) {
    this.prune(now);
    const rows = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM latency ORDER BY total_ms").all() as any[];
    const groups = new Map<string, any[]>();
    const add = (key: string, row: any) => {
      const group = groups.get(key) || [];
      group.push(row);
      groups.set(key, group);
    };
    for (const row of rows) {
      add(row.kind, row);
      if (!row.hidden) add(`${row.kind}/visible`, row);
      add(
        `${row.kind}/${row.provider}/${row.width <= 600 ? "mobile" : row.width <= 1024 ? "tablet" : "desktop"}/${row.hidden ? "background" : "visible"}`,
        row,
      );
    }
    // latest 20 foreground samples per kind: shows a fix (or a regression) that the retention-window pool hides
    for (const [key, group] of [...groups].filter(
      ([key]) => key.endsWith("/visible") && key.split("/").length === 2,
    ))
      groups.set(
        `${key}/recent`,
        [...group]
          .sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at || b.id - a.id)
          .slice(0, 20)
          .sort((a, b) => a.total_ms - b.total_ms),
      );
    const stats = (group: any[]) => ({
      count: group.length,
      median_ms: group[Math.floor((group.length - 1) * 0.5)].total_ms,
      p95_ms: group[Math.ceil(group.length * 0.95) - 1].total_ms,
      max_ms: group.at(-1).total_ms,
      mean_ms: Math.round(group.reduce((n, r) => n + r.total_ms, 0) / group.length),
    });
    // Viewer stages expose cold-module, transport, decode and drawing costs independently.
    // Missing stages stay absent, including on databases created before viewer measurements.
    const viewerStats = (group: any[]) => ({
      stages: Object.fromEntries(
        STAGES.flatMap((key) => {
          const values = group
            .filter((row) => row[key] != null)
            .map((row) => ({ total_ms: row[key] }))
            .sort((a, b) => a.total_ms - b.total_ms);
          return values.length ? [[key, stats(values)]] : [];
        }),
      ),
      counts: Object.fromEntries(
        ["events", "bytes", "wire_bytes"].flatMap((key) => {
          const values = group.filter((row) => row[key] != null).map((row) => row[key]);
          return values.length
            ? [
                [
                  key,
                  {
                    count: values.length,
                    total: values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0),
                    max: Math.max(...values),
                  },
                ],
              ]
            : [];
        }),
      ),
    });
    // The Run details table picks its own percentiles, so its two windows carry every sorted time (whole ms keep it small).
    const shown = (key: string) => /^[^/]+\/visible(\/recent)?$/.test(key);
    return {
      retention_days: RETENTION_DAYS,
      max_samples: this.maxSamples,
      count: rows.length,
      groups: Object.fromEntries(
        [...groups].map(([key, values]) => [
          key,
          {
            ...stats(values),
            ...(key.startsWith("viewer_") ? viewerStats(values) : {}),
            ...(shown(key) ? { samples_ms: values.map((r) => Math.round(r.total_ms)) } : {}),
          },
        ]),
      ),
      slowest: rows.slice(-10).reverse(),
      recent: this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM latency ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 20").all(),
    };
  }
  // Foreground samples of one kind in a window, for the dashboard's per-provider percentiles.
  samples(kind: string, from: number, to: number) {
    return this.db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT provider, total_ms FROM latency WHERE kind = ? AND hidden = 0 AND at BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY total_ms",
      )
      .all(kind, from, to) as { provider: string; total_ms: number }[];
  }
  close() {
    this.db.close();
  }
}

================
File: server/log-stamp.ts
================
import { StringDecoder } from "node:string_decoder";

type Write = (chunk: any, encoding?: any, callback?: any) => boolean;
type Stream = { write: Write };

// ISO 8601 with milliseconds and the local UTC offset, e.g. 2026-10-25T15:24:46.123+00:00 (same shape as bin/agent-console).
export function isoNow(date = new Date()): string {
  const pad = (value: number, width = 2) => String(value).padStart(width, "0");
  const offset = -date.getTimezoneOffset();
  const sign = offset < 0 ? "-" : "+";
  const abs = Math.abs(offset);
  return (
    `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())}` +
    `T${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}:${pad(date.getSeconds())}.${pad(date.getMilliseconds(), 3)}` +
    `${sign}${pad(Math.floor(abs / 60))}:${pad(abs % 60)}`
  );
}

// Wraps stream.write so every output line starts with a timestamp; the rest of each line is unchanged.
// Returns a function that restores the original write.
export function stampLines(stream: Stream, now: () => string = isoNow): () => void {
  const original = stream.write;
  const decoder = new StringDecoder("utf8");
  let atLineStart = true;
  stream.write = function (this: unknown, chunk: any, encoding?: any, callback?: any) {
    if (typeof chunk !== "string" && !(chunk instanceof Uint8Array))
      return original.call(this, chunk, encoding, callback);
    const text = typeof chunk === "string" ? chunk : decoder.write(Buffer.from(chunk));
    let out = "";
    for (const part of text.split(/(?<=\n)/)) {
      if (atLineStart) out += `${now()} `;
      out += part;
      atLineStart = part.endsWith("\n");
    }
    const writeDone = typeof encoding === "function" ? encoding : callback;
    return original.call(this, out, "utf8", writeDone);
  } as Write;
  return () => {
    stream.write = original;
  };
}

================
File: server/questions.ts

import {
  type Agent,
  cleanQuestions,
  type Question,
  type QuestionReply,
} from "./providers/types.ts";

export interface PendingQuestion {
  type: "question";
  id: string;
  questions: Question[];
  ts?: number;
  async?: true;
}

function validateAnswerValues(question: Question, values: unknown): string[] {
  if (
    !Array.isArray(values) ||
    !values.length ||
    values.length > 20 ||
    values.some((value) => typeof value !== "string" || !value.trim() || value.length > 10_000)
  )
    throw new Error("Answer each question before continuing.");
  if (!question.multiSelect && values.length !== 1)
    throw new Error("Choose one answer for this question.");
  if (new Set(values).size !== values.length) throw new Error("Duplicate answer.");
  if (
    question.options.length &&
    !question.allowOther &&
    values.some((value) => !question.options.some((option) => option.label === value))
  )
    throw new Error("Choose one of the available answers.");
  return values;
}

function collectAnswers(
  request: PendingQuestion,
  raw: Record<string, unknown>,
  answers: Record<string, string[]>,
) {
  for (const key of Object.keys(raw))
    if (!request.questions.some((question) => question.id === key))
      throw new Error("Unknown question.");
  for (const question of request.questions)
    answers[question.id] = validateAnswerValues(question, raw[question.id]);
}

function buildAnswerEvent(
  request: PendingQuestion,
  requestId: string,
  answers: Record<string, string[]>,
  skip: boolean,
  note: string | undefined,
) {
  const visible = Object.fromEntries(
    request.questions
      .filter((question) => answers[question.id])
      .map((question) => [question.id, question.secret ? ["••••••"] : answers[question.id]]),
  );
  return {
    type: "question_answer",
    id: requestId,
    answers: visible,
    ...(skip ? { skip: true } : {}),
    ...(note !== undefined
      ? { note: request.questions.some((question) => question.secret) ? "••••••" : note }
      : {}),
    ts: Date.now(),
  };
}

// Blocking requests belong to a live process; async messages remain answerable after it exits.
export class Questions {
  private threads = new Map<string, Map<string, PendingQuestion>>();
  list(thread: string): PendingQuestion[] {
    return [...(this.threads.get(thread)?.values() ?? [])];
  }
  all() {
    return Object.fromEntries([...this.threads].map(([id]) => [id, this.list(id)]));
  }
  add(thread: string, event: any): PendingQuestion {
    if (typeof event.id !== "string" || !event.id || event.id.length > 500)
      throw new Error("Invalid question request.");
    const questions = cleanQuestions(event.questions);
    if (!questions.length || new Set(questions.map((q) => q.id)).size !== questions.length)
      throw new Error("Invalid question request.");
    if (event.async && questions.some((q) => q.secret))
      throw new Error("Secret fields require a blocking question.");
    const pending = this.threads.get(thread) ?? new Map();
    if (pending.has(event.id)) throw new Error("Duplicate question request.");
    const request = { ...event, type: "question" as const, questions };
    pending.set(event.id, request);
    this.threads.set(thread, pending);
    return request;
  }
  remove(thread: string, id: string) {
    const pending = this.threads.get(thread);
    const request = pending?.get(id);
    pending?.delete(id);
    if (!pending?.size) this.threads.delete(thread);
    return request;
  }
  answer(
    thread: string,
    id: string,
    raw: unknown,
    agent?: Agent,
    skip = false,
    note?: string,
    deliver?: (reply: QuestionReply) => boolean,
  ) {
    const request = this.threads.get(thread)?.get(id);
    if (!request || (!deliver && (!agent?.alive || !agent.answer)))
      throw new Error("This question is no longer waiting for an answer.");
    if (!skip && note === undefined && (!raw || typeof raw !== "object" || Array.isArray(raw)))
      throw new Error("Invalid answers.");
    const answers: Record<string, string[]> = Object.create(null);
    if (!skip && note === undefined)
      collectAnswers(request, raw as Record<string, unknown>, answers);
    const reply: QuestionReply = {
      answers,
      ...(skip ? { skip: true } : {}),
      ...(note !== undefined ? { note } : {}),
    };
    // Remove first: an adapter may synchronously emit completion or a result while writing the response.
    this.remove(thread, id);
    let consumed: boolean;
    try {
      consumed = deliver ? deliver(reply) : agent!.answer!(id, reply);
    } catch (error) {
      this.add(thread, request);
      throw error;
    }
    return {
      consumed,
      event: buildAnswerEvent(request, id, answers, skip, note),
    };
  }
}

================
File: server/session-search.ts
================
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import fs from "node:fs";
import fsp from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
import { isPrCreate, parsePrRef, prsFromResult } from "./session-prs.ts";

const MAX_TEXT = 128_000;
const MAX_LINE = 8 * 1024 * 1024;
const bounded = (value: unknown, fallback: number, max: number) =>
  typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)
    ? Math.max(1, Math.min(max, Math.floor(value)))
    : fallback;
const SYNC_WAIT_MS = 3000;
const BACKGROUND_START_MS = 500;
const BACKGROUND_EVERY_MS = 60_000;
/** Names of the history tools themselves: `session_search`, `mcp__agent_console__session_search`, `agent_console:session_read`. */
const ECHO_TOOL = /(?:^|[^a-z0-9])session_(?:search|read)$/i;
const isEchoName = (name: unknown) => typeof name === "string" && ECHO_TOOL.test(name);
/** Already indexed rows: a tool call starts with its name; results carry the JSON shape session_search/session_read return. */
function isEchoRowText(text: string) {
  if (isEchoName(text.split("\n", 1)[0])) return true;
  if (!text.includes('"citation"')) return false;
  return (
    text.includes('"indexedSessions"') || (text.includes('"events"') && text.includes('"archived"'))
  );
}
const safeId = (id: unknown): id is string => typeof id === "string" && /^[\w-]{1,200}$/.test(id);

type TextCollector = { parts: string[]; length: number };
const SECRET_KEY = /^(data|image|source|signature|api_key|token|authorization|password|secret)$/i;
function addText(collector: TextCollector, value: unknown) {
  if (typeof value === "string" && collector.length < MAX_TEXT) {
    const text = value.slice(0, MAX_TEXT - collector.length);
    collector.parts.push(text);
    collector.length += text.length;
  }
}
function addToolObject(collector: TextCollector, value: any, depth: number) {
  if (value.type === "image" || value.type === "image_url") return;
  for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value))
    if (!SECRET_KEY.test(key)) addToolValue(collector, child, depth + 1);
}
function addToolValue(collector: TextCollector, value: any, depth = 0) {
  if (depth > 8 || collector.length >= MAX_TEXT) return;
  if (typeof value === "string") addText(collector, value);
  else if (Array.isArray(value)) for (const item of value) addToolValue(collector, item, depth + 1);
  else if (value && typeof value === "object") addToolObject(collector, value, depth);
}
// Returns "tool" when the block belongs to a tool call, otherwise undefined.
function addBlock(collector: TextCollector, block: any) {
  if (block?.type === "text") addText(collector, block.text);
  else if (block?.type === "tool_use") {
    addText(collector, block.name);
    addToolValue(collector, block.input);
    return "tool";
  } else if (block?.type === "tool_result") {
    addToolValue(collector, block.content);
    return "tool";
  }
  return undefined;
}

// Index displayed conversation content, not credentials, images, provider packets, or token accounting.
function eventText(event: any): { role: string; text: string } | null {
  if (!event || typeof event !== "object") return null;
  if (event.type === "user_input" && typeof event.text === "string")
    return { role: "user", text: event.text.slice(0, MAX_TEXT) };
  if (event.type !== "assistant" && event.type !== "user") return null;
  const collector: TextCollector = { parts: [], length: 0 };
  let role = event.type === "assistant" ? "assistant" : "user";
  const content = event.message?.content;
  if (typeof content === "string") addText(collector, content);
  else if (Array.isArray(content))
    for (const block of content) role = addBlock(collector, block) ?? role;
  const text = collector.parts.join("\n").trim();
  return text ? { role, text } : null;
}

export type SessionSearchHooks = {
  beforeRefresh?: () => Promise<void>;
  /** Pause the echo cleanup after this many batches (tests). */
  cleanupBatchLimit?: number;
};
const ECHO_BATCH_MS = 50;
const ECHO_PREFILTER = `(text LIKE '%session_search%' OR text LIKE '%session_read%' OR text LIKE '%"citation"%')`;
type EchoPhase = { key: string; table: string; read: string; remove: string };
/** Cleanup passes over rowid windows: `events` rows by id, then FTS rows by rowid (no stable link to events). */
const ECHO_PHASES: EchoPhase[] = [
  {
    key: "echo_events_last",
    table: "events",
    read: `SELECT id,text FROM events WHERE id>? AND id<=? AND role='tool' AND ${ECHO_PREFILTER}`,
    remove: "DELETE FROM events WHERE id IN",
  },
  {
    key: "echo_fts_last",
    table: "session_fts_content",
    read: `SELECT id,c1 AS text FROM session_fts_content WHERE id>? AND id<=? AND c4='tool' AND ${ECHO_PREFILTER.replaceAll("text", "c1")}`,
    remove: "DELETE FROM session_fts WHERE rowid IN",
  },
];
type LogScan = { bytes: number; lines: number; pending: Buffer; skipped: number };
export type SessionSearchInput = { query: string; provider?: string; limit?: number };
export type SessionReadInput = { thread: string; start?: number; limit?: number };

/** Persistent local FTS5/BM25 index of canonical logs, shared by all providers. Lines are 1-based. */
export class SessionSearch {
  private db: DatabaseSync;
  private pending: Promise<void> | undefined;
  private closed = false;
  private maintenance = false;
  /** Archive state lives in threads.json, not the logs, so it is refreshed on every sync rather than indexed. */
  private archived = new Set<string>();
  private dataDir: string;
  private hooks: SessionSearchHooks;
  private loggedError = "";
  private echoPending = false;
  private mapPending = false;
  private cleanupTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;
  private cleanupWindow = 1000;
  private settleCleaned: () => void = () => {};
  private cleanedPromise: Promise<void> = new Promise((resolve) => {
    this.settleCleaned = resolve;
  });
  /** Echo-cleanup progress, exposed for tests and measurements. */
  cleanupStats = { batches: 0, longestBatchMs: 0 };
  private timers: NodeJS.Timeout[] = [];
  constructor(dataDir: string, hooks: SessionSearchHooks = {}) {
    this.dataDir = dataDir;
    this.hooks = hooks;
    fs.mkdirSync(dataDir, { recursive: true });
    const file = path.join(dataDir, "session-search.sqlite");
    this.db = new DatabaseSync(file);
    fs.chmodSync(file, 0o600);
    this.db.exec(`PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;
      PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000;
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions (
        thread TEXT PRIMARY KEY, title TEXT NOT NULL, provider TEXT NOT NULL,
        bytes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, lines INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        size INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, mtime REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        inode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', tail TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
      );
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, thread TEXT NOT NULL, line INTEGER NOT NULL,
        role TEXT NOT NULL, text TEXT NOT NULL, UNIQUE(thread,line)
      );
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS events_thread_line ON events(thread,line);
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pr_calls (thread TEXT NOT NULL, tool_id TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(thread,tool_id));
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meta (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value INTEGER NOT NULL);
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS echo_calls (thread TEXT NOT NULL, tool_id TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(thread,tool_id));
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS prs (
        thread TEXT NOT NULL, line INTEGER NOT NULL, repo TEXT NOT NULL, number INTEGER NOT NULL, url TEXT NOT NULL,
        PRIMARY KEY(thread,repo,number)
      );
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS prs_number ON prs(number);
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS fts_map (
        thread TEXT NOT NULL, line INTEGER NOT NULL, fts INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(thread,line,fts)
      ) WITHOUT ROWID;
      CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS session_fts USING fts5(title,text,thread UNINDEXED,line UNINDEXED,role UNINDEXED, tokenize='unicode61 remove_diacritics 2');`);
    // Logs indexed before PR tracking have no prs rows: drop them once so the next sync rebuilds from the logs.
    this.db.function("is_echo", { deterministic: true }, (text) =>
      isEchoRowText(String(text)) ? 1 : 0,
    );
    const version = Number(this.db.prepare("PRAGMA user_version").get()!.user_version);
    if (version < 1) {
      this.db.exec(
        "DELETE FROM session_fts; DELETE FROM events; DELETE FROM sessions; DELETE FROM fts_map; PRAGMA user_version=3;",
      );
    } else {
      // user_version 3 adds fts_map (thread,line -> FTS rowid); older indexes backfill it in the background.
      this.echoPending = version < 2;
      this.mapPending = version < 3;
      if (this.echoPending || this.mapPending) this.scheduleCleanup();
    }
    this.scheduleBackground();
  }

  /** Resolves when the echo cleanup has finished or the index was closed. */
  whenCleaned() {
    return this.cleanedPromise;
  }

  private scheduleCleanup() {
    if (this.closed || this.maintenance || this.cleanupTimer) return;
    this.cleanupTimer = setTimeout(() => this.cleanupStep(), 0);
  }

  private metaValue(key: string) {
    const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key=?").get(key);
    return row ? Number(row.value) : 0;
  }

  /** One small, time-bounded batch of the user_version 2 migration; yields to the event loop between batches. */
  private cleanupStep() {
    this.cleanupTimer = undefined;
    if (this.closed || this.maintenance || this.cleanupPaused()) return;
    try {
      const began = performance.now();
      // Version-2 indexes finished the echo cleanup already; only the fts_map backfill remains.
      const phase = this.echoPending
        ? ECHO_PHASES.find((entry) => this.phaseLast(entry) < this.phaseMax(entry))
        : undefined;
      if (!phase && !this.mapPending) return this.finishCleanup();
      if (phase) this.cleanBatch(phase);
      else this.mapBatch();
      this.cleanupStats.longestBatchMs = Math.max(
        this.cleanupStats.longestBatchMs,
        performance.now() - began,
      );
      this.cleanupStats.batches++;
      this.adaptWindow(performance.now() - began);
      this.scheduleCleanup();
    } catch (error) {
      this.logOnce(error);
      this.settleCleaned();
    }
  }

  private cleanupPaused() {
    const limit = this.hooks.cleanupBatchLimit;
    return limit !== undefined && this.cleanupStats.batches >= limit;
  }

  private phaseLast(phase: EchoPhase) {
    return this.metaValue(phase.key);
  }

  private phaseMax(phase: EchoPhase) {
    return Number(
      this.db.prepare(`SELECT coalesce(max(id),0) AS top FROM ${phase.table}`).get()!.top,
    );
  }

  private adaptWindow(elapsedMs: number) {
    if (elapsedMs < ECHO_BATCH_MS / 2)
      this.cleanupWindow = Math.min(this.cleanupWindow * 2, 50_000);
    else if (elapsedMs > ECHO_BATCH_MS) this.cleanupWindow = Math.max(this.cleanupWindow >> 1, 50);
  }

  private cleanBatch(phase: EchoPhase) {
    const from = this.phaseLast(phase);
    const upto = Math.min(from + this.cleanupWindow, this.phaseMax(phase));
    const rows = this.db.prepare(phase.read).all(from, upto);
    const doomed = rows
      .filter((row) => isEchoRowText(String(row.text)))
      .map((row) => Number(row.id));
    this.db.exec("BEGIN");
    try {
      for (let offset = 0; offset < doomed.length; offset += 500) {
        const chunk = doomed.slice(offset, offset + 500);
        this.db.prepare(`${phase.remove} (${chunk.map(() => "?").join(",")})`).run(...chunk);
      }
      this.db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO meta(key,value) VALUES(?,?)").run(phase.key, upto);
      this.db.exec("COMMIT");
    } catch (error) {
      this.db.exec("ROLLBACK");
      throw error;
    }
  }

  /** Backfill one rowid window of fts_map from the FTS content table; searches stay correct meanwhile. */
  private mapBatch() {
    const from = this.metaValue("map_last");
    const top = Number(
      this.db.prepare("SELECT coalesce(max(id),0) AS top FROM session_fts_content").get()!.top,
    );
    const upto = Math.min(from + this.cleanupWindow, top);
    this.db.exec("BEGIN");
    try {
      this.db
        .prepare(
          "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO fts_map(thread,line,fts) SELECT c2,c3,id FROM session_fts_content WHERE id>? AND id<=?",
        )
        .run(from, upto);
      this.db.prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO meta(key,value) VALUES('map_last',?)").run(upto);
      this.db.exec("COMMIT");
    } catch (error) {
      this.db.exec("ROLLBACK");
      throw error;
    }
    if (upto < top) return;
    // Drop entries whose FTS row was removed meanwhile (echo cleanup), so a reused rowid is never mis-deleted.
    this.db.exec("DELETE FROM fts_map WHERE fts NOT IN (SELECT id FROM session_fts_content)");
    this.mapPending = false;
  }

  /** FTS rowids of a thread (lines above `after`) by rowid through fts_map; full FTS scan only while the backfill is incomplete. */
  private deleteFts(thread: string, after = -1) {
    if (this.mapPending) {
      this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM session_fts WHERE thread=? AND line>?").run(thread, after);
    } else {
      this.db
        .prepare(
          "DELETE FROM session_fts WHERE rowid IN (SELECT fts FROM fts_map WHERE thread=? AND line>?)",
        )
        .run(thread, after);
    }
    this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM fts_map WHERE thread=? AND line>?").run(thread, after);
  }

  private finishCleanup() {
    this.db.exec(
      "DELETE FROM meta WHERE key LIKE 'echo_%' OR key='map_last'; PRAGMA user_version=3;",
    );
    this.echoPending = false;
    this.mapPending = false;
    this.settleCleaned();
  }

  /** Catch the index up shortly after start and every minute, so queries rarely wait. */
  private scheduleBackground() {
    const tick = () => {
      if (!this.closed && !this.maintenance) this.start().catch((error) => this.logOnce(error));
    };
    const first = setTimeout(tick, BACKGROUND_START_MS);
    const every = setInterval(tick, BACKGROUND_EVERY_MS);
    first.unref();
    every.unref();
    this.timers.push(first, every);
  }

  private logOnce(error: any) {
    if (this.closed) return;
    const message = String(error?.message ?? error);
    if (message === this.loggedError) return;
    this.loggedError = message;
    console.error(`session-search: refresh failed: ${message}`);
  }

  /** Single-flight refresh; callers share one promise. */
  private start() {
    if (this.closed) throw new Error("Session search is closed.");
    if (this.maintenance) throw new Error("Session search is in maintenance.");
    if (!this.pending) {
      this.pending = this.refresh().finally(() => {
        this.pending = undefined;
      });
      this.pending.catch(() => {}); // callers that gave up waiting must not trigger unhandled rejections
    }
    return this.pending;
  }

  private hasIndex() {
    return Number(this.db.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM sessions").get()!.n) > 0;
  }

  /** Wait for the refresh at most SYNC_WAIT_MS; returns true when answering from a stale index. */
  private async sync() {
    if (this.maintenance) throw new Error("Session search is in maintenance.");
    const run = this.start();
    const limit = Number(process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_SEARCH_SYNC_MS ?? SYNC_WAIT_MS);
    let timer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;
    const waited = new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => {
      timer = setTimeout(() => resolve(true), Number.isFinite(limit) ? limit : SYNC_WAIT_MS);
    });
    try {
      const stale = await Promise.race([run.then(() => false), waited]);
      if (this.maintenance) throw new Error("Session search is in maintenance.");
      return stale;
    } catch (error) {
      if (this.maintenance) throw new Error("Session search is in maintenance.");
      if (!this.hasIndex()) throw error;
      this.logOnce(error);
      return true;
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }

  private remove(thread: string) {
    this.deleteFts(thread);
    this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM events WHERE thread=?").run(thread);
    this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM sessions WHERE thread=?").run(thread);
    this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM pr_calls WHERE thread=?").run(thread);
    this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM echo_calls WHERE thread=?").run(thread);
    this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM prs WHERE thread=?").run(thread);
  }

  /** Remember `gh pr create` calls and record the PR each one printed. Tool calls and results may arrive in different syncs. */
  private trackPrs(thread: string, line: number, event: any) {
    const content = event?.message?.content;
    if (!Array.isArray(content)) return;
    for (const block of content) {
      if (
        block?.type === "tool_use" &&
        typeof block.id === "string" &&
        isPrCreate(block.input?.command)
      )
        this.db
          .prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO pr_calls(thread,tool_id) VALUES(?,?)")
          .run(thread, block.id);
      else if (
        block?.type === "tool_result" &&
        typeof block.tool_use_id === "string" &&
        this.db
          .prepare("SELECT 1 FROM pr_calls WHERE thread=? AND tool_id=?")
          .get(thread, block.tool_use_id)
      )
        for (const pr of prsFromResult(block.content))
          this.db
            .prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO prs(thread,line,repo,number,url) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)")
            .run(thread, line, pr.repo, pr.number, pr.url);
    }
  }

  /** Drop the history tools' own calls and results (found by tool id, which may arrive in a later sync) from an event. */
  private stripEcho(thread: string, event: any) {
    const content = event?.message?.content;
    if (!Array.isArray(content)) return event;
    const kept = content.filter((block) => !this.isEchoBlock(thread, block));
    return kept.length === content.length
      ? event
      : { ...event, message: { ...event.message, content: kept } };
  }

  private isEchoBlock(thread: string, block: any) {
    if (block?.type === "tool_use" && isEchoName(block.name)) {
      if (typeof block.id === "string")
        this.db
          .prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO echo_calls(thread,tool_id) VALUES(?,?)")
          .run(thread, block.id);
      return true;
    }
    return (
      block?.type === "tool_result" &&
      typeof block.tool_use_id === "string" &&
      !!this.db
        .prepare("SELECT 1 FROM echo_calls WHERE thread=? AND tool_id=?")
        .get(thread, block.tool_use_id)
    );
  }

  private async fingerprint(file: string, end: number) {
    if (!end) return "";
    const handle = await fsp.open(file, "r");
    try {
      const head = Buffer.alloc(Math.min(1024, end));
      const first = await handle.read(head, 0, head.length, 0);
      const hash = createHash("sha256").update(head.subarray(0, first.bytesRead));
      if (end > 1024) {
        const tail = Buffer.alloc(Math.min(1024, end - 1024));
        const last = await handle.read(tail, 0, tail.length, end - tail.length);
        hash.update(tail.subarray(0, last.bytesRead));
      }
      return hash.digest("hex");
    } finally {
      await handle.close();
    }
  }

  private async readCatalog(): Promise<any[]> {
    let threads: any;
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {
      try {
        threads = JSON.parse(await fsp.readFile(path.join(this.dataDir, "threads.json"), "utf8"));
        break;
      } catch (error: any) {
        if (error.code === "ENOENT") {
          threads = [];
          break;
        }
        if (attempt === 2)
          throw new Error("Session catalog is unavailable; try again after it is saved.");
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 25));
      }
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(threads)) throw new Error("Session catalog must contain an array.");
    return threads;
  }

  private async refresh() {
    await this.hooks.beforeRefresh?.();
    const threads = await this.readCatalog();
    const valid = threads.filter((entry) => safeId(entry?.id));
    const ids = new Set(valid.map((entry) => entry.id));
    this.archived = new Set(valid.filter((entry) => entry.archived).map((entry) => entry.id));
    for (const row of this.db.prepare("SELECT thread FROM sessions").all())
      if (!ids.has(row.thread)) this.remove(String(row.thread));
    for (const entry of valid) await this.syncThread(entry);
  }

  private async logChanged(old: any, stat: fs.Stats | undefined, file: string) {
    if (!old) return true;
    if (!stat) return old.size !== 0;
    return (
      String(stat.ino) !== old.inode ||
      stat.size < old.size ||
      (stat.size === old.size && stat.mtimeMs !== old.mtime) ||
      (stat.size > old.size && (await this.fingerprint(file, old.bytes)) !== old.tail)
    );
  }

  private upsertSessionRow(id: string, title: string, provider: string, old: any) {
    if (!old) {
      this.db
        .prepare("INSERT INTO sessions(thread,title,provider) VALUES(?,?,?)")
        .run(id, title, provider);
      const inserted = this.db
        .prepare("INSERT INTO session_fts(title,text,thread,line,role) VALUES(?,'',?,0,'title')")
        .run(title, id);
      this.db
        .prepare("INSERT INTO fts_map(thread,line,fts) VALUES(?,0,?)")
        .run(id, inserted.lastInsertRowid);
    } else if (old.title !== title || old.provider !== provider) {
      this.db
        .prepare("UPDATE sessions SET title=?,provider=? WHERE thread=?")
        .run(title, provider, id);
      this.db.prepare("UPDATE session_fts SET title=? WHERE thread=?").run(title, id);
    }
  }

  private async syncThread(entry: any) {
    const file = path.join(this.dataDir, "logs", `${entry.id}.jsonl`);
    const title = String(entry.title ?? "Untitled").slice(0, 500),
      provider = String(entry.provider ?? "unknown").slice(0, 100);
    let old = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM sessions WHERE thread=?").get(entry.id) as any;
    let stat: fs.Stats | undefined;
    try {
      stat = await fsp.stat(file);
    } catch (error: any) {
      if (error.code !== "ENOENT") throw error;
    }
    if (old && (await this.logChanged(old, stat, file))) {
      this.remove(entry.id);
      old = undefined;
    }
    this.upsertSessionRow(entry.id, title, provider, old);
    if (!stat || (old && stat.size === old.size && stat.mtimeMs === old.mtime)) return;
    await this.indexLog({ id: entry.id, title, file }, stat, old);
  }

  /** Parse complete lines in one chunk, advancing the scan counters and collecting indexable rows. */
  private scanChunk(threadId: string, scan: LogScan, chunk: Buffer) {
    const data = scan.pending.length ? Buffer.concat([scan.pending, chunk]) : chunk;
    let begin = 0,
      newline: number;
    const batch: { line: number; role: string; text: string }[] = [];
    while ((newline = data.indexOf(10, begin)) >= 0) {
      const raw = data.subarray(begin, newline);
      scan.bytes += scan.skipped + raw.length + 1;
      scan.lines++;
      if (!scan.skipped && raw.length <= MAX_LINE) {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(raw.toString("utf8"));
          const item = eventText(this.stripEcho(threadId, parsed));
          if (item) batch.push({ line: scan.lines, ...item });
          this.trackPrs(threadId, scan.lines, parsed);
        } catch {
          /* Ignore malformed complete lines; keep exact physical line references. */
        }
      }
      scan.skipped = 0;
      begin = newline + 1;
    }
    scan.pending = data.subarray(begin);
    if (scan.pending.length > MAX_LINE) {
      scan.skipped += scan.pending.length;
      scan.pending = Buffer.alloc(0);
    }
    return batch;
  }

  private writeBatch(
    target: { id: string; title: string },
    batch: { line: number; role: string; text: string }[],
  ) {
    this.db.exec("BEGIN");
    try {
      const event = this.db.prepare(
        "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO events(thread,line,role,text) VALUES(?,?,?,?)",
      );
      const fts = this.db.prepare(
        "INSERT INTO session_fts(title,text,thread,line,role) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)",
      );
      const map = this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO fts_map(thread,line,fts) VALUES(?,?,?)");
      for (const row of batch) {
        event.run(target.id, row.line, row.role, row.text);
        const inserted = fts.run(target.title, row.text, target.id, row.line, row.role);
        map.run(target.id, row.line, inserted.lastInsertRowid);
      }
      this.db.exec("COMMIT");
    } catch (error) {
      this.db.exec("ROLLBACK");
      throw error;
    }
  }

  private async indexLog(
    target: { id: string; title: string; file: string },
    stat: fs.Stats,
    old: any,
  ) {
    const scan: LogScan = {
      bytes: old?.bytes ?? 0,
      lines: old?.lines ?? 0,
      pending: Buffer.alloc(0),
      skipped: 0,
    };
    // Recover a process exit between a batch commit and the file checkpoint.
    this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM events WHERE thread=? AND line>?").run(target.id, scan.lines);
    this.deleteFts(target.id, scan.lines);
    if (stat.size === 0) {
      this.db
        .prepare("UPDATE sessions SET size=0,mtime=?,inode=? WHERE thread=?")
        .run(stat.mtimeMs, String(stat.ino), target.id);
      return;
    }
    // Fixed-size stream chunks keep file I/O asynchronous and bound memory for malformed lines.
    const stream = fs.createReadStream(target.file, {
      start: scan.bytes,
      end: stat.size - 1,
      highWaterMark: 64 * 1024,
    });
    for await (const chunk of stream) {
      const batch = this.scanChunk(target.id, scan, chunk as Buffer);
      if (batch.length) this.writeBatch(target, batch);
      await new Promise<void>((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve));
    }
    const tail = await this.fingerprint(target.file, scan.bytes);
    this.db
      .prepare("UPDATE sessions SET bytes=?,lines=?,size=?,mtime=?,inode=?,tail=? WHERE thread=?")
      .run(scan.bytes, scan.lines, stat.size, stat.mtimeMs, String(stat.ino), tail, target.id);
  }

  /** Start an initial background build before the first provider or browser query. */
  async warm() {
    await this.start();
  }

  async beginMaintenance() {
    if (this.closed) throw new Error("Session search is closed.");
    if (this.maintenance) throw new Error("Session search is already in maintenance.");
    this.maintenance = true;
    if (this.cleanupTimer) clearTimeout(this.cleanupTimer);
    this.cleanupTimer = undefined;
    try {
      await this.pending;
    } catch (error) {
      this.maintenance = false;
      if ((this.echoPending || this.mapPending) && !this.cleanupPaused()) this.scheduleCleanup();
      throw error;
    }
  }

  private checkpoint() {
    const result = this.db.prepare("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)").get() as any;
    if (Number(result.busy) !== 0) throw new Error("Session search index checkpoint is busy.");
  }

  async endMaintenance(rebuild: boolean) {
    if (!this.maintenance) throw new Error("Session search is not in maintenance.");
    try {
      if (!rebuild || this.closed) return;
      this.db.exec("PRAGMA secure_delete=ON;");
      this.db.exec(
        "DELETE FROM session_fts; DELETE FROM events; DELETE FROM sessions; DELETE FROM fts_map; DELETE FROM pr_calls; DELETE FROM prs; DELETE FROM echo_calls;",
      );
      this.finishCleanup();
      this.db.exec("VACUUM;");
      this.checkpoint();
      await this.refresh();
      this.checkpoint();
    } finally {
      this.maintenance = false;
      if ((this.echoPending || this.mapPending) && !this.closed && !this.cleanupPaused())
        this.scheduleCleanup();
    }
  }

  async search(input: SessionSearchInput) {
    const query = typeof input?.query === "string" ? input.query.trim() : "";
    if (!query || query.length > 500)
      throw new Error("Search query must contain 1–500 characters.");
    const terms = [...query.matchAll(/[\p{L}\p{N}_]+/gu)].map((m) => m[0]).slice(0, 32);
    if (!terms.length) throw new Error("Search query needs words or numbers.");
    const match = [...new Set(terms)].map((term) => `"${term}"`).join(" OR ");
    const limit = bounded(input.limit, 20, 50);
    if (
      input.provider !== undefined &&
      (typeof input.provider !== "string" || input.provider.length > 100)
    )
      throw new Error("Invalid provider filter.");
    const stale = await this.sync();
    const rows = this.db
      .prepare(`SELECT f.thread,s.title,s.provider,f.line,f.role,
      snippet(session_fts,1,'[',']',' … ',40) AS snippet,bm25(session_fts,3.0,1.0) AS score
      FROM session_fts f JOIN sessions s ON s.thread=f.thread
      WHERE session_fts MATCH ? ${input.provider ? "AND s.provider=?" : ""}${this.echoPending ? " AND NOT (f.role='tool' AND is_echo(f.text))" : ""}
      ORDER BY score, f.thread, f.line LIMIT ?`)
      .all(match, ...(input.provider ? [input.provider] : []), limit);
    return {
      query,
      results: rows.map((row) => ({
        ...row,
        archived: this.archived.has(String(row.thread)),
        citation: `${row.thread}:L${row.line}`,
        url: `/#thread=${encodeURIComponent(String(row.thread))}`,
      })),
      indexedSessions: Number(this.db.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM sessions").get()!.n),
      ...(stale ? { stale: true } : {}),
    };
  }

  async read(input: SessionReadInput) {
    if (!safeId(input?.thread)) throw new Error("Invalid thread id.");
    const start = bounded(input.start, 1, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER),
      limit = bounded(input.limit, 30, 100);
    const stale = await this.sync();
    const session = this.db
      .prepare("SELECT title,provider,lines FROM sessions WHERE thread=?")
      .get(input.thread);
    if (!session) throw new Error("Unknown session.");
    const rows = this.db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT line,role,substr(text,1,12000) AS text FROM events WHERE thread=? AND line>=?${this.echoPending ? " AND NOT (role='tool' AND is_echo(text))" : ""} ORDER BY line LIMIT ?`,
      )
      .all(input.thread, start, limit);
    // Bound response bytes as well as event count; tool logs may be very large.
    const events: typeof rows = [];
    let size = 0;
    for (const row of rows) {
      size += String(row.text).length;
      if (events.length && size > 48_000) break;
      events.push({ ...row, citation: `${input.thread}:L${row.line}` });
    }
    const next = events.length ? Number(events.at(-1)!.line) + 1 : null;
    return {
      thread: input.thread,
      title: session.title,
      provider: session.provider,
      archived: this.archived.has(input.thread),
      start,
      next: next !== null && next <= Number(session.lines) ? next : null,
      events,
      ...(stale ? { stale: true } : {}),
    };
  }

  /** Sessions whose log changed after `since` (epoch ms), newest first. Empty logs are left out. */
  async recent(input: { since: number; limit?: number }) {
    const since = Number(input?.since);
    if (!Number.isFinite(since)) throw new Error("since must be a time in milliseconds.");
    await this.sync();
    return this.db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT thread AS id,provider,title,mtime,lines FROM sessions WHERE mtime>? AND lines>0 ORDER BY mtime DESC, thread LIMIT ?",
      )
      .all(since, bounded(input.limit, 50, 500))
      .map((row) => ({
        id: String(row.id),
        provider: String(row.provider),
        title: String(row.title),
        mtime: Number(row.mtime),
        lines: Number(row.lines),
      }));
  }

  /** PRs a session raised (by thread), or sessions that raised a PR (by `ref`: URL, `owner/repo#n` or number). */
  async prs(input: { thread?: string; ref?: string }) {
    const stale = await this.sync();
    const select = `SELECT p.thread,s.title,s.provider,p.line,p.repo,p.number,p.url FROM prs p JOIN sessions s ON s.thread=p.thread`;
    let rows: any[];
    if (input.thread !== undefined) {
      if (!safeId(input.thread)) throw new Error("Invalid thread id.");
      rows = this.db.prepare(`${select} WHERE p.thread=? ORDER BY p.line`).all(input.thread);
    } else {
      const ref = parsePrRef(input.ref);
      if (!ref) throw new Error("Give a GitHub PR URL, owner/repo#number, or a PR number.");
      rows = this.db
        .prepare(
          `${select} WHERE p.number=? ${ref.repo ? "AND p.repo=?" : ""} ORDER BY s.mtime DESC, p.line LIMIT 50`,
        )
        .all(ref.number, ...(ref.repo ? [ref.repo] : []));
    }
    return {
      prs: rows.map((r) => ({
        ...r,
        archived: this.archived.has(String(r.thread)),
        citation: `${r.thread}:L${r.line}`,
        sessionUrl: `/#thread=${encodeURIComponent(String(r.thread))}`,
      })),
      ...(stale ? { stale: true } : {}),
    };
  }

  close() {
    this.closed = true;
    if (this.cleanupTimer) clearTimeout(this.cleanupTimer);
    this.settleCleaned();
    for (const timer of this.timers) {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      clearInterval(timer);
    }
    this.db.close();
  }
}

export interface Thread {
  workState?: { version: 1; foreground: string[]; background: string[]; backgroundCount: number };
  id: string;
  provider: string;
  title: string;
  autoTitle?: boolean; // still carries the folder-name default: the first message renames it (cleared by a manual rename)
  titleLocked?: boolean; // the user chose this title (at creation or by renaming): the title helper never rewrites it. Threads from before this flag count as unlocked
  recent?: string[]; // legacy: the title helper now reads every user message from the thread log
  cwd: string;
  mode: Mode;
  model: string;
  effort?: string;
  advisor?: string;
  claudeAccount?: ClaudeAccount;
  idleCompaction?: boolean;
  idleCompactionTtlMs?: number;
  idleCompactionSource?: string;
  idleCompactionLeadMs?: number;
  idleCompactionMinTokens?: number;
  resolvedModel?: string;
  idleCompactionDueAt?: number;
  lastUserActivityAt?: number;
  lastProviderActivityAt?: number;
  idleCompactionConsumedAt?: number;
  lastRun?: {
    model?: string;
    effort?: string;
    source?: string;
    duration_ms?: number;
    input_tokens?: number;
    output_tokens?: number;
    cached_input_tokens?: number;
    timing?: ReturnType<typeof runFacts>;
  };
  sessionId?: string;
  forkFrom?: string; // forked from a Claude session: the first run continues a copy of it (cleared once the fork has its own session id)
  context?: { used?: number; max?: number; source?: string }; // tokens in the agent's context now, and the model's window when the harness reports it
  replay?: boolean; // forked from an agent with no native fork: the first message carries the earlier conversation as text
  replayLimit?: number; // explicit short handoff instead of native session or full text replay
  status: "idle" | "running";
  waiting?: number; // unanswered blocking requests or persistent async questions
  background?: number; // background subagents still working after the turn ended; their completion wakes the agent
  operation?: "compact";
  lastCompactedAt?: number;
  archived?: number; // when it was archived: it leaves the working list until unarchived or sent to again
  quotaPaused?: { provider: string; windowLabel: string; resetsAt: number }; // paused due to quota exhaustion; auto-resumes late in a later window (see resumeDue)
  schedule?: { task: string; run: string }; // a scheduled task's run: the sidebar files it under its task
  issueRun?: IssueRun; // started by window-dispatch for a GitHub issue
  usageStop?: { provider: string; at: number; resetsAt?: number; text: string }; // last turn ended on a usage limit; cleared when the next turn starts
  replyAt?: number; // when the last non-compaction turn ended
  readAt?: number; // when the user last viewed the thread
  cost: number;
  costMeasured?: boolean;
  createdAt: number;
  updatedAt: number;
}

const THREADS_FILE = path.join(DATA_DIR, "threads.json");

fs.mkdirSync(LOG_DIR, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });

// Thread ids reach file paths, so only plain names pass: no dots or slashes that could leave the data folder.
const SAFE_ID = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/;
export const safeId = (threadId: unknown): threadId is string =>
  typeof threadId === "string" && SAFE_ID.test(threadId);

// The only way to build a transcript path.
function logFile(threadId: string): string {
  if (!safeId(threadId)) throw new Error("Invalid thread id.");
  return path.join(LOG_DIR, `${threadId}.jsonl`);
}

// An idle agent keeps its process (warm next turn: ~0.3 s to start instead of ~20 s) for up to IDLE_KILL_MS. Idle processes are
// capped at IDLE_MAX, oldest killed first, and all but the newest two go early when available memory drops under IDLE_MEM_FLOOR.
const IDLE_KILL_MS = Number(process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_IDLE_MS ?? 30 * 60 * 1000);
const IDLE_MAX = Number(process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_IDLE_MAX ?? 8);
const IDLE_MEM_FLOOR = 0.2;
// Pre-warm: opening/focusing a thread spawns its idle agent so the first send skips the harness cold start. Spawning sends no
// prompt and makes no model request (verified per harness). At most WARM_MAX unused processes; unused ones are reaped sooner.
const WARM_MAX = Number(process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_WARM_MAX ?? 2);
const WARM_IDLE_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;
// Background tasks keep an idle thread's agent alive and block restart-when-idle. Claude caps a background command at 2 hours
// and a working subagent reports progress, so tasks silent for longer than this are hung: the sweep kills the agent.
const BACKGROUND_STALE_MS = Number(
  process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_BACKGROUND_STALE_MS ?? 135 * 60 * 1000,
);
// A running turn streams tokens, tool results or progress; a foreground tool call is capped at 10 minutes. A turn silent for longer
// than this, with no blocking question waiting on the user, is hung and would block restart-when-idle forever: the sweep ends it.
const TURN_STALE_MS = Number(process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_TURN_STALE_MS ?? 30 * 60 * 1000);

// First-party turn timing (metadata only, never sent anywhere). Unavailable storage must never affect a turn.
let timing: TurnTimingStore | undefined;
try {
  timing = new TurnTimingStore(path.join(DATA_DIR, "latency.sqlite"));
} catch {
  console.warn("Turn timing storage is unavailable.");
}
export const turnTiming = () => timing;
const timed = (fn: () => void) => {
  try {
    fn();
  } catch {}
};

// Chatter the UI never shows. Dropped before it is logged or sent (and filtered from older logs): a long Claude thread was 15% of it.
const NOISE = new Set([

const kindOf = (e: any) => `${e?.type}${e?.subtype ? "/" + e.subtype : ""}`;
const PAGE = Number(process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_PAGE) || 40; // events the UI gets when it opens a thread; older ones load on request
const PAGE_BYTES = Number(process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_PAGE_BYTES) || 96_000; // sent JSON per page: a page widened back to its turn's start can exceed 150 events
const TAIL_CHUNK = 256 * 1024; // bytes per backwards read of a log's tail
/** Lines of a file, streamed so a huge log never blocks the event loop. */
async function* readLines(file: string): AsyncGenerator<string> {
  const lines = readline.createInterface({
    input: fs.createReadStream(file, { encoding: "utf8" }),
    crlfDelay: Infinity,
  });
  try {
    yield* lines;
  } finally {
    lines.close();
  }
}
const RESULT_CLIP = 4000; // tool output in replayed history (the card shows at most 8000 anyway, and live output is already clipped at 20000)
const MAX_QUEUE = 20;

interface Queued {
  id: string;
  text: string;
  attachments: unknown; // [{id,n}] as sent by the browser; resolved when the message is actually sent
  revision: number;
  editing?: boolean;
  questionAnswer?: true;
  sourceThread?: { id: string; title: string; provider: string };
  at?: number; // when it was queued: queue wait in turn timing
}
const attachList = (q: Queued): any[] => (Array.isArray(q.attachments) ? q.attachments : []);
// The newest Claude rate-limit event in a log. Boot restores one per thread: read only the tail, never the transcript.
function lastClaudeWindows(file: string, provider: string) {
  let fd: number;
  try {
    fd = fs.openSync(file, "r");
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
  try {
    const size = fs.fstatSync(fd).size;
    for (const span of [64 * 1024, 4 * 1024 * 1024]) {
      const start = Math.max(0, size - span),
        buf = Buffer.alloc(size - start);
      fs.readSync(fd, buf, 0, buf.length, start);
      const found = scanWindowsTail(buf, start, provider);
      if (found) return found;
      if (start === 0) break;
    }
  } finally {
    fs.closeSync(fd);
  }
  return undefined;
}

// Newest usable rate-limit line in one tail buffer; `start` is the buffer's offset in the file.
function scanWindowsTail(buf: Buffer, start: number, provider: string) {
  for (
    let hit = buf.lastIndexOf('"unifiedWindows"');
    hit !== -1;
    hit = buf.lastIndexOf('"unifiedWindows"', hit - 1)
  ) {
    const from = buf.lastIndexOf(10, hit) + 1;
    if (from === 0 && start > 0) break; // the line began before this window
    const found = parseWindowsLine(buf, from, hit, provider);
    if (found) return found;
    if (from === 0) break;
    hit = from;
  }
  return undefined;
}

function parseWindowsLine(buf: Buffer, from: number, hit: number, provider: string) {
  const lineEnd = buf.indexOf(10, hit);
  try {
    const event = JSON.parse(buf.toString("utf8", from, lineEnd === -1 ? buf.length : lineEnd));
    if (
      Number.isFinite(event.observedAt) &&
      event.usageProvider === provider &&
      claudeWindows(event).length
    )
      return event;
  } catch {}
  return undefined;
}

// Boot: record the newest Claude rate-limit event from this thread's log.
function recordClaudeUsage(thread: Thread) {
  const key = claudeUsageKey(thread.claudeAccount);
  const last = lastClaudeWindows(logFile(thread.id), key);
  if (!last || !Number.isFinite(last.observedAt) || last.usageProvider !== key) return;
  usage.record(last.usageProvider, { windows: claudeWindows(last) }, last.observedAt, {
    observedAt: last.observedAt,
    usageProvider: last.usageProvider,
    ...(typeof last.session_id === "string" && typeof last.uuid === "string"
      ? { sourceSessionId: last.session_id, sourceEventUuid: last.uuid }
      : {}),
  });
}

// Fold one Codex telemetry sample into the thread's context window facts.
function applyCodexSample(thread: Thread, sample: any) {
  const context = sample.context;
  if (!context) return;
  if (!("reset" in context)) {
    thread.context = { ...thread.context, ...context };
    return;
  }
  thread.context = { max: thread.context?.max, source: context.source };
  thread.lastCompactedAt = sample.observedAt || thread.lastCompactedAt;
}

// A saved thread after a restart: nothing survives, so it is idle. False drops it (unknown Claude account).
function reviveThread(thread: Thread): boolean {
  thread.status = "idle"; // nothing survives a server restart
  delete thread.waiting;
  delete thread.operation;
  delete thread.background;
  thread.provider ||= DEFAULT_PROVIDER;
  thread.readAt ??= thread.replyAt ?? thread.updatedAt;
  if (thread.provider === "claude") {
    try {
      thread.claudeAccount = normalizeClaudeAccount(thread.claudeAccount);
    } catch {
      console.warn("Skipped a thread with an unknown Claude account.");
      return false;
    }
  }
  if (thread.provider === "codex" && thread.context?.source !== "codex/last_request")
    delete thread.context;
  return true;
}

function sanitizeQueued(items: Queued[]): Queued[] {
  return items
    .filter(
      (item) =>
        typeof item.id === "string" && typeof item.text === "string" && item.text.length <= 100_000,
    )
    .slice(0, MAX_QUEUE)
    .map((item) => ({
      ...item,
      revision: Number.isSafeInteger(item.revision) ? item.revision : 0,
      editing: item.editing === true,
    }));
}

type SendOptions = {
  auto?: "brain" | "subagent" | "quota-resume" | "continue";
  attachments?: unknown;
  mode?: "steer" | "queue";
  delivery?: "queued";
  questionAnswer?: true;
  sourceThread?: { id: string; title: string; provider: string };
  queuedAt?: number;
  instructions?: string;
  scheduled?: string;
};

type CreateOptions = {
  claudeAccount?: string;
  provider?: string;
  cwd: string;
  mode?: Mode;
  model?: string;
  effort?: string;
  title?: string;
  schedule?: Thread["schedule"];
  issueRun?: Thread["issueRun"];
};

type ThreadPatch = {
  claudeAccount?: string;
  mode?: Mode;
